## How to use this catalogue

Choose a work, not a file extension. A work can have several reading forms; a reference volume can contain more than one source document; a checkpoint can contain many works and supporting records. A PDF, its generated TeX and its Markdown input are not three independently authored papers.

The catalogue is arranged by intellectual subject. Read the short route below, then open the paper itself or use the detailed entry for its assumptions, editions and related results. Some entries are research notes, reviews or proposals; their role is stated in the entry. Primary literature cited by the research remains literature, and supporting receipts remain support.

| I want to understand… | Start with |
|---|---|
| The framework and its precise records | [Main exposition](#main-exposition) · [formal reference](#formal-reference) |
| What observations determine and how to acquire missing evidence | [Information and composition](#information-and-composition) · [continuation-complete information](#continuation-complete-information) · [fresh histories and access](#fresh-histories-access-and-source-warrant-the-twelfth-checkpoint) |
| Recognition, warranted cognition and correction | [Recognition and correction](#recognition-and-correction) · [dynamic orthability](#dynamic-orthability) |
| Foundational arguments and their premises | [Philosophical manuscripts](#philosophical-and-theological-manuscripts) · [original productive sourcehood](#original-productive-sourcehood-knowledge-and-action-selection) · [Seventh–Eleventh arguments](#foundational-arguments-from-the-seventh-through-eleventh-research) · [later Twentieth context](#later-twentieth-context-types-and-warrant) |
| Tensors, information geometry and learning | [Graded representations](#graded-representations-and-witnessed-revision) · [repair geometry](#continuation-repair-geometry-and-information) · [prior-sensitive learning](#prior-sensitive-learning-width-and-executable-inference) |
| Exact identity, proof rules and executable verification | [Identity and finite proof](#identity-representations-and-finite-proof) · [Fifteenth compendium](#six-papers-in-the-fifteenth-mathematical-compendium) · [Twentieth exact identities](#twentieth-exact-identities-modal-scope-and-grounded-support) |
| Applications and empirical research designs | [Application studies](#application-studies-contracts-and-runtime-outlines) · [programme reports and research software](#programme-reports-and-empirical-research-software) |
| What finite observations and permitted actions can preserve | [Twentieth live research and v6 synthesis](#twentieth-live-research-and-v6-synthesis) · [calibrated identification](#calibrated-identification-from-exact-laws-and-finite-observations) · [proof-sensitive inquiry](#missing-support-and-the-future-cost-of-warranted-inquiry) |
| Identification, source warrant and restoration under uncertainty | [AR8R research](#ar8r-identification-correction-and-warranted-application) · [recovered notes](#ar8r-recovered-transfer-and-restoration-notes) · [research designs](#research-designs-for-machinery-records-and-vocabulary) |
| Source studies, literature and historical document context | [Supporting reference](#supporting-reference-not-another-research-article) · [source-study appraisals](#source-studies-and-historically-distinct-appraisals) · [Twentieth reading methodology](#later-twentieth-reading-methodology) |

The [frontier guide](CURRENT-FRONTIERS.md) separates established statements at their stated scope from conditional applications and open research. The [editorial scope and qualification record](../project-closure/reader-refactor-issue-35/PROGRESS.md) identifies the coverage, review state and source-reading limits.

## Consolidated scholarly works

These directly readable treatments contain the arguments and proofs reconciled from their public predecessors. Their independent incorporation review is scoped; none silently replaces an accepted paper edition or confers owner adoption.

- [Information retained by inquiry](../../mathematics/information-and-inquiry.md): predictive states, posterior equivalence, inquiry value, Fisher dynamics, certificate selection and finite temporal guarantees. Includes exact graph-action costs, persistent evidence frontiers, optimal further inquiry and finite tree/shared-proof compilation.
- [Received actuality, intrinsic necessity, and original source unity](../../foundations/received-actuality-and-intrinsic-necessity.md): causal intelligibility and its limits, complete original-production arguments, connected-field coverage, explanatory countermodels, centered knowledge and informed practical agency.
- [Objective normativity and corrective cognition](../../foundations/objective-normativity-and-corrective-cognition.md): intelligibility and function, truth-calibrated correction, intervention, intrinsic reasons, source-owned undertakings and the strongest inspected contemporary rivals.
- [Recognition, warrant, defeat and restoration](../../epistemology/recognition-warrant-and-restoration.md): positive cognitive grounds, complete relevant-inference proofs and countermodels, necessary targets, proper function, cumulative evidence, surviving warrant and finite restoration/identification results.
- [Impredicative encodings, typed identity and proof witnesses](../../logic/encodings-and-witnesses.md): the dependent source calculus, literal arithmetic compiler, fixed witnesses, closed-term identity decisions, complete models and exact proof boundaries.
- [Collective inquiry: information, strategy and coordinated judgment](../../mathematics/collective-inquiry-and-strategic-coordination.md): distributed evidence, scalar and vector games, coordination bounds, statistical identification, conditional restoration, information-compatible transfer, finite-memory learning and fresh-innovation limits, with their complete proofs and counterexamples.
- [Tensor moments and corrective dynamics](../../mathematics/tensor-moments-and-corrective-dynamics.md): moment realizability, the arbitrary-coercive existence theorem, signed and time-dependent dynamics, hidden coordinates, nonmoment constructions, finite-prefix limits and effective recovery.
- [Common repair and the geometry of coherent forecasts](../../mathematics/common-repair-and-geometric-obstructions.md): quadratic and smooth rigidity, generalized means, minimax limits, coupled polynomial repair and counterexamples to local or score-only criteria.

The [incorporation and review record](../scientific-consolidation-review.md) states the exact reviewed editions, source-replacement scope, failed findings and remaining physical-removal gates.

## Core exposition and formal reference

### Main exposition

**Orthemma–Ortheme Systems: An Analysis-Relative Architecture for Auditable Classification and Handling.** Read the [Markdown manuscript][main] or its [governed PDF][render-orthemma-ortheme-systems-draft].

For the typeset result/pathway matrix, open section 8.4 on PDF pages 14–15. GitHub's Markdown preview currently rejects a notation macro and requires horizontal scrolling for that table.

**Why read it:** this is the principal account of how one concrete occurrence moves through observation, competing descriptions, evidence, placement, routing and correction. An *orthemma* is the situated occurrence; its *orthemes* are repeatable, consequence-bearing state-types under a declared analysis. The actual profile is distinct from both an observation and an actor’s inferred placement. Analysis-relativity does not let the actor manufacture what is true.

The [integration thesis](../../manuscript/orthemma-ortheme-systems-revised-draft.md#13-the-thesis-stated-as-an-integration-discipline) is deliberately specific: combine established classification, uncertainty, provenance and handling practices in a shared lifecycle and an auditable **episode record**. The six additions concern versioned lineage; analysis/actor/time indices; typed and expiring evidence; factorised candidates; per-burden closure; and episode reification. The manuscript does not claim that each neighbouring technique is new.

Its [stopped-clock validator](../../manuscript/orthemma-ortheme-systems-revised-draft.md#85-worked-example-the-stopped-clock-validator) returns a correct answer merely because a marker occurs in a log. Removing or spoofing the marker exposes a defective method. The [result/pathway matrix](../../manuscript/orthemma-ortheme-systems-revised-draft.md#84-the-result--pathway-matrix) also admits the converse: an adequate, sufficiently reliable procedure can produce a rare wrong result. Pathway adequacy is therefore non-factive; factive token truth linkage is reported separately. Undetermined required checks are neither passes nor automatic failures. The distinctions direct different repairs instead of blaming every error on the same component.

The [empirical programme](../../manuscript/orthemma-ortheme-systems-revised-draft.md#13-empirical-program) separates machinery benefit, episode-record benefit and vocabulary benefit. Its ready-to-run packets and conformance fixtures are not completed experiments or proof of mathematical completeness. These are this manuscript’s evidence boundaries, not a denial of later separately scoped research. The [limitations](../../manuscript/orthemma-ortheme-systems-revised-draft.md#151-limitations) also deny that task-relative success supplies the legitimacy of the task or its authority. Edit the Markdown, not generated TeX or the PDF; this exposition does not silently incorporate every later tranche.

### Formal reference

**Orthemic core reference.** Read the [composite PDF][render-orthemic-core-reference-draft], the [formal core][core], or the [multi-actor conflict note][multi].

The [publication profile][profile] binds **both** source documents to this one rendering. The core adopts a structured episode rather than identifying a run with its answer: observations, candidate families, a partial placement, claim ledger, residuals, authority and successors remain separately accessible. Its [endpoint-plus-bounded-trace contract](../../theory/orthemic-core-formalization.md#22a-endpoint-plus-bounded-trace) supplies ordered evidence/action history when the required verdicts need it. It does **not** demand maximal logging for every case; governance determines applicability and trace granularity.

The [distributed composition account](../../theory/orthemic-core-formalization.md#52-distributed-episodes-graph-and-composition) explains when a graph of sub-episodes also constitutes one boundary-level episode: compatible occurrence lineage and analysis, a declared actor/governance boundary, and an explicit fusion rule. Retries add later episode tokens, not backwards edges in the token DAG. Sharing a task alone does not establish that different analyses compose.

The multi-actor component’s [formal residue](../../theory/orthemic-multi-actor-conflict-note.md#3-formal-residue-assessed-against-the-existing-core) compares actor-specific targets by pulling them back to a **shared reachable-occurrence domain**. Similar goal schemata are not identical targets; differing information does not multiply the underlying occurrence. Game rules, objectives and strategies retain distinct roles, and strict zero-sum permits draws. This is a derived clarification, not a seventh addition or a deployed competitive-agent product.

The core’s [status ledger](../../theory/orthemic-core-formalization.md#status-ledger) reports analytic/definitional acceptance and scoped internal conformance, not experimental utility or universal consistency/completeness. Both editing components stay visible; their composite PDF is not a third independent manuscript.

## Philosophical and theological manuscripts

### Ground of intelligibility

**Orthability and the Ground of Intelligibility: A Transcendental Argument from Rule-Governed Resolution.** [Source][ground] · [Governed PDF][render-orthability-ground-of-intelligibility-draft].

**Why read it:** it asks what makes genuine recognition, error, fittingness and correction possible, rather than treating the engineering rulebook as its own ultimate justification. It distinguishes local analysis-relative aptness (**L**), objective conditions of evaluability (**O**) and created representation/application (**R**). The [labelled argument chain](../../companion/orthability-and-the-ground-of-intelligibility.md#42-the-chain-with-each-step-labeled) passes from *true* evaluations to their objective preconditions, not from an arbitrary local convention to a reified object called Orthability.

The affirmative reductio is that an intelligible construction of **all** objective evaluability would already use identity, possibility, lawful transition and success/failure conditions. It would therefore presuppose what it claims wholly to produce. The [adjudication](../../companion/orthability-and-the-ground-of-intelligibility.md#53-adjudication) makes its reach explicit: an opponent who instead accepts brute modal structure or refuses the intelligible-production model is not thereby demonstratively refuted. Reliability alone does not establish a Designer; the faculty argument separately needs irreducible teleology and causal adequacy. Intellect, Will, Power and Wisdom are not obtained by relabelling one result. The [step map][orthability-step-map] explicitly holds the common-premise Wisdom bridge.

The [disclosure argument](../../companion/orthability-and-the-ground-of-intelligibility.md#8-a-ground-capable-of-disclosure) supports at most a **conditional capacity** for intentional disclosure, not that the ground has actually spoken or that a particular text is its speech. The separate Atharī manuscript supplies its own revelational premises.

**Reading the historical claim correctly:** the source’s [later V11 authority qualification](../../companion/orthability-and-the-ground-of-intelligibility.md#later-v11-authority-qualification) says that this positive philosophical language is not itself the current audit verdict; it directs that verdict to Track T/B0–B16 and retains `NECESSARY_BEING_NOT_DEDUCTIVELY_ESTABLISHED`. That explicit qualification stays with this work. The [later source-bound foundational account](RESEARCH-ATLAS.md#a-positive-foundational-route) has its own premises and appraisals; this catalogue neither merges the assessments nor rewrites the older argument. Engineering conformance supplies no metaphysical proof.

### Atharī companion

**Orthability, the Divine Attributes, and the Uncreated Speech of Allah: An Atharī Creed-Internal Development.** [Source][athari] · [Governed PDF][render-orthability-divine-speech-athari-draft].

**Why read it:** this is the explicitly Atharī/Taymiyyan articulation of divine Knowledge, Speech, linguistic possibility and created reception. It is not merely the preceding philosophical conditional with a religious title attached. Its identification of Allah and actual divine Speech is revelational and creed-internal, rather than an engineering inference or a claim attributed to every Islamic school.

Its [seven Speech bearers](../../companion/orthability-divine-attributes-and-speech-athari.md#21-seven-speech-bearers) separate human convention, creaturely acts, voice/breath, media, Allah’s act of speaking, revealed Arabic wording as His Speech, and creaturely reception. The [perfection/genus/particular distinctions](../../companion/orthability-divine-attributes-and-speech-athari.md#32-perfection-genus-and-particulars-the-precise-taymiyyan-distinctions) articulate the source’s positive position: the perfection and genus of Speech are eternal, particular speeches occur when He wills, and a particular divine Speech is not thereby a created thing. Neither unqualified “eternal” nor a language label alone fixes the intended bearer.

The [recitation discussion](../../companion/orthability-divine-attributes-and-speech-athari.md#5-the-reciters-act-and-the-carried-word) distinguishes created reciting, voice, ink and page from the uncreated Word they carry within this account. “Uncreated grammar” is only the source’s defined metaphor for divinely grounded expressibility and fitting signification, not another eternal entity or a pre-existent human grammar book. The [reason/revelation accounting](../../companion/orthability-divine-attributes-and-speech-athari.md#8-reason-and-revelation-the-final-accounting) identifies which premises support each claim. Its contrasting Sunni account is comparative; the manuscript does not adjudicate that dispute on behalf of all schools.

Per-claim sourcing remains at the existing [source-status owner][source-status-owner] and the manuscript’s own loci. This synopsis does not reauthenticate classical quotations, endorse every historical narration or convert a PDF build into theological evidence.

### Dynamic orthability

**Dynamic orthing, noetic learning, and dynamic orthability.** [Source][dynamic] · [Governed PDF][render-dynamic-orthing-noetic-learning-orthability-draft].

**Why read it:** it turns the static question of evaluability towards the availability of correction trajectories: can error be discovered and representations improved **without the learner creating truth**? Its candidate concept is the analysis-relative availability of lawful, evidence-sensitive, correction-capable trajectories. Faculty/disposition, represented standard, normative type, case binding, execution, placement and objective result remain different bearers.

The [eight modalities](../../companion/dynamic-orthing-noetic-learning-and-orthability.md#81-eight-modalities-that-must-not-collapse) distinguish metaphysical possibility, nomological possibility, objective reachability and controllability from this actor’s accessibility, evidence-relative discoverability, representation-class learnability and guaranteed convergence. Merely having a correction available does not give every actor a way to find it. These modalities are not the fourteen stable nodes in the separate [argument map](../../companion/dynamic-orthing-noetic-learning-and-orthability.md#9-metaphysical-argument-map) and its [structured owner][dynamic-argument-map].

The map’s critical regularity-to-norm step is explicit: more examples of a learner converging do not, without the separate bridge premise, establish what the faculty *ought* to do. The OSM and DAEE examples are worked explananda at their stated levels, not proof of the metaphysical or revelational nodes. Within the manuscript’s school-labelled account, fiṭrah is a normative disposition, not a discourse-measured coordinate; its testimony/ecology discussion distinguishes corroboration from copied propagation. The [broader cognitive-vindication treatment](#recognition-and-correction) remains a distinct source route, not silently attributed to this paper.

Its earlier scalar-gradient proposal is likewise not a verdict that the separately scoped [tensor/Fisher mathematics](RESULT-INDEX.md#tensor-moments) is absent. Primary-text verification, secondary reconstruction, cross-source synthesis, Orthemological extension and creed-internal inference keep their own evidence roles. No guarantee about real human learning, soul access or theological validation follows from the model.

## Mathematical manuscripts and preserved editions

### Information and composition

**Information, Composition, and Self-Revision: Local Descriptions and Operative Access.** [Preserved PDF][information-pdf] · [Tracked TeX][information] · [Edition relationship note][lineage].

**Why read it:** this manuscript connects what a local description determines with what an observing method can learn by reconstructing its access. It does not infer an actual coupling from compatible component records. Its [relational strengthening theorem](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/0784acddd4770f1190f758f264ba52b2b110bd27/theory/lineages/h-ics-v5/information-composition-and-self-revision-v5.tex#L81) separates an invariant that *some* exactly compatible composition can guarantee from one that *every* compatible composition guarantees. With nonempty compatible records, the first asks whether `G = J ∩ P` still projects onto every declared local relation; the second requires `J ⊆ P`. The parity family shows that even all proper subsystem records can leave a joint constraint undetermined.

For probabilities, compatible support is not sufficient for compatible weights. The [output-determination proposition](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/0784acddd4770f1190f758f264ba52b2b110bd27/theory/lineages/h-ics-v5/information-composition-and-self-revision-v5.tex#L202) uses the span `D` of **feasible differences** between laws sharing the record: a channel has the same output law on that family exactly when it annihilates `D`. Replacing `D` by the whole record kernel requires the stated full-support condition. This matters at boundary laws, where algebraically possible directions need not be feasible perturbations.

Its [operative-access argument](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/0784acddd4770f1190f758f264ba52b2b110bd27/theory/lineages/h-ics-v5/information-composition-and-self-revision-v5.tex#L241) distinguishes reformatting an insufficient observation from acquiring an informative relation. A permitted reconnection can make an already reachable parity observation immediately available. That is neither evidence created from nothing nor a claim to expand beyond every inherited operation. The [sure-finite-decision theorem](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/0784acddd4770f1190f758f264ba52b2b110bd27/theory/lineages/h-ics-v5/information-composition-and-self-revision-v5.tex#L289) then gives an exact labelled-belief criterion for finite deterministic observation-based controllers. The labels retain the **initial** answer through state changes. In the destructive example, every pair has a distinguishing experiment, but neither first action preserves enough information for one policy to decide every initial possibility.

The version-bound workflow applies the same distinctions to candidate receipts: two success flags are insufficient when they omit the candidate identities. It requires the joint guard to remain valid through publication, not just individually consistent field reads. The source supplies conditional models and ordinary arguments, not a verified concurrent implementation, noise-tolerant controller, physical mechanism or metaphysical proof. It acknowledges the antecedents of its join, marginal and state-identification techniques rather than claiming each is new.

The lineage note establishes the title/abstract and companion relationship. It does **not** establish a byte-identical rebuild of the supplied PDF. The discussion above is grounded in the tracked TeX; that source is not silently promoted to the verified exact build input of the preserved edition.

### Decisive and reflexive revision

#### The preserved decisive-revision paper

**The Cost of a Decisive Revision: Evidence, Reuse, and Self-Application.** [Preserved four-page PDF][decisive-pdf].

The PDF itself presents the finite evidence-budget argument. Page 1 fixes the decision, authorised deterministic probes and positive costs; probes leave the situation unchanged or operate on resettable copies. Page 2 gives the minimum worst-case remaining probe cost and the adaptive-address example. Page 3 explains target-relative evidence reuse, predecessor-governed method revision and a changing-objective cycle. Page 4 shows why identical task behaviour does not by itself identify delivery cost. These are ordinary written results for the stated exact-observation model, not a universal runtime or a guarantee that revision converges.

This description is based on reading all four preserved pages, including their rendered equations, not solely on the differently titled TeX. It establishes what the edition says; it does not establish its build provenance or independent novelty relative to the related manuscript below.

#### The related reflexive-revision manuscript

**Reflexive Revision under an Evidence Budget.** [Tracked TeX manuscript][reflexive].

The [minimum-evidence-cost section](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/0784acddd4770f1190f758f264ba52b2b110bd27/theory/lineages/h-reflexive-v3/reflexive_revision_evidence_budget_v3.tex#L47) specifies `V_g(S)`: zero when the remaining situations agree on the decision; otherwise minimise probe cost plus the largest remaining branch cost. Its theorem characterises exactly when a finite budget guarantees a uniformly sound answer. Identifiability and affordability differ, and affording the next probe alone does not ensure that the entire deciding continuation fits. Policy computation and validation of the model are **not** included in these probe costs. Hard constraints remain vetoes rather than compensable penalties.

The [reuse and reflexivity section](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/0784acddd4770f1190f758f264ba52b2b110bd27/theory/lineages/h-reflexive-v3/reflexive_revision_evidence_budget_v3.tex#L86) explains why a retained record can settle one target yet fail a changed target. Under unchanged meanings, no new observation is needed exactly when the new decision is constant on each retained-record fibre. An injective record suffices for every Boolean target; a restricted future target family can need less. A common well-founded order can justify termination, but changing local losses alone cannot: the source's two-state example cycles while improving the predecessor loss at each step.

The [delivery-cost obstruction](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/0784acddd4770f1190f758f264ba52b2b110bd27/theory/lineages/h-reflexive-v3/reflexive_revision_evidence_budget_v3.tex#L114) supplies a practical limit: complete agreement on task outputs cannot decide which representation is cheaper without an observation linking behaviour to delivery cost. An authorised probe of that missing coordinate changes the decision problem; repeating behaviour-only tests does not. This is a reason to ask the right discriminating question, not a result that either representation is actually cheaper.

Keep both titles and their source roles visible. The [lineage note][lineage] relates the PDF and TeX, but explicitly leaves exact source-to-PDF provenance unverified. Similar arguments and direct PDF inspection do not turn that relation into a verified build link. The catalogue retains the existing two records without counting them as established independent discoveries.

### Graded representations and witnessed revision

**Graded Representations, Witnessed Revision, and Inquiry Landscapes — a conditional mathematical extension of Orthemology.** [Manuscript][graded-representations]. September 2026 research draft.

This work starts from concrete episodes and a **declared representation map**, not an assertion that cognition or an episode is intrinsically a tensor. It constructs finite typed record representations, separates a prospective inquiry landscape from historical ecology, and asks what compression and revision must preserve. Its target criterion is constancy on representation fibres; the arbitrary-arity parity construction explains why fixed-order marginals cannot capture every possible joint distinction. That does not prohibit a small target-specific representation.

The selected later statements give a deterministic future-safe quotient criterion and connect representation, transitions, retained obligations and inquiry readouts. The manuscript also states conditional strategic and stability constructions. Ordinary proofs, proposed definitions, finite examples and transfers to real cognitive or software systems keep their different scopes; no proof-assistant certification or empirical adoption follows from this catalogue entry.

### Continuation-complete information

**Continuation-Complete Orthings: Sharp information loss, minimal predictive memory, and safe strategic composition.** [Research supplement][continuation-complete]. September 2026 supplement v1.

This is the preceding manuscript’s **explicit stochastic-continuation extension**, not another filename for the same work. In its finite reference model, Theorem 1 and its proof identify the exact minimax error left by a linear moment summary: the distance of the target observable from the retained observable space equals half the largest disagreement between laws that share the retained record. This supplies both a quantitative loss and indistinguishable countermodels.

The abstract and central account go on to construct the least space closed under the permitted action–observation operations and state a sharp continuous-coordinate cost. Its pairwise binary update family can require exponentially many predictive coordinates as the horizon grows. The object being summarised is an uncertain **law**, not an already observed bit string. These conclusions depend on the declared future inquiries, finite model and representation assumptions; the paper does not claim to solve fluid dynamics or certify an application to a mind.

### Modal support and necessary being

**Orthability and Necessary Being: Modal support, concrete grounding, and the common-bearer frontier.** [Research supplement][modal-grounding]. September 2026 supplement 02 / v2.

This work moves from continuation of representations to an explicitly modal and metaphysical question. It separates necessary truths, necessary instantiation, one necessarily existing bearer, uniform underivability, necessity in itself and a knowing common bearer. Its actual-ancestry theorem gives a positive conditional route: a nonempty complete ancestor domain, well-founded dependence and the stated explanation requirement for contingent members yield an actual underived necessary bearer. The explanatory requirement is substantive, not merely a ban on self-causation.

The manuscript’s broader programme includes joint-support and minimal-cut analysis, conditional further routes and countermodels separating stronger conclusions. It does not make necessary personhood or knowledge follow from a tensor representation. Its September frontier is a dated source assessment: read the [later affirmative foundational route](RESEARCH-ATLAS.md#a-positive-foundational-route) alongside it, rather than replacing later source-owned appraisals with this older draft’s limits.

### Stateful cross-substrate realisation

**The Last Moat, Third Edition: Physical Realisability, Cross-Substrate Communication, and Holarchic Agency.** [Root TeX manuscript][last-moat] · [Edition 3 changes and evidence ledger][last-moat-changes].

This is **one componentised manuscript**. Its root includes twenty-two [TeX components](../../theory/lineages/h-moat-e3/), all retained in the crosswalk as components, not twenty-two additional papers. The root and included files belong together for authoring; this catalogue does not assert that a governed PDF or a freshly verified build exists for it.

The work asks when an organised process can preserve a specified stateful contract while replacing its original host. It distinguishes behavioural realisation from copying weights, the joint realisation from each constituent, and role/classification claims from evidence maturity. Its introduction and revision ledger describe a useful paired contrast: a declared parity-memory witness supplies two models and state-preserving handovers, whereas two individually correct processors and a correct packet channel can still fail continuation if the exported information omits consequential hidden state.

The mathematical witness covers its stipulated model; recorded simulations are not a laboratory migration. The source’s literature discussion is distinct from newly performed experiments, and its own revision ledger rejects an unsupported blanket absence claim about alternative processors. No personal conscious identity or support-free agency is inferred here. Component identities are fully bound, but this entry does not claim fresh substantive reading of every included section.

### Epistemic profiles and intra-agential equilibrium

**Epistemic Tuples and Intra-Agential Equilibrium.** [Complete immutable text extraction][epistemic-tuples] · [Readable notation and architecture companion][epistemic-tuples-reading] · [Separate ET05 correction][epistemic-tuples-correction].

The original work preserves heterogeneous evidentiary strengths as a tuple rather than forcing a premature scalar ranking. Cognition is endogenous to the account: perception, source monitoring, memory, coherence, defeater recognition and judgement mutually condition admissible responses. With justified functional decomposition, finite response sets, meaningful mixtures and appropriate real-valued payoffs, an intra-agential game supplies conditional mixed Nash existence. Equilibrium is not thereby truth, a unique correct weighting or the only suitable solution concept.

The later reading companion restores notation and a diagram **alongside the original**, rather than replacing it or becoming a revised paper. The separate ET05 assessment distinguishes absence of nonzero nonnegative gains from absence of gains strictly positive in every coordinate. Its finite mixed-game results explain what strictly positive versus merely nonnegative scalarisation weights establish; the weights are existence witnesses, not prescribed epistemic trade-offs. Preserve the original statement and the correction as distinct source roles.

The original PDF’s publication hold remains in force. The companion’s readable transcription does not release that binary, and the refactor does not requalify all inherited transcription or binary-coverage findings. This entry’s new substantive reading covers the original executive statement through its conditional Nash discussion and the named correction passage, not every original page.

### Finite coordination information

**A bounded information consequence for coordination explanations.** [Ordinary mathematical text][coordination] · [Twelfth collection guide][twelfth].

The paper analyses the information needed by a complete coordination mediator. In its ordinary countably additive setting, a fixed positive bounded-capture probability forces logarithmic information growth with the horizon; terminal tightness yields the asymptotic coefficient one half, attained by a consistent Gaussian anchor. A uniformly bounded complete-record entropy cannot supply that dependence across all horizons. Its finite-alphabet capture result supplies a sharper exact fixed-horizon Gaussian bound and a matching construction. Its exact role and limitations appear in the [result entry](RESULT-INDEX.md#finite-mediator): a finite program description need not be a complete mediator, and single-horizon attainment does not establish one forward-causal history across all horizons.

This substantive work is readable in Markdown. Lack of a PDF is not a reason to omit it.

### Tensor moments and coercive dynamics

**Full tensor moment dynamics under polynomial damping.** [Written predecessor][tensor].

**Square positivity forces exact dynamics for every coercive polynomial.** [Written successor][coercive].

These are separate mathematical works. The successor explicitly strengthens the square-positive-to-representable bridge under its stated conditions, while relying on the earlier recovery/exhaustion result. Preserve the predecessor, including its historical frontier, and explain the stronger successor rather than rewriting either original.

The [tensor result entry](RESULT-INDEX.md#tensor-moments) distinguishes all-grade moment dynamics, complete-path identification and initial-record ambiguity. Neither paper is labelled a new kernel proof by this catalogue.

### Fisher geometry and retained information

**Finite-audit Fisher descent: exact criterion and sharp cardinality threshold.** [Source][fisher].

**When the finite sidecar theorem meets a continuous audit fibre.** [Corollary][sidecar] · [Existing ordinary review][sidecar-review].

The first work defines a full-source Fisher metric and gradient-descent problem on finite positive joint laws. The corollary states what preserving selected ambient norms requires on a fixed weight fibre. A finite-valued supplement cannot distinguish that entire fibre when it has positive dimension; the sharp continuous-coordinate count is fixed-fibre, not a general global chart theorem.

The [geometry result entry](RESULT-INDEX.md#fisher-geometry) specifies the stronger preservation contract. These are actual conditional constructions; identifying their variables with a real person's cognition requires additional modelling and evidence.

## Fresh histories, access and source warrant: the Twelfth checkpoint

The [Twelfth collection guide][twelfth] brings together nine current research texts, not nine PDF renderings or nine independent confirmations. The coordination paper is [catalogued above](#finite-coordination-information); the other eight texts are distinguished below. The [checkpoint identity][t12-identity] fixes this particular nine-text population. Earlier two-paper recovery records and repeated copies remain historical material, not substitutes for it.

The sequence is useful to read as a connected inquiry. A concentration bound identifies incompatible chance and capture assumptions; an explicit diffuse history shows what remains mathematically possible without capture. Observation and reporting then make the resource and policy questions concrete. The source-use and chain-foundation texts examine different actual-warrant questions rather than treating the mathematical model as proof of their premises.

### Fresh innovation histories and original support

**Fresh histories, bounded observation, and original support.** [Complete written article][t12-fresh] · [Mathematical review][t12-concentration-review].

The article proves a Gaussian anti-concentration bound for an ordinary real-valued **finitely additive** joint probability. Independence and the Gaussian marginal's tail control suffice; tightness of the other variable and joint countable additivity are not required. Applying the bound to arbitrarily long fresh innovation blocks, all bound to one terminal law, makes every bounded terminal interval null. Thus even one warranted positive bounded-capture probability contradicts that package.

Its constructive counterbalance is equally important: a fixed ultralimit of uniform-anchor laws gives a coherent fresh history in which every sample position is finite, the full noise marginal is ordinary iid Gaussian, and every bounded position interval has probability zero. The article also supplies finite forward interventions and distinguishes a stable contractive process from its accumulating-variance obstruction. These are positive mathematical constructions, not merely warnings about evidence.

For an original-support argument, the article identifies an additional joint-realisability burden concerning the actual received support, stochastic mechanism and observation contract. It credits the inherited original-possession and recognition arguments without pretending that this stochastic route establishes an actual original participant, unique complete bearer or intrinsic necessity. The [source-specific frontier](CURRENT-FRONTIERS.md#fresh-histories-access-and-rational-policy) preserves that distinction rather than turning a local limitation into a verdict on every foundational route.

### Bounded acquisition and diachronic policy

**Bounded access and diachronic coherence.** [Complete assessment][t12-access] · [Separate final clarification][t12-clarification].

The positive operational result replaces a blanket preference for countable additivity with a specified acquisition task. If successful acquisition within resource budget `m` confines the target to a bounded range, then finite subadditivity bounds success by bounded capture plus the resource tail. A warranted positive success probability and a sufficiently small tail therefore force positive bounded mass. The law, information state, target and magnitude-sensitive task must stay fixed. A finite output alphabet or a correct actual answer does not by itself supply that contract.

A second construction uses a countable partition of positive-probability exact reports. Each report gives a specified event posterior zero although its prior is one half. Under the stated reporting and trading interface, two locally favourable transactions incur a fixed loss in every completed outcome; a quadratic forecast calculation supplies a related prospective-accuracy comparison. Every finite deadline retains a no-report branch and does not inherit the same completed-policy conclusion.

The final clarification is a **separate interpretative supplement**, not an edited edition of the article. It distinguishes mathematical incompatibility of positive bounded capture from domination of a particular completed policy: the history, reporter and losing behaviour are mathematically consistent. Abstention, precommitment, model revision and rejection of an unwarranted service assumption remain different responses. Outright resale can lock in the loss at report time without an extra executed settlement stage, while warranted ticket value and transfer conditions remain necessary.

### Total finite-input reporting

**A total integer report protocol with non-tight resources.** [Complete supplied-input construction][t12-integer] · [Clarification of completed execution][t12-clarification].

For the retained integer analogue, the paper gives an explicit finite-state reporting procedure using a private sign bit, a finite unary magnitude tape and sequential noise bits. The end marker caps the waiting rule, so **every valid finite input and every tape—including all tails—terminates**. The public clock, silence and pulse are specified so the original exact-report probabilities and policy calculations survive. This positively answers the objection that the integer report is merely measurable or only almost-surely computable.

Total execution is not a uniform or tight resource guarantee. Every fixed bound on the full magnitude input has probability zero in this law, whereas the runtime meets a fixed deadline with the paper's stated positive probability on one branch; its tail tends to one half. These are different resource claims. The procedure needs fixed finite control plus the supplied tape, not infinite working memory in an actual run. Supplying that input, maintaining the clock/channel/opportunities, generating the diffuse law and handling Gaussian exact-real acquisition remain distinct application questions. No physical implementation or fresh execution is claimed here.

### Finite forward interventions

**Exact finite forward intervention semantics.** [Complete ordinary mathematical supplement][t12-interventions].

The supplement supplies Borel overwrite maps on the anchored path space. A prescribed innovation replacement leaves all earlier states fixed and changes subsequent states by exactly the replacement displacement; at negative times an explicit anchor correction is essential. Finite disjoint replacements compose and commute, and pushforward preserves the relevant recurrence and transformed past/future independence. This is additional, constructive intervention structure—not an inference of intervention semantics from a causal-looking arrow.

Overlapping replacements commute only when their prescribed values agree; otherwise the last replacement wins. Arbitrary infinite-past, adaptive or future-dependent replacements are not included. Unmodified Gaussian noise retains its law, the targeted coordinates become fixed, and bounded capture is not created. The operation is pushforward, not conditioning on a Gaussian-null event. Its mathematical availability leaves actual manipulability and productive realisability as separate questions.

### Concentration review and consistency construction

**Finitely additive concentration and a past-complete innovation law.** [Complete scoped ordinary-mathematics review][t12-concentration-review].

This substantive review checks the finite-partition proof, event measurability, endpoints, sharp Gaussian form and common-terminal-law consequence. It also develops the full Borel ultralimit construction, including reanchoring, full past/future factorisation and an integer analogue used by the later total-report protocol. Its discussion of non-Archimedean values distinguishes the coarse bound with strict slack from conclusions whose limit step requires ordinary real values.

It is catalogued with the role **review**, not counted as another independent discovery of every reviewed theorem. Its recorded acceptance is limited to the ordinary arguments and their hypotheses. This refactor's reading is not a new independent mathematical review, human-specialist endorsement or external priority assessment. The [final verification scope][t12-scope] retains the same distinction between recorded checks and stronger claims.

### Quantitative capture and positive controls

**Quantitative independence, bounded capture, and positive controls.** [Complete quantitative supplement][t12-capture].

Using the event-distance convention `d_TV(P,Q) = sup_E |P(E)-Q(E)|`, the supplement turns bounded capture into a finite-horizon lower bound on departure from an independent state/noise benchmark. One positive capture probability gives its corresponding asymptotic lower bound; a tight common terminal law strengthens this to distance tending to one. The benchmark needs the specified marginals and common event domain; the finitely additive construction states its integration order rather than assuming product uniqueness.

Its positive controls matter to interpretation: an expanding search can terminate at every finite-valued sample while missing every fixed budget almost surely under the diffuse law; a contractive Gaussian autoregression has a tight fresh history because block variance stays bounded; and deterministic translation has a nonstationary entrance law despite lacking a countably additive stationary probability. These are scoped applications and standard controls, not claims of new general probability-theory priority or original-source existence.

### A declared standard and an attested occurrence

**A declared standard and an attested occurrence.** [Complete source-use report][t12-occurrence].

The report records two distinct positive findings: the selected divergent reading is attested in an imaged 1911 printed witness, and that witness's introduction declares the Leonine edition as a revision/correction standard. Attestation can exclude a later stage as the first introduction of an already present feature; the declaration supplies defeasible evidence of an editorial relationship. Neither alone identifies the exact Latin exemplar, responsible actor or local production operation.

This is a claim-relative improvement to actual source-use evidence, not merely another provenance label. The report preserves the stronger genealogy question and distinguishes declared practice from universal occurrence-level conformity. Its own image-inspection and source-review record is inherited evidence here: the refactor read the report, **not** the 460-page facsimile or private captures. A further occurrence-level production finding would need discriminating evidence such as an identified working exemplar, annotated proof or contemporary correction, not another mirror of the same wording.

### A local route to original participation

**A local route to an original participant.** [Complete source-bound foundational candidate][t12-chain].

The candidate applies its attributed conditional first-cause argument to the complete incoming-closed ancestor cone of **one** relevant ancestor of the correction. If that cone has a root under the stated acyclicity, chain-foundation/common-bound and size restrictions, that root is also an ancestor of the correction. The conditions need not hold on every other branch. This is a useful reduction of the warrant burden, not a new general theorem or an actual witness already supplied.

Incoming closure prevents a selected mechanism from being called independent merely because its earlier suppliers were omitted. Identifying a root with original existential supply additionally requires that the chosen relation exhaust the relevant supply respect. The text retains the positive fixed-input principle and original possession's prima-facie force, but does not infer that ordered receiving acts identify one common input. A root, an original participant, a unique complete bearer and intrinsic necessity remain separate claims. The present synopsis attributes the argument to the inspected programme text; it is not a fresh inspection of the external author notes.


## Identity, representations and finite proof

The following lineage asks when a representation preserves a typing certificate, when a semantic identity has any syntactic proof, and what finite evidence can decide. `Has`, `HasPlus` and `HasE` name **different rule systems**, not successive spellings of one adopted calculus. A result's later formal component does not rewrite the status of its original paper.

### Opaque atoms and prescribed typing

**Opaque atoms and fixed polynomial semantic incompleteness.** [Read the complete written proof][t13-opaque] · [Thirteenth source owner][t13-group].

A structured polynomial and an opaque atom can evaluate to exactly the same raw program while only the structured representation is typable at the selected polymorphic identity type. The proof replaces one whole opaque label in every possible derivation and uses a singleton conversion-class interpretation to force a contradiction. It covers the unchanged typing rules, including finite import and dependent identity elimination, rather than reporting that a search found no proof.

The positive structured representation remains available. Thus this is **failure to type a prescribed representation**, not proof that the target has no inhabitants. Its discarded-loop corollary also shows that having a finite-typed representative modulo raw conversion need not type the supplied opaque atom. This is backward representation expansion, not a counterexample to forward subject reduction. The note is ordinary written mathematics; its inherited normalization and semantic-soundness results keep their own evidence.

### The atom/application bridge

**A hypothetical atom application conversion bridge.** [Read the written design and proofs][t13-bridge].

The single added conversion schema exposes an application hidden inside an atom: `atom(app(r,s))` is convertible to `app(atom(r),atom(s))`. In the separately copied `HasPlus` judgement it supports closed packing and makes closed polynomial conversion exactly match raw conversion of evaluations. The paper supplies a mutual soundness argument using the original `F/G` interpretation, including newly formed types; it does not simply reuse a theorem whose input still expects old `Has` evidence.

This is a constructive answer to the preceding representation problem, with a real admission trade-off. The old replacement invariant no longer survives the new generator. It does **not** identify every semantically equal pair, decide conversion, or prove full semantic completeness. The general packing theorem needs closed term polynomials; open environments are not silently encoded as one atom. The source's written-only, hypothetical status remains historical even though later separately identified code studies the bridge.

### The historical I/S KK question

**Residual identity completeness feasibility.** [Read the historical note][t13-residual] · [Later full-rule resolution][t14-separation].

The note constructs a formed identity type between `I` and `S K K` at the polymorphic identity-function carrier. The original semantics admits a proof witness, while direct identity introduction is blocked because the endpoints are not raw-convertible. The note correctly stops short of ruling out all derivations: elimination or dependent `J` could, in principle, supply a different route.

Its attempted shortcut—strengthen only the identity interpretation while keeping the old context relation—fails a transport requirement. That failed route is part of the intellectual content, not disposable bookkeeping. **One note supports two historical result scopes, not two papers.** The later resolution below answers the missing all-rule obligation; the original unresolved header remains unchanged.

### Full-rule identity separation

**Intensional identity separation and closed identity conversion.** [Read the complete paper][t14-separation] · [Later formal declarations][t14-separation-lean] · [Exact formal-suite owner][t14-suite].

A coherent saturated-set interpretation makes every type relation refine raw conversion, including its context and substitution behaviour. Checking all typing constructors—particularly finite imports, both binders and both coordinates of `J`—then excludes **every** inhabitant of the selected `I/S K K` identity type in both `Has` and `HasPlus`. The unchanged original `F/G` interpretation still admits that type's semantic witness. This is incompleteness of those exact syntactic judgements for that semantic target, not unsoundness of the original semantics.

The same argument supplies a useful positive criterion: for a formed carrier and two already typed, term-closed endpoints in `HasPlus`, existence of an identity proof, the canonical `atom(I)` proof, and raw conversion of evaluations are equivalent. The carrier may retain free type parameters. The paper's written proof and later formal component are **one work with distinct evidence stages**, not duplicate discoveries. The recorded suite covers named declarations; this reader refactor does not claim a fresh Lean run or whole-paper formalisation.

### Finite certificates and unbounded search

**Finite certificates and inhabitation limits for exact P01AC and HasPlus.** [Read the complete written argument][t14-certificates].

A fully annotated finite derivation can be checked by a total mathematical validator. Even finite import has a finite certificate: one formation child for each image-list position. That is different from receiving only a desired conclusion and searching for a proof. Enumeration semidecides positive derivability and inhabitation, without a stopping rule for unsuccessful searches.

The paper reduces raw conversion to formed closed identity targets in two distinct ways: **opaque** endpoints in `HasPlus`, and **exposed** polynomial endpoints in unchanged `Has`. Its undecidability conclusion retains the explicitly imported weak-combinatory representation premise and the proof that added inert constants preserve the relevant closed problem. No executable parser or global complexity formalisation is supplied by this paper. Later [restricted semantic checkers][t15-group-followup] have their own narrower input contracts; their existence is not a general solver for this problem.

### A separate typed extensional repair

**Typed extensional repair in HasE — a formal development.** [Exact positive and preserved-negative statements][t14-repair] · [Rule definitions][t14-repair-syntax] · [Finite witness][t14-repair-witness] · [Original-semantics soundness][t14-repair-soundness] · [Recorded completion scope][t14-review].

`HasE` adds two finite typed-extensionality rules, for dependent functions and polymorphic `All`, while retaining the imported bridge conversion. The supplied witness first proves the endpoints agree on an explicit open argument, then uses one function-extensionality and one `All`-extensionality step to obtain the previously missing **typed** identity.

This is positive additional typing power, not permission to rename an old theorem. The old `Has`/`HasPlus` noninhabitation results remain true; the same endpoints are still not raw-convertible, and their **Raw-carrier** identity remains uninhabited even in `HasE`. The original `F/G` soundness is separately supported. Formal code, an inherited checked suite and a demonstrated witness are present, but operational adoption, minimality and universal semantic completeness do not follow.

## Six papers in the Fifteenth mathematical compendium

The source's [contents and projection notice][t15-compendium] identify six constituent mathematical papers. The original volume and its derived Markdown are reading forms, not additional independent papers. Each entry below reaches the exact paper, rather than only the top of the volume. The [reading and coverage record][fifteenth-coverage] distinguishes selected argument reading from unexamined dependencies.

The first five papers retain **research P01AC's current `Has` judgement and original `F/G` interpretations**. They do not change public P01DF or adopt the separate HasE extension. The [existing result owner][t15-identity-owner] and [declaration map][t15-declarations] distinguish written whole-result arguments from their recorded formal components.

### Raw-valued semantic identity

**Exact Complexity of Original Semantic Identity.** [Read Paper 1][t15-paper-1].

At the fixed carrier from Church naturals to `Raw`, the paper gives a Π₂⁰-complete classification both for bare endpoint codes and for inputs carrying checked endpoint-typing certificates. This is an actual set of codes: the recursive matrix handles invalid or untypable inputs, not merely a promise that typing has already been established. The canonical-test equivalence uses **both endpoint typings** to carry independently related arguments through the unchanged semantics.

Its consequence is that neither all valid identities nor all invalid identities in that typed slice admit a sound, complete, recursively enumerable certificate system. A sound effective repair such as HasE therefore cannot prove every original semantic identity there. The argument uses an explicitly imported representation premise and inherited computability methods; its canonical-test and semantic bridge components are formally checked in the recorded development, not the entire external hierarchy argument. The result is neither an arbitrary-carrier classification nor a claim about finite restricted expression families.

### Boolean identity with typing certificates

**Boolean Identity Completeness.** [Read Paper 2][t15-paper-2].

For certified current-typed functions from Church naturals to Church Booleans, identity is Π₁⁰-complete. Every semantic inequality has a finite negative witness: a canonical input and two finite conversion derivations to opposite inert markers. The Boolean codomain makes each output's observation effectively discoverable, while the unbounded natural input domain still prevents a universal finite equality test.

There is no computable uniform upper bound on a distinguishing input over this whole certified domain—even for a proposed bound terminating on every valid annotation. That does not deny a least witness for each unequal pair, finite maxima at a fixed syntax size, or separately proved bounds for a restricted family. The ordinary completeness proof has substantial checked standardness, compiler, mismatch and semantic-transfer components; its machine-indexing and full hierarchy argument remain written mathematics. Removing the supplied typing certificates changes the problem, as the next paper explains.

### Bare Boolean inputs and typability

**Bare Boolean Identity and Fixed Type Typability.** [Read Paper 3][t15-paper-3].

On bare endpoint syntax, the paper expresses Boolean identity as `D \ M`: the enumerable set of currently typable pairs minus the enumerable mismatch set. It proves d.c.e.-completeness for equality and Σ₁⁰-completeness for closed typability at the fixed function type. A supplied certificate is therefore not merely a more convenient display of the same decision problem.

The reduction separates two computations: one gate must halt for typing to become possible; once it returns, equality depends on a second machine not halting. The negative argument preserves the whole trailing argument list under head reduction—an unapplied divergent subterm alone would not suffice. Formal support checks positive typing/equality transport and a stated semantic non-typability interface. The complete head-reduction argument and complexity classifications remain written; no general normalisation or type-inference algorithm is claimed.

### A complete restricted identity checker

**Restricted Semantic Identity Completeness for Natural Number Expressions in P01AC.** [Read Paper 4][t15-paper-4] · [Lean checker and certificate theorem][t15-checker-lean] · [Compiler bridge][t15-restricted-compiler].

This is a positive completeness result for finite expressions with natural constants, finitely many variables, addition, multiplication and nested **zero tests**. Each zero/nonzero input mask fixes a polynomial normal form. Agreement of all mask coefficient maps is equivalent to equality on every natural tuple and to the original semantic identity of the literal compiled endpoints. The written paper also gives a degree-bounded finite grid and bounded distinguishing witnesses; arity zero is included.

The Lean development proves the executable coefficient checker's full semantic correctness and finite typed-certificate completeness for this grammar. It is not only a compiler bridge. Its finite sparse certificate lists may contain harmless redundant rows, split coefficients and zero entries, provided every required mask has a correct aggregate map. The separate [Python reference][t15-json-reference] requires a strict canonical **decoded-JSON** object; standard decoding is not a proved duplicate-key-rejecting parser. [Finite Python/Lean comparisons][t15-runtime-reference] do not establish universal parser or implementation refinement.

Literal endpoint binding, the original semantics and the grammar restrictions are substantive. The theorem does not recognise arbitrary conversion-equivalent P01AC programs as fragment members, derive every current syntactic identity, include subtraction or arbitrary equality tests, or certify service availability under resource exhaustion. The explicit grid and Python interfaces retain their written-proof/reference-code scope beside the stronger recorded Lean coefficient theorem.

### Equality tests and the univariate exception

**Polynomial Equality Tests and the Boundary of Effective Semantic Identity Completeness.** [Read Paper 5][t15-paper-5] · [Formal root-test bridge][t15-root-compiler] · [Univariate reference][t15-univariate-reference].

Replacing the structural zero test with equality between two positive arithmetic expressions changes the effective problem. In the separately declared extension, an input is either an old expression or one root-equality test of two pure arithmetic expressions. Equality of that test to constant zero means **absence of a nonnegative root**, not that two polynomials are identically equal. With arity included in the input, the paper gives a Π₁⁰-complete classification using its attributed external computability premise. The formal file checks the compiler and semantic equivalence, not that complete external reduction.

The one-variable subcase remains decidable. Exact integer coefficient aggregation yields a polynomial tail and root bound; the resulting inclusive finite interval `0..M` suffices. Different tails provide a witness at their bound, while equal tails need only the finite prefix. The Python implementation is a separately tested reference, not formally refined Lean code. No particular hardness arity of at least two is supplied, and no result for arbitrary nested equality-test programs follows. The [interface guide][t15-reference-guide] preserves these distinctions.

### What final answers can identify

**What final answers can identify about collective evidence use.** [Read Paper 6][t15-paper-6] · [Observation-contract guide][t15-attribution-guide].

The paper constructs a matched Gaussian pair: a one-input mechanism and a genuinely two-input mechanism have the same final-score and final-answer law, although the second input is causally consequential in only one mechanism. Whole-sequence agreement needs the same complete fixed or exogenous joint design and fresh noises; matching stimulus marginals alone is insufficient. Final answers cannot discriminate this pair, but that is not a claim that all mechanisms are equally plausible.

Same-trial linked passive evidence, even one threshold bit, separates the stipulated laws when the measurement contract is retained. Independent bit error attenuates that separation; a fair random flip removes it. A fixed-sample error guarantee needs a known positive gap. An exact-real, one-sided sequential service can instead detect each fixed positive covariance without known marginals, while offering no terminating null answer or uniform deadline. The near-null argument explains why a uniformly reliable, almost-surely terminating two-answer service cannot generally be added.

These are ordinary mathematical arguments about an observation design, not a fitted participant model, a new human experiment or a certified floating-point classifier. The [separate reanalysis guide][t15-reanalysis-guide] must not be mistaken for empirical identification of the paper's constructed mechanisms. The [recorded verification][t15-final-verification] does not become fresh independent review merely because the paper is now easier to find.

The two fits reconcile the original workbook estimates while retaining the negative slope and keeping timing missingness from discarding choice trials.
