### Noetic orthing as a domain application

**Noetic orthing: daee-epistemics as a worked application.** [Complete narrative](../../applications/daee-epistemics/NOETIC-ORTHING-APPLICATION.md) · [Typed correspondence](../../applications/daee-epistemics/DAEE-ORTHEMOLOGY-CROSSWALK.yaml) · [Fuller runtime correspondence](../../applications/daee-epistemics/CURRENT-RUNTIME-CROSSWALK.yaml).

The narrative types a concrete engagement, public observations, candidate interpretations, governing criteria, case bindings and response routing as distinct parts of an orthing episode. Its constructive contribution is a two-way test: Orthemology supplies distinctions with which to describe a designed noetic system; the application exposes places where the general architecture must not collapse observations into interior conditions or a diagnosis into a restorative route. The Diagnostic IR is a composite episode-internal control representation, not one metaorthemma or ground truth.

The narrative's `direct`, `crosswalk` and `creed-internal` labels distinguish attributed DAEE descriptions, this project's interpretation and the analysis's premises. The [source boundary](../../applications/daee-epistemics/SOURCE-BOUNDARY.md) and [runtime boundary](../../applications/daee-epistemics/CURRENT-RUNTIME-BOUNDARY.md) preserve the recorded external pin, co-development limitation and non-transfer of runtime closure to human restoration. Later [multi-target precision](#multi-target-noetic-claims) must accompany a subject-level placement; the original engagement-level description is not permission to identify discourse with its speaker.

### Epistemological and metaphysical placement

**Epistemological and metaphysical placement of the daee application.** [Complete placement note](../../applications/daee-epistemics/EPISTEMOLOGICAL-AND-METAPHYSICAL-BOUNDARY.md).

The note explains why the application is a worked concretisation of first-order versus higher-order assessment, concrete versus sound reasoning, distinct warrant channels and evaluator symmetry. It also provides a worked **explanandum**: a designed practice whose operation presupposes standards of proper and improper cognition and fitting correction. This is substantive philosophical placement, not merely a software provenance disclaimer.

Operationalising those commitments does not establish their ultimate ground. The note does not infer a Necessary Being, divine attributes, the truth of its deformation taxonomy or irreducible teleology from the system's existence. Its Atharī/Taymiyyan scope and modern comparative use of proper-function terminology remain explicit. Those limitations qualify this application route; they do not erase the separate affirmative foundational arguments linked in the [atlas](RESEARCH-ATLAS.md#recognition-sound-cognition-and-correction).

### Multi-target noetic claims

**Multi-target noetic orthing.** [Complete target-separation note](../../applications/daee-epistemics/MULTI-TARGET-NOETIC-ORTHING.md) · [Worked target map](../../applications/daee-epistemics/NOETIC-TARGET-MAP.example.json) · [Evidence-bound claim examples](../../applications/daee-epistemics/NOETIC-CLAIM.example.json).

This application-level refinement separates the observed discourse, person-at-time, runtime state, released response and later uptake occurrence. An observation can be *from* discourse and *about* a subject without being true, sufficient or uniquely identifying evidence of the subject's condition. Every claim therefore needs its own target identity/version, analysis, evidence scope, alternatives and uncertainty.

The worked records make the distinction operational: a discourse-level interpretation can be asserted while the subject-level profile remains held with live alternatives; motive and culpability are out of scope, and a runtime-closure claim does not settle the person's uptake. This supplies a concrete way to represent restraint without discarding the positive discourse analysis. The [target schema](../../applications/daee-epistemics/NOETIC-TARGET-MAP.schema.json), [claim schema](../../applications/daee-epistemics/NOETIC-CLAIM.schema.json) and [evidence registry](../../applications/daee-epistemics/NOETIC-EVIDENCE-REGISTRY.example.json) support the distinction; validating their structure is not empirical diagnosis or soul access.

### Represented standards, carriers and memetic ecology

**Meta-noetic memetics and dynamic orthing.** [Complete study](../../applications/daee-epistemics/META-NOETIC-MEMETICS-AND-DYNAMIC-ORTHING.md) · [Dynamic correspondence](../../applications/daee-epistemics/DYNAMIC-CROSSWALK.yaml) · [Ecology example](../../applications/daee-epistemics/MEMETIC-ECOLOGY.example.json).

The study distinguishes a normative standard type, an actor/institution's represented standard, an episode-local binding and its execution. A represented standard persists and travels across episodes; it cannot be replaced by a case token. Its typed ecology tracks carriers, inheritance, compression, mutation and replacement. This permits changed wording with a stable carried rule, or unchanged wording with a changed rule. The [relation-level assessment](../../applications/daee-epistemics/REP-META-ASSESSMENT.example.json) also keeps fidelity/stance towards different standards separate rather than forcing one global assessment onto the carrier.

For testimony, copying and dependence can be analysed without converting report count or graph degree into warrant. The separate [qualitative tawātur record](../../applications/daee-epistemics/TAWATUR-WARRANT.example.yaml) requires school-labelled, source- and subject-sensitive judgements; its two subjects can have different access/warrant dispositions while the proposition retains the same identity and unadjudicated objective truth. This record is an illustrative reconstruction, not an assessed historical proposition. The [broader cognitive-vindication account](RESEARCH-ATLAS.md#recognition-sound-cognition-and-correction) remains a distinct source route, not something replaced by this dependence model.

### Feasibility-first corrective descent

**Sound descent: G0 / G1 / G2 model comparison.** [Complete comparison](../../applications/daee-epistemics/SOUND-DESCENT-MODEL-COMPARISON.md) · [Field-dynamics contract](../../applications/daee-epistemics/NOETIC-FIELD-DYNAMICS.yaml) · [Current worked transition](../../applications/daee-epistemics/CORRECTIVE-TRANSITION.example.json).

The comparison distinguishes metaphor (G0), a proposed order-theoretic/route-ranked account (G1), and literal differentiable flow requiring additional structure (G2). G1 first excludes routes violating hard constraints, preserves disclosure/provenance and uncertainty, and only then compares admissible successors by a declared order. Hard constraints are not another dimension that a favourable score may outweigh. Newly discovered burdens can increase the ledger while improving the epistemic position; deleting unresolved burdens is not progress.

Fast episode-level correction and slow changes to represented standards, repertoires and analyses have different histories. In the current transition example, one criterion burden is addressed, a source-independence dependency is retained, and rereading returns **HOLD**. Neither admissibility nor pathway adequacy entails factive strict soundness. The [retained v1 example](../../applications/daee-epistemics/CORRECTIVE-TRANSITION.v1.example.json) is a supporting historical version, not another work. The source's unsupplied DAEE G2 structure must not be misread as denying the separately established conditional [T20 Fisher construction](RESULT-INDEX.md#fisher-geometry), which has a different state space and contract.

### Typed runtime semantic operators

**DAEE semantic-operator contract.** [Complete typed contract](../../applications/daee-epistemics/SEMANTIC-OPERATOR-CONTRACT.yaml) · [Schema](../../applications/daee-epistemics/SEMANTIC-OPERATOR-CONTRACT.schema.json) · [Fixtures](../../applications/daee-epistemics/SEMANTIC-OPERATOR-FIXTURES.yaml).

This is a supporting formal contract, not a conventional paper or a deployed runtime. Its seven operators give route pressure, event transition, field divergence, field curl, loop interruption, whole-state rereading and runtime closure explicit input/output types, targets, preconditions and owners. Route pressure ranks already eligible routes; divergence/curl need an explicit multi-node field. Acyclic residual propagation is not automatically a loop, and a single scalar summary supplies neither diagnostic.

The contract's predicate registry separately binds non-factive governance predicates and the claim-relative conjunction required for strict soundness. A successful parse, a control-state delta and terminal runtime status therefore cannot silently become truth or restoration. The [comparison's diagnostic discussion](../../applications/daee-epistemics/SOUND-DESCENT-MODEL-COMPARISON.md#divergencecurl-diagnostics-require-an-explicit-target-field) supplies the interpretation boundary. The contract's own `current` schema status does not adopt a literal differential theory of cognition.

### Latent-state learning and governed updates

**Dynamic orthing and latent-state learning.** [Complete application study](../../applications/latent-state-orthing/DYNAMIC-ORTHING-AND-LATENT-STATE-LEARNING.md) · [Four-level update coupling](../../applications/latent-state-orthing/UPDATE-COUPLING.yaml) · [Definitions](../../applications/latent-state-orthing/OSM-DYNAMICS-DEFINITIONS.yaml) · [Reported-source correspondence](../../applications/latent-state-orthing/OSM-CSCG-ORTHEME-CROSSWALK.yaml).

The study distinguishes inference with fixed analysis/model, representation learning, repertoire revision and analysis-version change. A world transition is not a learner update. Matching final representation geometry does not identify a learning trajectory or mechanism, and latent state to orthemic profile is a partial many-to-many relation. A latent split earns the stipulated analysis-relative admission only when its declared merger contrast exceeds the tolerance; orthogonality alone does not define an ortheme.

The update contract supplies per-level triggers, authority, invalidation, calibration, reopening and rollback. There is no silent blanket transport of old claims after changing the model or analysis; repertoire revision changes what the declared model distinguishes, not worldly facts. The OSM comparison is source-reported computational exemplification and constraint, not fresh empirical evidence for human noetics or metaphysics. Its external article/code are bounded references, not new acquisitions or an expanded research assignment here. The [fixture collection](../../applications/latent-state-orthing/DYNAMIC-FIXTURES.yaml) tests representational distinctions, not a biological mechanism.

### Somnus candidate inventory

**Somnus candidate inventory.** [Complete outline](../../applications/agentic-runtime/SOMNUS-CANDIDATE-INVENTORY.yaml) · [Application-family boundary](../../applications/agentic-runtime/README.md).

Fifteen candidate operations distinguish live capture and claimant checks from later recurrence assessment, intervention disposition, proposal production, downstream actuation and collective exchange. Recording an interruption, assessing an unresolved recurrence and proposing a repair are different operations with different inputs, events and residual behaviour. No-change, investigation and evidence requests remain legitimate outcomes; proposal generation is not forced by every assessment.

Every candidate is explicitly outline-only, externally/downstream owned and **not implemented here**. The inventory does not install executable skills, a scheduler, durable ledger service, learning loop or automatic authority. Its value is the discriminating contract for a future implementation, not an existing runtime feature count.

### Guarded writeback adoption outline

**Hermes writeback adoption profile.** [Complete outline](../../applications/agentic-runtime/HERMES-WRITEBACK-ADOPTION-PROFILE.yaml).

The profile treats guarded writeback as a first-class downstream actuator: proposal, review, approval/rejection, validation, dry run and application/reversion remain distinct. Assessment, intervention disposition, mutation proposal, authorisation, applied mutation and later-outcome evaluation have separate records. Ordinary memory/fact/skill targets are retained alongside governance targets, rather than excluding normal reflective writeback from the architecture.

Its external source-code, licence, commit and test verification are recorded as pending. Automatic proposal, authorisation, application and writeback are prohibited; the profile does not demonstrate implemented writeback, empirical superiority or governed learning. The filename's word “adoption” supplies no adoption or execution authority.

### Collective Somnus and transclusion

**Collective Somnus/transclusion profile.** [Complete outline](../../applications/agentic-runtime/COLLECTIVE-SOMNUS-TRANSCLUSION-PROFILE.yaml).

The profile distinguishes independent type-convergent activity, provenance-preserving exchange followed by local reassessment, and intentionally coordinated council activity. Transclusion makes source-preserved material available; it does not make it locally governing or executable. Evidence artifacts, represented standards and operational DSL/IR have different receiving gates. Source envelopes remain immutable, while receiving assessments retain local versions, residuals, redactions and dissent.

The positive design admits plural local placements, bounded agreement with dissent and unresolved conflict—not only unanimous closure. Shared types provide no transport service by themselves; multiple operators imply neither independence nor truth or tawātur. This is an unimplemented downstream outline. Its local-authorisation, source-authenticity, semantic-reconstruction and execution-witness requirements are not bypassed by receiving or parsing a packet.


## Common histories, shared stores and actual batches: the Eighth sequence

The Eighth works ask whether several separately proved mechanisms really describe **the same history and state**. Their [original result/evidence guide][t08-guide] distinguishes twelve result scopes and four physical suites. Repeated imports or several result bindings do not create several independent replays. The following six work families retain the successive source contracts.

### Exact common histories and the typed lifecycle

Read the [bounded lifecycle argument][t08-lifecycle] and its [declaration guide][t08-theorem-guide]. The common interface exposes local admission and effects without assuming the safety conclusion. Exact inverse state maps and a timestamp-erasing event correspondence recover every original finite history, including damaged states. The typed specialisation retains complete successors, ordered paths, nonces, custody and separate installation/repair counters. A fresh installation may leave the displayed rule unchanged while increasing its version/history; literal state idempotence would be false.

A stronger `Aligned` relation, not the earlier weak snapshot, connects actual preparation, attempt, authentic certification and recorded or unrecorded delivery to primitive histories. False preparation can still leave commitments. The original raw epochs 2 and 3 remain unchanged; proof-side subtraction does not legitimise commands below the base. Initial alignment establishes the stated post-boundary class, not a fabricated prehistory. Correct numerical epoch shape alone does not authenticate the full policy. The bound 3,013-byte witness constructs genuine installation then repair; it does not discover a fault-free path or verify a physical observation/clock service. Exact results: [common correspondence][t08-common-evidence], [typed histories][t08-typed-evidence] and [actual-call lifecycle][t08-lifecycle-evidence].

### Aggregate cancellation retains partial effects

The [cancellation successor][t08-cancellation] extends the actual operation class rather than reading stronger claims into the old lifecycle. A valid call writes complete-envelope tombstones at intact responders **before** its Boolean threshold is decided. A false reply can therefore leave a durable partial effect. Distinct acknowledgements accumulated over several calls are not the same as the responder count in the current call.

A true call yields an intact selected tombstone and blocks that exact full envelope over every later finite trace of the enlarged class. The converse Boolean implication fails. Cleanup does not undo an earlier landing; changing the path order, action, successor or nonce changes the command. The actor-string equality is a model property, not authentication of a real person. The [original declaration and review route][t08-cancel-evidence] retains these positive results without inventing a global nonce ban, authentic time acquisition or general progress theorem.

### Expiry is not clock authentication

The [reference-clock extension][t08-clock] explicitly introduces a new numerical update, absent from the accepted compiled service. Arbitrary set-time preserves structural alignment because the common state has no clock field. The separately named monotone trace class forbids backward time while permitting equal values and arbitrarily large forward jumps.

Live admission implies `now < leaseEnd`. A fixed expired envelope remains rejected through every monotone continuation. The same unlanded, uncancelled envelope can revive after rollback outside that class; a genuine tombstone survives even rollback. Updating the world's annotation does not refresh the actor's stored observation. The [scoped formal result][t08-clock-evidence] is thus useful safety mathematics, not proof of clock authenticity, elapsed deadlines, bounded skew or service progress. A large valid numerical jump can exhaust a lease without doing work.

### Actual batch success and complete-envelope freshness

This is one evolving actual-batch work: read the [initial refinement][t08-batch], [native/source successor][t08-batch-successor], [exact surviving-trial characterisation][t08-batch-survival], and [corrected retry analysis][t08-batch-retry]. The unchanged four-path `runBatch` refines an actual operation history, retaining partial preparations and cleanup. For its fixed action and literal withholding flag, at most one landing occurs without needing the stronger alignment hypotheses. Existence of a landing is a separate result.

With the specified actor/world admission to the same full successor and synchronised, unrevoked good-root policies, exactly one landing occurs **iff** a predicted envelope selects only good roots and has no pre-existing exact tombstone at those selected roots. This exact characterisation needs neither a global nonce high-water condition nor freshness of every trial. The surviving-path predicate is proof-side state information, not an observation oracle supplied to the actor. Fault-bound and authenticity assumptions for other alignment results remain their own.

The corrected source retry is instructive: an actor can receive a new policy before the roots, fail all trials, and nevertheless tombstone its envelopes through cleanup. Later policy delivery alone does not rescue the same envelopes. A genuinely new valid envelope can recover; a separately authorised changed lease can do so even with old nonce numbers. This is not permission to alter a real grant. The [two-batch source projection][t08-native-evidence] retains the 3,013-byte installation/repair fixture and exact histories. It does not certify the surrounding IO runner, arbitrary interleavings or a physical retry service. The [batch result][t08-batch-evidence] preserves the distinction between a synthetic `closed=true` proposal-rejection record and an actual cancellation certificate.

### Shared storage is not independent label memory

Read [SharedAlias's result and limits][t08-shared] and the [retained review supplement][t08-shared-review]. One actual root's shared store can acquire a commitment through a selected label and expose it at an unselected alias. Literal pullback into independent label-local memories is therefore obstructed. The positive replacement is a **weak forward simulation**: exact plant/control and genuine receipts are retained, commitments are restricted to selected labels, and shared revocations/tombstones may add vetoes. Concrete permission implies abstract permission, not conversely.

Under the fixed map and lifetime-fault contract, finite traces preserve genuine landing commands, requesters and times; typed admission and exact successor/custody/counter results follow. Internal proof-witness steps do not manufacture additional acknowledgements or form an actor-visible costed transcript. The compact seven-label witness readies five gate occurrences through four actual stores and demonstrates one ABC rule installation, not source-text repair. The [obstruction/simulation][t08-alias-evidence] and [typed consequences][t08-alias-typed-evidence] supply no hidden-map secrecy, opaque-search lower bound or complete shared runtime/lifecycle composition. The review supplement also preserves failed wrapper attempts and the explicit withdrawal of an unrecorded interpreter-version attribution.

### A protected schedule is not an unconditional time bound

The [shared-controller argument][t08-controller] gives a positive finite schedule without trusting success receipts or reading hidden faults: synchronise, then prepare, attempt, cancel and close every prescribed path. Authentic source admission, accurate starting observation, good service slots, fresh complete commands and a protected window are actual premises. The [execution clarification][t08-execution] identifies the whole interpreter as a classical/noncomputable mathematical reference—not an extracted or natively executed successor to the separate four-path source batch.

For the fixed seven-label/five-label order, the reference counts 259 logical events; one admissible world makes the first 20 trials leave the Plant unchanged and the 21st attain the installation. This is [sharpness for that order/world][t08-attained-evidence], not optimality over all controllers. The [finite bad-store extension][t08-bad-store-evidence] permits any finite number of writes at the fixed bad roots while preserving the final good-state effect. Total length includes those added events and can be arbitrarily delayed; an infinite hostile prefix has no termination guarantee. Read the [protected-controller result][t08-controller-evidence] for the complete contract. Neither event counting nor unchanged source-gate code establishes a physical service deadline.

## Twentieth live research and v6 synthesis

These families group scientific questions and their developed arguments. The [live collection](../../research/t20-live-20261009/README.md) preserves the checkpoint sequence; the [v6 collection](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/README.md) supplies the later synthesis and precise contribution register. A checkpoint, revised copy or review receipt is a reading form or evidence item, rather than an additional discovery. Mathematical assumptions, affirmative philosophical warrant and each source's recorded evidence level remain distinct.

### Calibrated identification from exact laws and finite observations

The [single-calibration construction](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP01/t20-next-frontier-20261008/single-calibration-design/RESULT.md) encodes finite route multiplicities in prime valuations of exact rational response probabilities. Known finite root signatures, independent route response and the complete guarded effect record are part of the instrument; “one calibration” does not mean one finite observation or one scalar for every effect. The [reconciliation of the identification chain](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/calibrated_prose/REPORT.md) connects this positive result to bounded-count integer rounding, countable signatures with finite inventories, noise allowances and adaptive-rate lower bounds. A supplied count ceiling makes exact separated recovery possible; arbitrary approximate frequencies do not supply exact rational population values. These results recover anonymous counts under a response model, with numerical precision, sample cost and productive-source identity remaining separate questions.

### Persistent grouped mixtures and calibration invariants

Holding a latent inventory fixed while redrawing conditionally independent emissions turns [grouped mixture identification](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP02/t20-next-frontier-20261008/grouped-mixture-identification/RESULT.md) into a finite-atomic moment problem. Moments through order $2k-1$ identify a law among competitors with at most $k$ atoms; support certification against arbitrary positive representing laws needs the stronger moment information stated in the source. Exact Hankel singularity also needs exact zero testing. At a genuinely interior setting of an unknown calibration shared across components, [the invariant theorem](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP05/t20-next-frontier-20261008/grouped-calibration-invariants/RESULT.md) recovers the weights and zero-atom flag and, when positive-count mass is present, the primitive positive integer count vector, leaving an absolute scale ambiguity. The later source reconciliation distinguishes finite-catalogue sampling, stable support estimation and crossed retained-label interventions from these exact-law statements. Equal observed pairs alone do not establish a persistent latent process; that assumption must belong to the experiment.

### Interaction calibration and the boundary of absence detection

The calibration programme first characterizes an exact interaction anchor, sampled-value gauges and isolated unary ambiguities. A finite panel cannot recover an arbitrary calibration function between its samples. Its absence obstruction concerns unbounded nuisance counts and arbitrary valid pointwise Cauchy names; it leaves exact law distinction and positive semidecision intact. [Bounded interaction detection](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP06/t20-next-frontier-20261008/bounded-interaction-detection/RESULT.md) then changes that contract by supplying a valid total count ceiling: separated thresholds decide support and anchored multiplicities, with unanchored unary counts confined to an explicit budgeted integer fibre. An actually supplied or discovered interaction cover gives another sufficient route. These are successive conditional results, not a universal decoder. [The complete reconciliation](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/calibrated_prose/REPORT.md) also preserves the earlier source-local formula erratum without treating its later rediscovery as a new theorem.

### Retained-threshold replay and robust count testing

The [calibration reconciliation](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/calibrated_prose/REPORT.md) connects the retained-replay results to the wider identification programme.

Unknown within-route dependence admits genuine Joe-copula aliases even when fresh endpoint laws and coordinate calibrations agree. Retaining the same thresholds across different commands exposes a joint law that fresh independent probes do not reveal. [The robust four-probability panel](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP07/t20-next-frontier-20261008/finite-panel-replay-robustness/RESULT.md) uses $t=1/[3(n+1)]$ and a simultaneous error box of radius $1/[10000(n+1)^2]$ to separate a specified count from every wrong positive finite count. Its finite-sample rule tests the reference model; a same-count nonreference model can reject. The [adaptive-rate bounds](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP07/READ_FIRST.md) and [charged adaptive-second-face extension](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP08/READ_FIRST.md) preserve the fourth-power hard-pair barrier for their exact replay instruments. Known/selectable true rates, independent groups, charged first probes and unconditional terminal correctness matter; none of these results certifies arbitrary global copula independence from a finite grid.

### Sharp skyline information and finite geometric readout

For the specified $n$ versus $n+1$ hard pair, [the sharp skyline theorem](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP12/t20-next-frontier-20261008/sharp-skyline-information/RESULT.md) obtains $D(Q_0 \parallel Q_1) \sim \log^2(m)/(4m^4)$ and $H^2(Q_0,Q_1)\sim\log^2(m)/(8m^4)$, where $m=n+1$ and this Hellinger convention has no factor $1/2$. An alternative-only stratum makes the opposite KL divergence infinite; it is retained in the experiment. Exact-skyline testing therefore has fixed-confidence independent-vector complexity $\Theta(n^4/\log^2 n)$. [The finite rational score](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP12/t20-next-frontier-20261008/finite-geometric-score/RESULT.md) matches that vector order eventually, with expected total probes $O(n^4)$ and a separate worst-case bound. Its cutoff $n_0$ is not effective in the retained result, so it supplies neither an automatic all-$n$ budget nor total-query optimality. [The final checkpoint guide](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP12/READ_FIRST.md) identifies the completed bound and preserves CP10–11's weaker estimates as history.

### Qualitative tensors and shared-witness semantics

The [qualitative tensor construction](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP08/t20-next-frontier-20261008/qualitative-tensor-transport/RESULT.md) represents finite extensional relations by Boolean characteristic tensors and proves compatibility with specified conjunction, projection, padding, permutation, equality and shared-variable operations. Existential projection uses Boolean disjunction; arithmetic contraction instead counts witnesses. Nonnegative support preserves the positive operations, while negation needs an additional operation and signed cancellation can break the transfer. Explicit low-arity completion supplies the needed shape guards. The construction also shows why separately satisfiable scalar summaries do not recover an oriented joint relation. It is useful mathematics of representation and binding; extension is not thereby intension, and a semantic representation does not establish actual obtaining. The accompanying [ontology and source reports](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP08/READ_FIRST.md) retain their philosophical comparisons separately.

### Modal transport and coherent selected bearers

On a finite connected alignment graph, [coherent-bearer transport, §§10.5–10.10](../../foundations/received-actuality-and-intrinsic-necessity.md#105-coherent-assignments-across-locally-indexed-worlds) pulls local acceptance conditions back to a root and intersects them with the common fixed points of cycle holonomy. This distinguishes a coherent selected witness from a globally trivial alignment atlas; a nontrivial permutation can still fix one bearer. Restricting the domain can also delete a supplier needed to witness reception unless the stated witness-preservation bridge is supplied. The inherited [singleton-fibre correction](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/specialist_b_history/REPORT.md) removes a redundant guard: one eligible carrier at each world plus eligibility-preserving edges already forces coherence of the selected section. Its triangular swap countercontrol belongs to a weaker nonsingleton setting. These are mathematical consistency and transport results; numerical bearer identity and metaphysical eligibility remain additional interpretations. The [earlier finite mathematical report](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/a7ff0e33d1a299db640041c46b1af81ca70851f6/research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP13/t20-next-frontier-20261008/modal-identity-transport/RESULT.md) remains a historical edition; its recorded review and execution scopes are not transferred to the synthesis.

### Graph-constrained parity continuation

The [graph continuation theorem](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP19/graph-constrained-continuation-20261009/RESULT.md) studies Boolean states, edge-local updates $x_i\leftarrow x_ix_j$, fixed silent action words and terminal unary expectations. Pulling a unary question backwards yields a parity question. For a nonempty mask $S$, it transports the known CNOT parity-synthesis cost $2\tau_G(S)-|S|-1$ into this continuation contract; $\tau_G(S)$ is the minimum connected support size and is infinite across components. Accessible masks need not be downward closed: on a path, a larger parity can be cheaper than a disconnected submask. After adjoining the constant, the accessible questions determine the sharp continuous coordinate requirement over the full probability simplex. The cost and pullback core has source-recorded Lean evidence; rank/topology and tree corollaries have separate ordinary arguments. Bits, samples, feedback, joint terminal questions and physical memory require different contracts.

### Missing support and the future cost of warranted inquiry

For a finite admitted proof catalogue, persistent authenticated evidence and scoped verdicts, [warranted discrimination](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/warranted-discrimination-research-20261009/RESULT.md) represents each candidate by missing support and terminal checking cost. Under the full single-token acquisition contract, Pareto normalization produces a canonical frontier: two states agree precisely when every additional evidence set gives the same achievable terminal-cost function. A restricted acquisition menu can admit a coarser quotient. Acquiring evidence subtracts it from missing support and normalizes again. A separate finite-test completion criterion and Bellman argument charge both queries and final proof checking. [The formal companion](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/warranted-discrimination-kernel-20261009/RESULT.md) certifies the support/cost-pair theorem at its stated abstraction level. It does not supply evidence authenticity, actual epistemic warrant, proof-object realization or the whole Bellman result. Defeat, expiry or changing rule validity would require a richer state.

### Finite certificate compilation with cycles and sharing

The [compilation result](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/certificate-compilation-boundary-20261009/RESULT.md) connects a finite ground rule grammar to finite terminal support/cost frontiers. With $n$ fully scoped judgments, substitutable premises and nonnegative additive costs, repeated-judgment cycles can be pruned and the tree frontier stabilizes within $n$ rounds. Shared-node DAG checking changes the optimization: one proof node may serve several uses, so pruning an intermediate proof solely by its local support/cost pair can discard the globally cheaper shared proof. The source supplies a counterexample and a finite global enumeration route. These are written general arguments and finite implementation controls. They do not turn an unrestricted calculus into a finite grammar or provide a decision procedure for HasE inhabitation.

### Explanatory bridges and their exact logical premises

The [plural-explanation audit](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP15/plural-explanation-theorem-audit/RESULT.md) gives a short positive proof: choose $Q$ explaining all actual facts and $R$ explaining $Q$; because $R$ belongs to the totality, complete transitivity makes $Q$ explain itself. A countable control and an atomless regular-open construction retain plural PSR while dropping both transitivity clauses and output projection. The [particular-explanans audit](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP15/particular-explanans-bridge-audit/RESULT.md) isolates a different reverse-parthood gap and supplies sufficient repairs. [Total-explainer uniqueness](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP18/total-explainer-uniqueness/RESULT.md) uses cross-membership, member projection and No Extended Circles; [its supplemented Boolean control](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP18/total-explainer-uniqueness/BOOLEAN_SCOPE_ADDENDUM.md) still allows several explainers of a narrower target that excludes them. Identity of an explanatory fact does not supply bearer identity. Finally, [the modal refinement](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP21/complete-explanation-modal-review-20261009/symmetry-addendum.md) uses symmetry alone to transport the admitted necessary-adequacy implications; full S5 is unnecessary for that local step. These are explicit conditional proofs and model controls, with the philosophical interpretation and actual-world premise warrant remaining separate.

### Centered self-knowledge and practical conformance

The [centered construction](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP13/t20-next-frontier-20261008/de-se-sovereignty/RESULT.md) enriches an epistemic-free or compatible base model with centers $(w,b)$, an indexical denoting $b$, explicit perspective switches, and singleton epistemic accessibility. It gives full factive centered knowledge while preserving the old world, bearers, acts, policies and productive relations. Metaphysical accessibility remains a distinct same-bearer relation. Thus truthful first-person knowledge alone cannot add a new non-epistemic uniqueness consequence to a retained reduced model. The source also gives a positive recognition–conformance route: independently establish a constitutive defect and a practical norm, then correct self-knowledge can mediate the exclusion of knowingly enacting it. The construction does not establish actual perfect cognition, certify a full plural-original ontology, or show that the retained policy acts on recognized first-person reasons. That operative-basing link remains an additional interpretation.

### Received existence and affirmative original adequacy

This source argument begins with actual warranted correction and distinguishes a received thing, its completed realization and the resource that makes that completion adequate. The current claim owner preserves the total received-field argument without replacing it by “each part lacks a source, therefore the whole does,” or by a first-event argument. [CP23's affirmative necessity report](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/affirmative-intrinsic-necessity-20261009/REPORT.md) then attributes intrinsic existential adequacy to the same actual original bearer. Its modal step is an openly defended rational judgment about self-adequacy, not a deduction from anti-self-production alone. The [current foundational claims FND-001–006 and FND-008](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/foundations/CURRENT_FOUNDATIONAL_CLAIMS.json) keep completion, realization and support transport with their distinct premises. This is positive philosophical argument with defeasible warrant; it supplies neither every necessary effect nor a complete unique divine history.

### Common-source unity and the scope of nonduplication

The current common-source appraisal judges one actual complete original of the connected created field sufficiently warranted for provisional belief under its stated source/perfection and coverage premises. Its earlier plural-explanation, source-relative total-explainer and modal-actualization studies distinguish a conditional theorem from warrant for its productive premises. [The later exclusivity appraisal](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/affirmative-original-exclusivity-20261009/REPORT.md) focuses on the same numerical effect, actual state and productive respect: two allegedly entire original manifestations conflict only with the independently warranted local noncoincidence judgment in place. Merely having different intrinsic acts is insufficient. The [current claims FND-007 and FND-009–012](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/foundations/CURRENT_FOUNDATIONAL_CLAIMS.json) also distinguish finite gluing of already eligible singleton fibres from establishing eligibility, coverage or numerical identity. Coactive restricted rivals retain their explanatory force without becoming certified complete plural originals; the appraisal does not exclude every disconnected original across every possible field.

### Knowing production, wise determination and normative reasons

The agency sequence separates cognition, a willing bearer and determination of this particular completed act. [Wise determination](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/affirmative-wise-determination-20261009/REPORT.md) develops a source-attributed almsgiving test: foreknowledge of a good response does not replace that response's actual realization, while achieving that end explains why the literal transfer can become unnecessary. It is a concrete argument about means and ends, not a proof of the only possible history. [Intrinsic normative reasons](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/intrinsic-normative-reasons-20261009/REPORT.md) distinguishes due right and value from threatened punishment and actual desire; its normative premise remains substantive. The [completed Avicenna comparison](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP23/intellectual-production-rival-20261009/REPORT.md) recognizes intellectual willing and locates disagreements about identity, purpose and succession. It is stopped at the owner's boundary, with no certified rival defeat or reopening implied. [FND-013–022](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/foundations/CURRENT_FOUNDATIONAL_CLAIMS.json) retain the separate knowledge, will, wisdom, authority, obligation and veracity claims.

### Recognition, operative basing and source-specific attestation

The [rational basing continuation](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP12/t20-next-frontier-20261008/rational-basing-continuation/RESULT.md) corrects a weak attention objection to Dretske: the inspected present-use condition already requires the relevant informative use. It then poses the stronger question about actual competent inference, retained premise knowledge and the operative factive or nonfactive ground. [The System R successor](../../research/t20-live-20261009/checkpoints/CP20/compound-ground-necessity-20261009/REPORT.md) supplies an eight-valued countervaluation: a complete material ground and a theorem target can both be designated while the relevant internal conditional and sensitivity formula fail. The ground still entails the theorem; this is not a knowledge-closure counterexample. The [current recognition and attestation claims FND-023–034](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/foundations/CURRENT_FOUNDATIONAL_CLAIMS.json) distinguish basic recognition from prior faculty certification, mediated identification from universal firsthand access, cumulative indications from a fixed testimony count, and source-disclosing performance from already trusted testimony. The [lunar request study](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/sources/8b9d6cce24bcd2249b7d23acab8b0b03e95a4190e47d80d07b78063b316d5389/REPORT.md) retains explicit lower-link transmission support and positive, defeasible attestation force; Anas's unidentified event informant and the stronger expected-publicity objection remain unresolved. Commissioned guidance can warrant source-attributed content without unique autograph recovery, while continuing access needs its separately defended premise. Delivery, interpretation and normative authority also require their own arguments.

### Conditioning error and finite mediation

[V6-C01](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/CONDITIONING_CROSS_CONTRIBUTION.md) proves the common-likelihood bound $\operatorname{TV}(B_Lp,B_Lq)\le\min(1,\delta/\max(A,B))$ for $0\le L\le1$, positive normalizers $A,B$, and $\delta=\operatorname{TV}(p,q)$. A distinct relative-likelihood error term and endpoint transport term can be added; the joint sum is not claimed sharp and the likelihood itself still needs warrant. [V6-C04](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/type_collective/ROBUST_FINITE_MEDIATOR_BOUND.md) adds a sharp one-horizon event-TV allowance to the inherited finite-mediator Gaussian capture bound: $\min\{1,2\Phi(NL/(2n^{1/2}))-1+\eta\}$. The mediator must be complete, have at most $N$ values and satisfy the stated conditional-independence/reference-law contract. The construction does not preserve a prescribed output marginal or produce a coherent all-horizon process.

### Additional moment directions, reset quotients and hidden activation

[V6-C03](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/APPROXIMATE_CONTINUATION_AUGMENTATION.md) first removes information already retained in a fixed linear space, then uses the residual spectrum of $q$ accessible Walsh questions to bound $k$ additional moment coordinates: $\sum_{j>k}\lambda_j\le4q\varepsilon^2$ is necessary for uniform error $\varepsilon$ over the full simplex. The zero-error rank endpoint is attained; positive-error spectral truncation is not asserted minimax sufficient. [V6-C06](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/multivalued_reset/RESULT.md) identifies the exact continuation quotient of a finite full product under all coordinate resets and total coupled actions: each coordinate is reduced to its contextual value classes, and their product is the coarsest sufficient state. [V6-C11](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/type_collective/cumulative_damping_prose/ACTIVATION_BOUNDARY.md) supplies the complementary boundary. Distinct hidden lognormal lifts can share every old moment history, yet new quadratic damping of the hidden coordinate makes the laws determinate and finitely moment-separable. Enlarging the operation family changes sufficiency; no uniform separating degree or reconstruction algorithm follows.

### Literal HasE independence and the limits of HasJ completion

The exact [original-identity source](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/sources/5ee0cbdab27fa18866d5e7b1b6b13131bbdb649b5bacab839a67c46e11619d08/OriginalIdentityNoninhabitation.lean) and [its controls](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/sources/1654c2cc234cc2eb8155d9b84590dd3b2b884cc01ce62f1a8b9779aa9d59cda9/IdentityReviewControls.lean) prove that no polynomial inhabits the unchanged HasE $x+0$ identity and no closed proof inhabits its internal negation; the identity is valid in the faithful FullPER interpretation. This is the later outcome for that literal calculus, beyond the earlier written negative-route tests. HasJ is a separately defined positive completion. [V6-C07](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/type_collective/JOINT_COMPLETENESS_BOUNDARY.md) joins its source enumerability to non-enumerable faithful semantic validity, while the [explicit Raw gap](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/sources/c445a2c1d5f8115c11109e8498472dec5d920a93a61c6778061aa4a6ee8fa6ea/ExplicitRawGap.lean) gives a named valid-but-unprovable type using a diagnostic interpretation; the diagnostic is not a metaphysical rival. [V6-C08](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/type_collective/GraphBridgeNegative.lean) excludes joint inhabitation of two typed graph bridges in HasE without deciding each separately. [V6-C05](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/type_collective/ERASURE_WORK_BOUNDARY.md) supplies an accepted proof pair with identical erasure but source charges 22/34 and exporter counts 2/3, showing precisely which cost information erasure loses. These are source-calculus and cost statements, with their own formal/ordinary evidence boundaries.

### Finite histories, weak repair and law-based restoration

[V6-C09](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/type_collective/pd_checker_annex/FINITE_HISTORY_TRANSPORT.md) transports a checker's one-step **joint** observation/state pushforward equality to every finite recorded-history law under a common randomized history policy, matching initial erasure and identical initial-history/label conventions. Separate observation and state marginals do not suffice, and the result supplies no unbounded liveness. [V6-C10](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/type_collective/formal_annex_residue/LINEAR_SLACK_EXTENSION.md) shows that every positive linear slack coefficient admits the uninformed constant report $1/2$ on a nondegenerate parameter interval for the stated weak repair predicate. It does not address strict improvement or arbitrary losses. The inherited [V6-R03 reconciliation](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/terminal_annex_prose65/REPORT.md), with its [bounded review](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/early_remaining/finite_law_synthesis_review/REPORT.md), connects finite-catalogue product interpolation, sticky regenerative restoration and stopped permissions. At most $\max(1,m-1)$ independent product observations allow a bounded statistic with prescribed expectations across $m$ known distinct laws; this is not deterministic model identification or a uniform conditioning bound. Exit hazards and recovery costs remain separate.

### Law geometry beyond local scores

The inherited [V6-R02 joint-hull construction](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/geometry_audit_annex/JOINT_HULL_OBSTRUCTION.md) gives coupled and product truth sets with the same ambient score functions, all their jets, affine hull and block projections, while one admits strict local common repair and the other admits no weak common repair at the specified interior witness. Their admissible truth-indexed score lists differ: the joint feasible truths carry information absent from the local summaries. The [signed-energy reconciliation](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/signed_energy_prose/REPORT.md) retains full square-weighted hierarchy, determinacy and integrability assumptions, and separates static coercive-SOS representation from a dynamic PDE conclusion. The [Hessian/Gaussian reconciliation](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/root_math/hessian_gaussian_prose/REPORT.md) distinguishes metric, law, action-clock and path-time claims. These reports extend the route to the existing tensor-moment, coercive-successor, Fisher-audit and finite-sidecar entries; they do not duplicate those works or grant every local geometric model an implementation in cognition.

### Twentieth v6 cumulative scientific synthesis

The [whole-corpus synthesis](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/synthesis/WHOLE_CORPUS_SYNTHESIS.md) connects foundations, typed calculi, controller mathematics, empirical work, occurrence interpretation and tensor/law geometry. Its scientific thesis is operation-relative: a representation must retain the distinctions needed by the allowed future questions, actions and source-warrant conditions. The [fifteen-result register](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v6/synthesis/CROSS_CONTRIBUTIONS.json) distinguishes eleven new/refined contributions from four inherited reconciliations; it does not announce fifteen independent discoveries. For older work, the [eighteen-family historical crosswalk](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/research-v5/CUMULATIVE_CROSSWALK.json) and [individual-result disposition index](../../research/t20-v6-20261012/metadata/FINAL_REGISTERED_RESULT_DISPOSITIONS.json) retain original owners. The substantial chapter reports below are reading routes to those arguments and their correction histories, not new works for each manifest, duplicate body, review receipt or implementation capture. Existing C02 finite-sidecar and R04 occurrence-feasibility entries remain their catalogue homes.
