## AR8R: identification, correction and warranted application

The [AR8R collection][c03-ar8r-guide] develops a recurring question: **what extra information, authority or structure makes a proposed conclusion follow from an observed profile?** Its historical theorem notes, philosophical candidates, research proposals and later formalisation answer different parts of that question. The [existing result owners][c03-ar8r-results] preserve their identities and scientific standing. The sections below introduce the substantive works; their audits, checkers and custody records remain supporting material at those owners.

### AR8R historical theorem corpus

The [canonical historical registry][c03-ar8r-origin] is the source directory for the earlier theorem corpus. Its ten [mathematical and philosophical families][c03-ar8r-families] cover profile factorisation, causal contrasts, fixed points and restoration, same-token nonorigination, higher-order blindness, route adequacy, ground and attribute arguments, rival structures, and source interpretation. Search the registry by an existing theorem label to find its statement, assumptions, family and source references. Its identity counts are not independent-discovery counts, and its historical source references are not all available as standalone public proof files.

The [later recovery overlay][c03-ar8r-recovery] is necessary when interpreting that older registry. It distinguishes recovered exact payloads from the T150 role-preserving replacement, the T366 semantic reconstruction and unresolved historical identities. Exact recovery also leaves T354's formal defect in place. This composite route preserves the scientific corpus and its status distinctions without converting each custody row or family relationship into another paper.

### AR8R recovered transfer and restoration notes

The [direct note lookup](RESULT-INDEX.md#ar8r-research-notes-direct-source-lookup) opens every retained exact note by its existing title or label.

The [V8 collection][c03-ar8r-v8] preserves eighteen exact historical notes, with [direct source paths and audit status for each identity][c03-ar8r-v8-index]. Together they make several substantive distinctions that are easy to lose in a summary of the programme.

| Question | Argument and consequence |
|---|---|
| When can a formal theorem constrain a target domain? | [T130–132][c03-ar8r-v8-t130] require target antecedents to reach the formal theorem and formal conclusions to reflect back, with the same token, respect and dependence relation. Validity on the interpretation's attained image is enough; a countermodel outside it need not defeat that particular application. These guards permit conditional transfer while preventing direct transfer by resemblance. |
| What does an auxiliary signal contribute? | [T133–134][c03-ar8r-v8-t133] show that an adapted signal sampled only from the existing transcript can be simulated internally. Finite zero-error, almost-sure bounded-depth computation then admits a deterministic seed. A state-sensitive signal may escape that restriction while still revealing only a coordinate irrelevant to the registered target. |
| What authorizes a conclusion or a final status? | [T137 and T142][c03-ar8r-v8-t137] distinguish least-fixed-point authorization from an independently chosen coinductive semantics. T135–136 and T141 explain why summary adequacy, finite policy stabilization and burden emptiness cannot establish irreversible future completeness by themselves. [T143][c03-ar8r-v8-t143] shows that a model-conservative bridge cannot force a previously unentailed old-language conclusion. |
| Does an oriented powers network establish grounding? | [T139–140][c03-ar8r-v8-t139] first block an equivariant direction choice when an automorphism swaps the linked pair, then distinguish an independently supplied asymmetry from the further claim that the oriented relation is worldly grounding. T138 supplies a compatible many-sorted combination as a relative nonintegration control. |

The later group addresses restorative paths. **[T351][c03-ar8r-v8-t351]** identifies an untouched error-effective path as an obstacle to its declared stable-pathway condition. **[T352][c03-ar8r-v8-t352]** adds path completeness, effective and sound local repair, no newly created error path, independently fixed target adequacy, and custody with reread to give guarded sufficiency. **[T353][c03-ar8r-v8-t353]** supplies the self-sealing binary-rule example: clamping one answer changes the current output, while a repaired discrimination rule changes the counterfactual behavior. A sufficiently sound downstream decoder can also intercept every harmful route; intervention at the original deformation is not required.

The [audit and repair owner][c03-ar8r-v8-repairs] preserves two material qualifications. T351's proof replacement uses the unchanged-path clause; a stronger assertion of currently live target error needs an additional live-source premise. **[T354][c03-ar8r-v8-t354] remains blocked** because its displayed strict-improvement relation is not reflexive and therefore is not the asserted preorder. The related [common-model replacement for T150][c03-ar8r-v8-rp] and [declared-profile reconstruction for T366][c03-ar8r-v8-rc] are separately identified post-campaign proposals. They preserve elementary nonentailment and factorisation arguments without recovering the missing originals or supplying new general mathematical novelty.

### AR8R graphs, authentication and fixed points

**Graph-only root-authentication factorisation boundary (AR8R-T227).** [Read the note and proof][c03-ar8r-t227]. A directed provenance graph records which roots can reach an item. For a nonempty independently specified class of admissible root-authentication assignments, the graph alone determines every item's authenticated support exactly when every root's authentication bit is already fixed across that class. The short proof evaluates support at each root itself, using reflexive reachability. The contribution is a precise test for what a provenance graph leaves undetermined: a graph can propagate authenticated support once authentication is supplied, but reachability does not supply it.

**Strict progress and finite unary coinductive collapse (AR8R-T228).** [Read the note and proof][c03-ar8r-t228]. For the finite operator that adds facts and their one-edge consequences, a strictly increasing natural-number rank exists exactly when the graph is acyclic. That certificate makes the least and greatest fixed points coincide with fact-reachable vertices. The converse implication from fixed-point agreement to acyclicity fails: a fact-seeded two-vertex cycle can already fill the graph. These are scoped written results in familiar graph/factorisation families; neither gives a new authentication procedure or a metaphysical foundation theorem.

**[Finite positive-Horn coinductive truth residual guard][c03-ar8r-horn].** If facts are true and all Horn rules preserve an independently specified truth set, the least model is true. The greatest model is true exactly when its additional self-supporting residual is true. The short argument isolates that extra truth burden; circular support does not discharge it. Read the note under its existing recovery identity, `AR8R-HR-P236P242-T236-v1`: the [collision owner][c03-ar8r-horn-collision] records that the emitted T236 label names a different proposition from canonical V5 T236, and its historical T235 dependency is not the current V5 T235 theorem. The visible payload is preserved; no new theorem origin or present revalidation follows.

### AR8R hidden matching and the value of adaptivity

**Candidate 1 — exact hidden-matching threshold complexity.** [Read the theorem packet][c03-ar8r-matching]. An unknown arbitrary partial matching in a complete bipartite graph with sides of sizes `m` and `n` is accessible only through deterministic edge-membership queries. For `1 ≤ t ≤ min(m,n)`, deciding whether the matching has at least `t` edges has exact worst-case adaptive query depth

\[
D(m,n,t)=mn-\binom{t}{2}.
\]

The upper bound scans rows, deleting both endpoints of a found edge; the adversary lower bound uses a residual matching and disjoint charges supported by König's theorem. Nonadaptive querying needs `mn` queries. For a `2 × 2` instance with threshold `2`, the formula gives three adaptive queries against four nonadaptive ones. This is an exact information-access result for the stated oracle, rather than a claim about arbitrary matching algorithms. The packet records bounded finite checks alongside the written argument; external mathematical and prior-art review and owner adoption remain open. Its later provenance-label extension belongs to the separate PMR proposal series.

### AR8R causal landing and lower-order blindness

**Matched-intervention burden-landing certification (AR8R-T299)** and **lower-order alignment can be blind to causal restoration (AR8R-T300)** are two distinct [historical notes][c03-ar8r-t299], [with the parity construction here][c03-ar8r-t300]. T299 asks whether an observation profile determines a declared landing label in a finite deterministic operation/no-operation comparison. Exact certification requires that equal profiles always have equal labels. The causal and spontaneous twins show why an identical before/after record can conceal a different counterfactual explanation.

Read T299 together with the [Fable correction][c03-ar8r-fable-correction]. The factorisation characterisation survives for any declared label. However, if the packet's six conditions are read as merely necessary conditions, its separate statement that the complete matched profile is sufficient is not established. The recorded Lean development proves a repaired, scoped interpretation and a counterexample to that stronger reading; it is not an end-to-end verification of the historical packet.

T300 uses even- and odd-parity laws on at least three binary variables. Every proper-subset marginal can stay unchanged while the full parity target changes. A second construction changes the pairwise profile while keeping the target true. Thus movement of that lower-order profile is neither necessary nor sufficient for the specified target restoration. The conclusion concerns those exact distributions and targets; a learned representation or a human restoration measure needs its own analysis.

The separate [historical synthetic pilot][c03-ar8r-pilot] reports no causal landing and no tested T300 support in eight seeds. It used overlapping support and independently sampled, non-case-paired runs, so it retires the exact tested configuration at that evidential class. It neither proves nor refutes the mathematical constructions and is not one of the public benchmark packets below.

### AR8R source premises, recipient warrant and restoration

The PMR-001 notes make affirmative conditional arguments as well as identifying missing premises. **[Necessary existence, originator, and Creator][c03-ar8r-pmr-originator]** separates an originated-to-originator inference, an additional necessity/external-actuality bridge, and the further Will/Power/Knowledge guards for its scoped Creator conclusion. **[The impersonal-originator comparison][c03-ar8r-pmr-rival]** identifies exactly which premise packages leave that rival available. These finite first-order arguments clarify a translated-primary source boundary; the formal implications do not establish the premises' world-directed truth or a complete attribute ascent.

**[Receipt-profile insufficiency and local re-orthing][c03-ar8r-pmr-adoption]** supplies a concrete recipient contract. Receipt must be joined to applicability, authority, version compatibility, evidence, capability, permission and closed invalidators before that contract warrants adoption. A received proof or message can be identical while recipient versions differ. The resulting same-profile/different-adoption witness explains the need for a local check. The note identifies its AR2/AR3 specialisations and has no implemented-runtime claim.

**[Fiṭrah, impairment, reminder, and restoration][c03-ar8r-pmr-fitrah]** is a source finding, not a psychological experiment. It distinguishes a latent normative disposition, present recognition, an articulated proposition, inference and behavioural assent. In the consulted contemporary Atharī synthesis and secondary scholarly reconstruction, reminder and sound reasoning can rehabilitate an impaired disposition; a correct verbal answer alone does not show that rehabilitation occurred. Arabic-primary verification and an observed proxy for fiṭrah are not supplied.

The [PMR-002–006 synthesis][c03-ar8r-pmr2-report] and [dependency discussion][c03-ar8r-pmr2-reread] extend this work across qiyās eligibility, translation, contextual meaning, representation alignment, attribution, common knowledge and the distinct stages of Speech capacity, occurrence, qualification, naming and revealed content. They publicly describe eleven scoped outputs and their corrected dispositions. This retained selection supplies the reports, rather than all eleven complete proof/model packets.

**Language, identity and warrant-bearing communication programme.** Its five source discussions concern [agentic communication](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/programs/agentic-communication.md), [translation and version custody](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/programs/language-translation-and-version-custody.md), [identity and evidential independence](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/programs/tac-sac-identity-and-independence.md), [grammar and articulability](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/programs/uncreated-grammar-and-articulability.md), and [temporal restoration and provenance](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/programs/daee-temporal-restoration-and-provenance.md). They distinguish the scoped results already supplied from the source, interpretation, implementation and world-directed evidence still required by each programme. Receiving an artifact, preserving a selected meaning, establishing identity and warranting an action remain different achievements.

### AR8R correction, unification and the source ascent

Read the [Track T and source-ascent programme](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/programs/candidate-n-r5-track-t-and-source-ascent.md) and its [P597–P620 source-ascent assessment](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/programs/p597-p620-source-ascent-status.md) for the source-ascent components and their stated status.

**[Candidate E: recursive corrigibility][c03-ar8r-candidate-e]** asks where the authority for a radical correction comes from when the current standard is itself defective. Under its objective-correction and authority-adequacy premises, that same defeated token basis cannot supply all of the correction's authority. Its alternatives include a retained higher rule, a standard extending across states, a holistic normative fixed point, a primitive normative fact, or abandoning the stipulated objective reading. The partition does not choose a function theory or establish teleology.

**[Candidate G: derivational-unification parity][c03-ar8r-candidate-g]** tests whether moving independent explanatory coordinates into one bearer changes the explanation. Under a bijection preserving outputs, permitted interventions and all cross-coordinate constraints, the added bearer label leaves explanatory freedom unchanged. A useful unification must add an independently supported shared derivation, constraint or dependence. This is a concrete criticism of a proposed unification, while leaving genuinely explanatory common-ground hypotheses available. Both historical candidates remain [deferred for insufficient audit][c03-ar8r-programme].

The [three-track architecture][c03-ar8r-tracks] keeps mathematical Track M, conditional transcendental Track T and school-internal Track N visible together. [Candidate N][c03-ar8r-candidate-n] is a revisable modern source synthesis; its particular six-box representation is not uniquely required by the preserved queries. [Track T's accepted scope][c03-ar8r-track-t] retains the same-token/same-respect inadequacy result and conditional world-directed reflection, with the positive-ground and Necessary-Being bridges separately open. These are the status owners for this research lineage; later, separately scoped foundational works retain their own arguments and assessments.

The **[minimal nonintegration control][c03-ar8r-nonintegration]** makes the current comparison concrete: each track may retain its own results and interact through explicit interfaces without thereby sharing one ground or bearer. A proposed integration must produce an independently warranted, target-faithful consequence unavailable to that separated realization. Shared vocabulary, common outputs or interoperable software alone do not meet the condition. This is an undefeated control over the declared architecture, with a listed defeat condition; it is not a universal theorem excluding integration.

### AR8R modal transport and temporal restoration proposals

The [direct note lookup](RESULT-INDEX.md#ar8r-research-notes-direct-source-lookup) links each of the exact, later reissue, central and specialist notes by its existing title or label.

The [Round 11–20 index][c03-ar8r-round-index] leads to ten mathematical notes within the [exact theorem-note directory][c03-ar8r-exact-notes]. They connect coherent modal transport, local agreement, temporal control, version eligibility, certificate communication, query complexity and provenance-resilient restoration. The existing identities locate the individual statements; their common proposal status requires external review and owner adoption.

Three useful starting points are modal-lineage transport (`PMR-007-MLT-1`), where closed-walk holonomy must fix the chosen anchor for a coherent anchored section; **[co-Büchi restoration (`PMR-007-COB-1`)][c03-ar8r-cobuchi]**, which distinguishes eventual target persistence from forced entry into one invariant target core; and provenance-resilient restorative cutsets (`PMR-007-PRRC-1`), whose static fixed-path criterion uses the minimum transversal of required-root sets. The temporal result assumes finite, commonly observed game states and eligible actions. The cutset result assumes a complete declared path registry, authenticated roots and one-shot corruption with conjunctive dependencies. Changing those assumptions changes the problem.

### AR8R proper function, semantics and architecture proposals

Open the individual notes by title or label in the [direct note lookup](RESULT-INDEX.md#ar8r-research-notes-direct-source-lookup).

The [Deep A–BK index][c03-ar8r-deep-index] preserves 63 further notes alongside the ten earlier rounds. Earlier Round 11–20 and A–AP snapshots remain preserved editions of this growing series, rather than additional discoveries. The index's existing titles, typed settings and limitations provide the full directory; the following are subject routes into it.

| Research question | Starting work and contribution |
|---|---|
| What fixes a truth-linked standard? | **[Norm fixing and proper function][c03-ar8r-norm]** distinguishes regularity, function fixation, truth linkage, and warranted operation. The profile criterion identifies when a proposed function-fixing account supplies enough information for the declared truth target. |
| What would discriminate explanatory architectures? | The series develops conservative-expansion countermodels, interaction constraints, predictive surplus, likelihood-preserving representations and explicit source-to-world interpretation families. A selected pair can be distinguishable while the full proposed class remains unidentified. |
| When does evidence from testimony accumulate? | **[Source testimony and the world-selection gate][c03-ar8r-testimony]** uses a joint likelihood ratio. Multiplication over root reports needs conditional independence under each compared hypothesis; copied or translated repetitions do not supply additional independent factors. Its probabilistic model is not an identification of the broader Taymiyyan account of tawātur. |
| What is the common mathematical structure? | **[The deterministic typed-descent note][c03-ar8r-descent]** isolates exact target recovery through a representation and explains the additional dynamics, probability, group actions, convex structure or algebra needed by the native result families. A shared quotient construction alone does not establish one explanation or bearer. |

These are finite or otherwise explicitly scoped research proposals. The formal comparisons carry their declared interpretations and rival classes; source compatibility, actual-world adequacy and empirical discriminators require additional evidence.

### AR8R later semantic reissues

Open the individual reissued notes by title or label in the [direct note lookup](RESULT-INDEX.md#ar8r-research-notes-direct-source-lookup).

The [Deep BL–BV collection][c03-ar8r-bl] and [Deep BW–CJ collection][c03-ar8r-bw] preserve later research in a different reading form: semantic reissues, with the original ephemeral proof/checker bodies unavailable to the degrees stated by their owners. The [retained BL–BU report](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/post-merge-proposals/pmr007-deep-bl-bv-semantic-reissue/source-files/PMR-007_DEEP_BL_BU_OWNER_REPORT.md) has summary-level custody rather than recovered individual theorem identities. **[Deep BV's common-intervention criterion][c03-ar8r-bv]** asks whether a target is constant on the complete intervention-profile fibres of a finite declared model class. Its common interventions need compatible semantics and a target-independent implementation in every compared architecture.

The fourteen [BW–CJ notes][c03-ar8r-bw-index] extend that question to finite adaptive intervention laws, eligible anchor cover, resource constraints, source interpretation, norm orientation, distributed attribute bearers, and content/fittingness-sensitive control. For example, the anchor-cover construction makes exact identification equivalent to covering every same-profile/different-target pair with independently eligible separating information. Its minimal cost is relative to the supplied factor registry and costs.

The collection's correction is material: the original CI/CJ PASS counted unexecuted twin controls; the first replacement merely repeated tautological predicates. V3 checks the declared finite signatures and specified mutants. That supplies bounded constructed evidence, while original-byte identity, implemented architecture discrimination, external review and adoption remain distinct open questions.

### AR8R six-lane research

Open the Central and Specialist notes by title or label in the [direct note lookup](RESULT-INDEX.md#ar8r-research-notes-direct-source-lookup).

The [six-lane intake][c03-ar8r-six] brings together Central CK–CT, Specialist A's formal/computational notes, Specialist B's source/noetic notes and bounded dispositions of three external research reports. The underlying notes remain separate works. The public collection links their local indexes and [correction owners][c03-ar8r-six]; it does not adopt the proposals.

**[Switching-standard robust viability (Deep CQ)][c03-ar8r-cq]** is a particularly useful mathematical starting point. A finite family of candidate standards supplies target regions, eligible actions and deterministic transitions. One state-feedback policy must work under arbitrary switching between standards. A greatest fixed point identifies states that can remain in every target forever; its least-fixed-point attractor identifies states from which a common policy can force finite entry. **[Deep CR][c03-ar8r-cr]** then explains how a justified, nonempty restriction of the candidate standards can enlarge those regions when the remaining targets and dynamics are inherited unchanged. Its source-relative restriction and world-directed applicability are separate premises.

Specialist A's [minimum collision-cover note][c03-ar8r-spa10], [history-state construction][c03-ar8r-spa11] and [predictive quotient][c03-ar8r-spa12] connect evidence selection, finite adaptive behaviour and depth-specific memory requirements. The [Specialist B restoration note][c03-ar8r-spb4] distinguishes latent rehabilitation from memorisation, compliance, altered reporting or transient suppression of a defeater, and specifies the additional measurement and causal work a study would need.

Read these with the intake corrections: [Central replay is incomplete][c03-ar8r-central-correction]; [Specialist A R7/R9 need an off-range decoder guard][c03-ar8r-spa-correction]; [Specialist B lacks its supplied checker and has thinner formal signatures than portions of its prose][c03-ar8r-spb-correction]. Separately preserved Lean repairs have their own bounded receipts. Read [Challenger A on common intervention](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/post-merge-proposals/external-challenges/challenger-a-common-intervention/source-files/BOUNDED_CHALLENGER_A_COMMON_INTERVENTION_PACKET.md) and [Challenger B on formal ancestry and the source boundary](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/post-merge-proposals/external-challenges/challenger-b-formal-ancestry/source-files/BOUNDED_CHALLENGER_B_DEEP_BV_FORMAL_ANCESTRY_SOURCE_FIREWALL_PROPOSAL.md), with their [collection's qualifications][c03-ar8r-challenges]. These are useful challenges with explicit limitations, not successful external acceptance of the programme.

### AR8R a common mathematical layer and its guards

**[AR8R Codex corpus synthesis V14][c03-ar8r-synthesis]** gives an affirmative common result with a precise limit. For `E : X → O` and `Q : X → T`, an exact decoder on the attained image exists exactly when `Q` is constant on every `E`-fibre. Decoding on all of `O` additionally requires either surjectivity of `E` or an inhabited target type `T`. The empty-source/nonempty-observation/empty-target example exposes why the extra guard matters.

Its [complete Lean source][c03-ar8r-synthesis-lean] also shows that constancy of the pair `(Q,G)` is equivalent to constancy of each coordinate, and that adding a refinement `R` succeeds exactly when `Q` is constant on the joint fibres of `(E,R)`. Thus content can be recoverable while a separate version, authority or applicability guard is not. The synthesis uses this result across proof applicability, translation and intervention profiles while keeping the native causal, temporal, normative and source mechanisms distinct. The [recorded standalone Lean receipt][c03-ar8r-synthesis-receipt] supports these declarations; it is not a fresh replay, project-wide build, new theorem origin or scientific adoption.

### AR8R Fable specification and rival cores

The [corrected Fable R1 collection][c03-ar8r-fable] contains five substantive lines of work: the [T299 specification-defect study](../project-closure/ar8r-v11/fable-research/AR8R-FABLE-R1-T299-SPECIFICATION-DEFECTS.md), two rival core models, a separation index, countermodels and an OSM source-reading attempt. Their evidence levels differ.

**[The rival cores][c03-ar8r-fable-cores]** ask different questions. The profile core asks whether a target is uniform on an observed class; its central factorisation characterisation has a scoped Lean result. The provenance calculus asks whether a certificate retains the required roots through transport. Its calibration example illustrates how the same visible profile may remain while a deployment lacks the calibration warrant. The latter calculus, the example's tabulated verdicts, the proposed robustness laws and much of the surrounding mathematics are author-recorded `DERIVED_BUT_UNVERIFIED` material. The rival-core note also contains a local inconsistency: its claim that a constant profile certifies every system conflicts with its own criterion. A constant profile certifies only a target constant on the declared backgrounds; the reader uses that scoped criterion.

**[The separation-index note][c03-ar8r-fable-separation]** tries to connect certification feasibility, query cost and survival under damaged resources. Its own conclusion is informative: one common typed observation model does not automatically transfer the hidden-matching theorem's adaptive saving or establish a common explanatory calculus. **[The countermodel note][c03-ar8r-fable-negative]** retains the triangle's transversal/packing difference and a failed matroid exchange example. The [correction][c03-ar8r-fable-correction] withdraws the claim that these refuted the Round-20 theorem; that theorem already used the correct transversal criterion.

### AR8R representations and empirical discrimination

The [representation programme][c03-ar8r-representation] distinguishes legitimate Boolean, relational, matrix and tensor encodings from a claim that one encoding is privileged ontology. Its retrospective controller comparison does not decide learned-representation value. The prospective representation design remains blocked because six candidate representations are named but the five experimental arms are not fixed. Controller labels A–G are a different list.

The [higher-order/PRH note][c03-ar8r-prh] connects the parity counterexamples to a proposed diagnostic experiment without claiming that arbitrary learned geometry is determined by low-order marginals. The [OSM learning-trajectory crosswalk][c03-ar8r-osm] connects an already-owned external study to questions about identical observations, terminal representations and learning trajectories. The separate [Fable typed extraction][c03-ar8r-fable-osm] is retained as quarantined `SOURCE_LEVEL_UNVERIFIED` research leads. That narrower failure of source review does not erase the older, separately scoped source custody or create a current biological conclusion.

### AR8R formalisation and specification warrant

The [Ten Advances status note][c03-ar8r-ten] is a public account of formalisation and source questions, rather than another adopted mathematical paper. Its aggregate declaration inventory is static evidence. The [Connes-rigidity dispute][c03-ar8r-connes] records that a critique addressed a different construction from the public object it purported to refute, while the positive proof also has unresolved fatal gaps. The recorded overall disposition requires operator-algebra specialist review. This route helps a researcher find the exact unresolved question without reproducing excluded source texts or transferring a successful standalone repair to the entire inventory.

## Earlier companion assessment and drafting material

The **[Orthemic Modal–Metaphysical Assessment][c03-companion-assessment]** is the substantial earlier argument assessment behind the companion lane. It distinguishes a task-indexed operational signature, counterfactual evaluation, the stronger token-modality thesis, the constitution argument and theological identification. Its steelman states the dependence, totality, mentalism and revelational premises; the opposing infinitist, primitivist and abstract-ground positions remain visible. The [companion owner][c03-companion-guide] identifies its Thesis C as subsequently replaced by C′ and otherwise retains the assessment as technical background. Its dated judgement should be read at that scope.

The **[earlier Atharī paper outline][c03-companion-outline]** preserves the drafting argument, premise table and source restrictions that the later Atharī manuscript realised. It is an outline, rather than an additional completed manuscript. The **[Objections and Replies][c03-companion-objections]** is a useful adversarial reading companion: replies are conditional on named premises, several objections remain live, and the Hume self-refutation attribution is explicitly qualified as overstated. Its L/O/R equivocation discussion records why local evaluability, objective conditions and created representation must be distinguished. Source and current manuscript owners retain their separate roles.

## Research designs for machinery, records and vocabulary

The public empirical programme separates three questions that a single success score could conflate. The [canonical experiment owner][c03-experiment-status] currently records no run for any public packet.

| Work | Question, design and standing |
|---|---|
| **[FCSP-2: false closure and selective prediction][c03-fcsp]** | Does explicit alternative maintenance, scoped evidence, routing and per-burden closure improve a matched ordinary baseline? The protocol pairs 50 items across arms at matched information and budget; repeat runs are technical replicates. The public protocol and software are ready-to-run at their owner, with execution and external registration outstanding. |
| **[ER-2: episode-reification value][c03-er]** | Does an explicit episode/verdict record improve defect discovery, remedy selection, completion judgement and traceability? Both arms receive the same fact atoms in 20 paired cases; the treatment changes organisation and links. It shares the ready-but-unrun standing. Historical ER-1 and FCSP-1 remain preserved with their corrected, superseded status. |
| **[Terminology Pilot 0 v2][c03-term-pilot]**, followed by [Pilot 1][c03-term-pilot1] and a [confirmatory template][c03-term-confirm] | The filler, ordinary-language, coined-language and sham-language arms separate teaching, vocabulary and label-specificity effects. Pilot 0 is feasibility-only and awaits blind human matching review. Pilot 1 and confirmatory remain drafts; only the adequately powered confirmatory stage can adopt or retire a term. |

Mock executions and synthetic power inputs qualify instruments; they supply no observed treatment effect. The [completed AR8R synthetic pilot][c03-ar8r-pilot] belongs to its separate historical evidence class.

## Worked examples with different failure causes

**[Sensory aliasing][c03-example-aliasing]** is an analytic case about an honest observation that leaves consequentially different states indistinguishable. It distinguishes the concrete encounter, the observation, a plural latent-candidate set, a materially bound configuration and the inference executed under it. The correct output can remain partial until the task-relevant distinction is resolved. Its latent-state labels do not become ground truth merely by appearing in a fitted model.

**[The stale steer][c03-example-stale]** separates an authentic recovered instruction from its current force. A system can faithfully execute a superseded instruction and therefore need a repair to its governing binding or currentness check. The other [worked fixtures][c03-example-directory] illustrate related result/pathway, shared-source, episode-composition and Somnus cases. They are examples and validator inputs with their own existing contracts, rather than completed empirical studies.

## Supporting reference, not another research article

**Orthemology notation gallery.** [Markdown reference][gallery] · [Governed PDF][render-notation-gallery].

The publication profile classifies this as a diagnostic reference. It helps inspect notation/rendering; it is not another independent scientific paper merely because it has a PDF.

## Reading versions, editing sources and history

The [publication profile][profile] owns the six governed outputs and their source components. Generated files under `publication/latex/` are build inputs derived from the substantive source owners, not places to revise the prose independently. Other preserved PDFs, such as the two lineage editions above, retain their distinct provenance rather than being silently absorbed into this pipeline.

Use the [verification guide](VERIFICATION-GUIDE.md#reading-and-editing-a-publication) for the relevant checks and citation route. The [collection directory](RESULT-INDEX.md#collection-owners) leads to the wider research owners and their distinct substantive reading routes. The catalogue's [subject directory](#how-to-use-this-catalogue) brings those works together without treating every supporting record as a separate paper.

## Original v5 construction and convergence families

The original consolidation is a research lineage and executable projection, not 119 papers. Its [origin-scoped index](../provenance/v5-consolidation/THEOREM_INDEX.md) supplies the earlier result IDs and hypotheses; the following works expose the arguments and code behind those records. Historical missing-compiler language must be read with the later [mapped-source amendment](../provenance/v5-consolidation/FORMAL_SOURCE_AMENDMENT.json), not silently applied to every later component.

### Internal polymorphism and observational boundaries

*Internal polymorphism, observational boundaries, and reflective probability*, version 3, constructs a universe of reduction-invariant sets of raw programs. A universally quantified code is itself in that universe and may instantiate its own quantifier, while its realiser remains one common program. That positive construction does not supply the full same-universe abstraction/application interface forbidden by Girard. The manuscript also states the positive-singleton atomicity criterion for unrestricted almost-sure conjunction and its observation-map and compactness qualifications. Continuous randomness hidden within a single observable class is not prohibited.

[Read version 3](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/source/history/Orthemology_v3_Critical_Handoff/inputs/v3/MATHEMATICAL_PROOF.md#1-common-definitions-and-the-retained-construction) · [Atomicity and observation](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/source/history/Orthemology_v3_Critical_Handoff/inputs/v3/MATHEMATICAL_PROOF.md#2-the-sharp-probabilistic-boundary-almost-sure-universal-typing) · [Preserved version 2](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/source/history/Orthemology_v3_Critical_Handoff/inputs/v3/inherited_v2/MATHEMATICAL_PROOF.md). These are predecessor/version relationships in one manuscript lineage, not independent discoveries. The ordinary arguments, original formal candidates and later mapped formal receipts retain different scopes.

### The v4 proof continuation

The [v4 continuation](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/source/PROOFS_V4.md) extends that line with quantitative observation-refinement, finite invariant checks, revision-sensitive execution, finite logical relations, source-certificate export and a productive irrational-threshold sampler. Its positive result is useful execution in a declared language, with a known forbidden full-section interface kept separate; it is not a new foundational calculus merely because those properties are conjoined.

Its [sampling argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/source/PROOFS_V4.md#v4-s1--productive-threshold-source-mass-and-cost) distinguishes almost-sure completion from completion on every stream, and expected random bits from CPU work. The source's later mathematical extensions are catalogued separately below. Formal component checking does not automatically formalise the measure theory or the complete Python runtime.

### Observation refinement and the infinite residual

**Existing family `V5::CV-R`.** [Exact observation-refinement accounting](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/mathematics/REFINEMENT_CALCULUS.md) defines defect as the mass outside positive singleton atoms. For coherent measurable refinements, finite changes telescope into exact nonnegative debt. The [infinite-chain theorem](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/mathematics/REFINEMENT_CALCULUS.md#3-infinite-chain-a-necessary-residual) adds a residual for paths whose finite observation cells all have positive mass but whose limiting cell has zero mass. Every finite fair-bit prefix has zero defect; the complete fair stream has defect one.

The full measurable event repertoire and singleton measurability are load-bearing. Zero defect is not equal information or zero entropy. Its exact budget-transport rule concerns the truth of a semantic bound; the authority of an old issued certificate is a different question. This source supplies ordinary mathematics, not a fresh measure-theoretic kernel proof.

### Defect computability and its finite-state exception

**Existing family `V5::CV-C`.** [Exact computability of the point-concentration defect](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/mathematics/DEFECT_COMPUTABILITY.md) first fixes a total primitive-recursive synchronous observer presentation, then gives the rational-threshold hierarchy. Zero defect is `Π⁰₃`-complete and positive defect `Σ⁰₃`-complete in that presentation; the earlier non-halting subfamily is not the whole classification. Invalid syntax has a total declared meaning, so the result is not hiding a productivity promise.

The constructive [finite-state exception](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/mathematics/DEFECT_COMPUTABILITY.md#5-finite-state-observers-exact-decidability-not-merely-an-upper-bound) computes atomic mass as the probability of eventually entering an input-insensitive state relation. Finite Markov reachability and rational linear algebra then decide the thresholds. This is an ordinary proof with explicit prior-work/priority limits, not an assertion that all productive observers can be certified by a finite checker.

### Revision-sensitive licences and semantic transport

**Existing family `V5::CV-O`.** [A small revision-sensitive operational calculus](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/mathematics/ABSTRACT_OWOU.md) supplies a nonempty typed plan system with useful primitives, composition and checked source application. Under complete captured dependencies, persistent issuance records and strictly increasing versions, its [licence-transport theorem](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/mathematics/ABSTRACT_OWOU.md#4-exact-licence-transport-theorem) says an old licence survives exactly when no captured dependency is touched. Restoring a permission's value does not restore its earlier version.

A mathematically surviving probability bound can therefore need a newly issued operational licence. The source includes absent-rule and vocabulary dependencies, resource refusal, locking and issuer assumptions; it does not prove distributed revocation or the full Python/parser refinement. Its generic-wrapper comparison explains why the bare conjunction with internal polymorphism is an integration rather than established foundational novelty.

### The native reference and formal audit package

The [repository-native package](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/README.md) is one research-software work: inherited reference code, finite source and operational checks, a staged formal project, tests and a new integration adapter. Its [formal source amendment](../provenance/v5-consolidation/FORMAL_SOURCE_AMENDMENT.json) separately records 145 inherited targets and a 146-target shadow audit including the upstream cross-check. Those recorded mapped-source outcomes must neither be erased by an older README's missing-build account nor expanded into whole-runtime refinement or formal proofs of CV-R/C/CV-O.

Use the [native entry point](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/verify_repository.py) and [formal staging instructions](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/formal_project/README.md) under their own toolchain and exit-code contract. Static `--check`, runtime-only success, actual kernel checks and a source-world application remain different observations. Original off-repository archives are not invented public downloads.

### The bounded adversarial comparison

[The convergence comparison](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/comparison/ADVERSARIAL_COMPARISON.md) and its [earlier v4 comparison](../../experiments/orthemology-v5/source/PRIOR_ART_COMPARISON.md) identify what is elementary or inherited, what the exact effective presentation adds, and what historical/specialist checking remains. This is a review/comparison work, not another proof of every result it discusses or a new literature survey performed by the reader refactor. Its references do not recursively expand this repository's corpus.

## Generated controllers, observation laws and executable delivery

These works follow one source-bound progression: observed-history policy construction, exact law transport, quantitative logical error cost, and executable source delivery. They have different domains and success contracts. The [result owner](../provenance/v5-research-continuations/RESULT_STATUS.json) and [Sixth-final successor map](../provenance/v5-research-continuations/SIXTH_FINAL_OVERLAY.json) preserve the later resolutions; earlier "pending" phrases are not current vetoes over those exact successors.

### Support changes and a deterministic repair policy

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/b092780b29dd9fa00ac276142b090a5b8b88da79da9c278268264669e08f3d8c.md). For finite observed states, a finite known family of static hidden rational models and the stated support-dependent lawful menus, the ordinary theorem characterises common almost-sure finite bad visits by a recursive winning region. That same region admits a policy with finite expected bad visits. Proper-support exits and self-verification use the **full** transition rows; conditioning away an exit can erase the information needed for repair.

[The deterministic round-robin strengthening](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/9438fdcbea8747c7e5599483096eca9541cfdf673d6a96866c019c5ced06519a.md) constructs one history policy without fresh random action selection. Rotors retain within-stage progress; resetting at each action can starve the informative action. Finite augmented-state reasoning supplies a computable, potentially very large bound; source counters can grow without limit. The theorem is neither a stationary finite-memory implementation nor a uniform bound as positive probabilities tend to zero. [The allowance clarification](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/34c1f25cd1144b386ff4c70a2fbfacc7da2a0fc87f5cd78b430badf260ab49f6.md) makes its accuracy budget effective without a noncomputable input real. This ordinary source family must not borrow the later global parity theorem's formal status.

### A common generated policy for hidden parity

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/c0d09be76eaa0275ece132844892c15e23b99cde304d11c3fdd0d27b3e0f426e.md). The formal research package identifies its computed winning region with existence of a common lawful policy for a finite observed-state rational family and model-dependent priorities. Its deterministic Unit-seed generated history policy is chosen before the true model. It reconstructs live support from receipts, cycles candidates and source-state action visits, and applies acquired-row tests. It does not assume that adaptive random sample counts are iid or that a successful policy is supplied.

The [SCC successor](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/3320baf0b6442d9cf5ca383c65a1552d48441a39f711a38922800596c1f2dcd6.md) links the efficient region implementation to that same global semantic endpoint. The [explicit winning record](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/5c160ab18ede3157e49ccd2b30f89731535dc51a1245d714665cf78f92ea43b6.md) makes the record's policy and seed law the literal generated ones rather than an outer existential choice. These are genuine component advances, not different independent papers or automatic extraction of every internal classical choice.

Minimum-even parity can allow infinitely many odd-priority visits. Finite expected **all** bad visits requires the separate coBüchi/cost contract below. A compiled formal witness is also distinct from an executable controller-source implementation. The original full/reused compilation receipts govern this package; no new 89-module replay is claimed here.

### Restarting from the actual observed prefix

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/7f31b4561bdcb9c6a09bdac5448229a57a06eff04354f1d3102d41d6d74fada8.md). This formal continuation package constructs the law obtained by restricting the original experiment to a positive-probability complete observed prefix, normalising and shifting. It then proves equality with the correctly restarted policy/history law, including posterior private-seed effects and the acquired state. The conditional law is not defined to be the desired answer.

Full cylinder identities support arbitrary infinite observed events, not just one next-step marginal. Shared posterior seeds across live models require the proved likelihood cancellation; null histories receive no invented positive-probability guarantee. A killed-before-exit subprobability transport can retain a matching recurrent component without asserting that all future trajectories stay there. The result deliberately avoids an unproved statement about every unused raw oracle after an adaptive stopping time.

[The actual conditional-law declaration](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/1547d34d2a53302e070e7a02131f242f9cb1d80f6aa0f96fc478844f630703a3.lean) and [the derived common-seed posterior](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/adc48e40e89c35782be3f30eafb0a62bb6100de48bf853a84e081631da8092e0.lean) expose the controlling inputs directly. This is an enabling source-law component, not by itself the global parity theorem, finite total cost, or executable extraction. Those claims require their actual further interfaces. The retained report specifies its original module/checking closure; this entry does not rerun it.

### Why mixed hidden starts can make control exponentially hard

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/174e674f40b87fcfbed689a9bdd13f258604b7586954be66caf7fd6a780eb066.md). The ordinary complexity argument proves EXPTIME-completeness for the explicitly represented **posterior-deterministic joint-kernel** problem with a mixed initial hidden belief; the lower bound already uses observable absorbing reachability. Posterior-deterministic means an observed receipt and old hidden state determine the next hidden state, not that the controller knows which hidden state it occupies.

A polynomial construction hides a checked tape cell of a clock-bounded alternating machine. False local claims expose a positive-probability absorbing failure; a common policy cannot safely learn the checker by cheating. Clock-bounded halting is essential: an indefinitely repeated universal option is not equivalent to forced finite acceptance. The upper construction tracks a descriptor space exponential in the number of initially possible hidden states.

A known single initial hidden state can instead be tracked under this transition property; silently adding a hidden initialisation row may violate the same property. The [recorded proof review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/a846bea274160636dc05518bc1fe8188a4625e379c449dbd47867c0d3f388748.md) retains those distinctions, randomized-policy quantifiers and the finite observable-state lift. This is ordinary mathematical analysis of a specified representation, not a generic claim that every related controller interface is EXPTIME-complete or a new Lean complexity theorem.

### The actual generated controller has finite and exponential error budgets

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/2600ec078aeacd393c1d9fa249ef513879c50445a428a301402372c1970fa80a.md). Under the finite observed-state family, initial winning membership, positive separating tolerance and the coBüchi encoding (bad priority 1, good priority 2), the original proof bounds the actual generated policy's total bad-action count. It includes the first action and excludes any fictitious default history label. A measurable last-error cutoff is **not a stopping time**: the argument uses genuine stopped histories and an unconditional shell transfer rather than conditioning future data on eventual accuracy.

The [ordinary concentration addendum](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/20b7b22149860c3faaa10850224f6e174b587cb39e8b634dc12267da94c04b78.md) exploits non-resetting row counts for a linear global charge budget and an exponential tail. The [formal finite-cost successor](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/f801e82642d5a0234ffe5ac7d12c59b934023799d61db382bbf822512768ad16.md) then proves actual all-moment/finite-mean endpoints; the [pointwise exponential composition](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/1f8ec4ba8c4961fe0d3a5719db29030c5eaff3aaf4c07ea9f41361a608997489.md) closes the stronger formal exponential endpoint. Finally, the [fixed-family common-range corollary](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/fd6a5f2e156d0ae95e9e1e0a5f44e3266cbe77a9f6d4dabbfc51f9d1979b2e6b.md) provides one positive open range for every model in a fixed finite admitted family. Its [separate retained review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/a96b3fc7e9c37ccb4ef28fea5d652e2b4229a704b97b8d7531c5595a08cf1ad9.md) distinguishes one freshly checked module over 152 qualified objects from a new 153-source cold build.

[The actual all-moment declaration](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/8b4014de8d7ae893631060dbe780cce434bccbce16524e6303e2c692f3fce46b.lean) and [its stopped-cost review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/db9a2fff3d920e05b7d65afad780241979b588ffa391d1fca47201cf6d0fbf87.md) preserve the terminal transition, global charge budget and prefix-free random-start union. [The intermediate scope update](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/69d5f496653087c349abae02d2060d1352ee751eb89a6db476800d7735cfd95d.md) records what that earlier union-bound route did not yet establish; it does not negate the later exponential endpoint above.

These are successive results about the **same specified generated witness**, not all almost-sure winners. The finite common bound may depend on the chosen exponential parameter; there is no rate uniform over arbitrary input families, no cost bound for physical waiting/CPU/memory, and no bound on the time of the last error. Explicit almost-sure cost finiteness prevents extended-real conversion from hiding infinite cost.

### Classical finite-memory tools and the hidden-badness distinction

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/09b4a02871f01bb4a676c5a9ebb751344074d349107336b7f86ff1ee719183f1.md). This bounded comparison credits existing finite-memory results for common observable objectives: finite-chain reasoning already yields an exponential total-error moment for an almost-sure coBüchi winner in that subclass. It therefore locates the source-specific contribution in the literal generated controller, hidden model-dependent badness and its exact observation/cost proof chain—not generic invention of all-moment existence.

Its two-model Bernoulli example separates autonomous finite-state controllers from a history-dependent empirical policy. Both observation values have positive probability under both models, so the autonomous finite-state chains share recurrent supports; one cannot make the hidden model's wrong action disappear under both. The history-dependent policy has an exponential error-count tail. This does not assert almost-sure failure in one fixed model for every controller, nor exclude a small internal state using unrestricted **external time**.

The paper's earlier actual-controller exponential frontier is resolved by the later cost compositions above. Its primary-source comparison remains attributed to this dated review; the present refactor did not repeat its external literature search or assert historical novelty.

### From supplied machines and histories to literal P02 execution

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/874f807af570c4e35879f3eef69c96ee21f3cda2cdb243a201ff95932cc91c9d.md). The generic finite-Mealy compiler produces a literal P02 source program and proves its decoded and scheduled-stack outputs equal the supplied table on **every input tape**, with exact complete-law and defect consequences. Its later natural-state and encoded-history compilers remove a finite-memory restriction when actual local source-program certificates are supplied. Neither a bare computability assertion nor an assumed whole-stream equality replaces those obligations.

The rational feedback wrapper uses one common observation-only controller and a model-indexed **generative environment**. One fixed code/decoder/fair-input law cannot equal two different environment laws without an appropriate input interface. Rejection waits are not controller observations, pending frames are not receipts, and ticks are not CPU steps. In the stated denominator-three framed fixture the ordinary argument gives expected acquisition cost `32/3` per logical receipt, not bounded worst-case latency or practical runtime.

The source's later complete-phase and probability compositions are linked below rather than erased by older extraction/law gaps. All-tape physical output can coexist with a null tape that never completes a logical receipt. Exact decoded parity does not imply equality of the richer physical stream's nonatomic defect.

### Exact rational receipts do not preserve every physical-stream property

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/5a6678d026753f2dbf1716aab83a59290fca8bc418c8b9b787654e368d38c0dc.md). The formal probability package derives literal fair-bit block probabilities, rational rejection-sampler receipts, their joint rejection-clock factors, almost-sure finite-prefix completion and a measurable complete receipt decoder. It binds those laws to actual P02 runtime output for the stated supplied history-policy source and rational fixture. It does not assume fresh randomness at an adaptive stopping time or infer infinite-law equality from finite native traces.

Two explicit source counterexamples distinguish this positive exact receipt-law result from stronger claims. An acknowledgement stream can be atomless while its decoded receipts are constant. Even the two-state **held output without extra acknowledgement bits** can be atomless although run-change decoding yields a deterministic alternating path. Positive finite stuttering preserves the stated parity event, not necessarily atomic mass or defect. Countable decoder fibres give a sufficient exact-preservation criterion, not a necessary universal condition.

The null forever-rejection tape remains a non-completion, not a timeout-produced receipt. Earlier source-to-canonical-coordinate work is completed by the final phase-law composition below. Acquisition expectations remain separately ordinary; this packet is not a new CPU/wall-clock or universal host-refinement theorem.

### A complete Boolean phase source with a canonical-law bridge

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/0cabc5d1332aadecc2d5a515f1c92f59b14b4eadbf3843ea4dd7a8b24fba8950.md). The executable arity-one P02 policy program reproduces the retained phase controller on acquired histories for **two models, two observed states and two actions**, with unbounded histories, phases, visits and counts. It preserves support-first reset, protected simultaneous statistics, actual target choices and the empirical gate on the updated history. Literal finite selector tables and their exact 36-cell binding certificate are required; those values are not obtained by silently evaluating opaque classical choices.

The [final canonical-law successor](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/391c42755a77de41fe022878d058f8c7423ff81b0e2a06b42bec96214836006a.md) composes the phase source, actual indexed runtime, rational sampler and common output-only decoder into the retained full canonical history law and its decoded parity success. It derives that law instead of assuming complete-history agreement. The law/parity wrapper additionally requires the declared kernel to match its fixed 1/3-versus-2/3 simulated environment and the stated initial state.

The computed rational tolerance supplies valid separation, but changing tolerance can change finite-history decisions; it is not exact extraction of an arbitrarily previously chosen real tolerance. Automatic selector extraction, a concrete universal selector-certificate instance, arbitrary dimensions or rational environments, practical resource bounds and full physical-defect preservation remain outside the theorem. The [combined source-only replay guide](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/ed3d671599c4d246dafb7f66bcdc1222d5e43e8667b9f527776e2181d6ff8081.md) identifies the final module and the qualified-versus-cold checking distinction.

## Original productive sourcehood, Knowledge and action selection

### From actual correction to original sourcehood

[From actual evaluable activity to original existential actuality](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/95164207fe5aa8b4dd81fcf5c85891e36c64495202d5e52bc98dd6ff050d7bc4.md) begins with a real exercise of correction, not an attempt to turn a necessary logical condition into a concrete object. Its positive argument follows received efficacy: another genuine recipient can contribute causally while still requiring an account of the actuality it receives. A complete original account must supply that actuality, rather than merely specify conditional transfers. This is a productive-completion argument, not a rule excluding every infinite sequence or denying dependent agency.

The useful formal factorisation is **complete ancestry coverage → independence**, given strict transitive/irreflexive productive ancestry. A covering source need not sit in a globally well-founded graph. The [exact infinite controls and modal composition](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/8ba10c48339b650a5d7a65408a5d2157df70fceea1b3b93ed83d8cdb530bc0cd.md) distinguish that claim from existence of a covering bearer, local coverage from one common source, and actual roothood from necessary existence of this same bearer. The latter uses the stated contingent-reception and essential-reception premises. The argument defends those metaphysical warrants; a labelled graph alone neither proves nor refutes their application. This Sixth-continuation assessment does not overwrite later, separately scoped foundational appraisals.

### Original cognitive adequacy

[Original sourcehood and intrinsic Knowledge](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/b96009b730afa83484b1ed804d470e283874958f5ec71b4f3c09bdff5d0999a2.md) treats genuine truth apprehension—not merely a successful output—as an actuality to explain. Its **respect-specific original-giver priority** argues that an independently complete original giver of an eligible intrinsic excellence cannot be wholly inferior in that very excellence. Productive range is not a substitute for cognitive achievement, and original giving is not ordinary partial causation with independent materials. The source distinguishes positive apprehension from creaturely acquisition, fatigue and error.

This is a defended positive source argument with a specific warrant, not the definition of efficient sufficiency. It keeps actual sourcehood, de re necessity, history-inclusive complete coverage and cognitive priority as distinct steps. The [retained scoped review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/8b7f7fd900d28e4ae19b0820e475d1c76d7976ef3f77b35305296766dd1edc57.md) credits that rationale without treating the weaker productive-relation package as already containing it. The [modal scope study](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/021db3edf2e9f9ce30204ca58c515f4ee89a6f1b7eb9ab3f957785d55bd2707b.md) asks what further premise makes Knowledge standing rather than contingent on a cognitive recipient. Formal interpretability of a weaker rival is not a certificate of its metaphysical possibility.

### Complete creative determination and Knowledge of effects

The [constructive self-Knowledge appendix](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/1a50c35624a22aa0ccb2c955bc20950cfc27e85611b58d6bd490a8cc7ad4d643.md) supplies a positive next step: Knowledge of the actual complete creative determination, Knowledge of its efficacy for an effect, and the stated consequence-completeness entail Knowledge of that effect. Generic standing capacity alone need not distinguish different actual exercises. This is not an initial proof of source cognition, nor does knowledge of one effect establish unlimited coverage.

The separately retained [noninferential-Knowledge clarification](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/bc92ae5f37ea73f1196696314646006b5fdfb693c0293c1c92c6d16ec8845308.md) is essential to the reading: **the entailment is our argument, not a process in which the source learns, infers or acquires a previously missing cognition**. The [formal consequence and finite information contrast](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/c6378e90f5347069abaaa3e7d165a6029d6643cd1b551bdd31678906adea9cb1.lean) are static. They distinguish contingent acts/effects from standing Knowledge; information determination is not itself consciousness, and a conditional efficacy relation does not make its antecedent necessary.

### Actual giving and standing Knowledge

[Exact sourcehood with actual Knowledge and a standing Knowledge boundary](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/021db3edf2e9f9ce30204ca58c515f4ee89a6f1b7eb9ab3f957785d55bd2707b.md) preserves the imported source schemas and adds actual source cognition plus a worldwise cognitive-giver rule. Its recipient-free alternative retains productive activity, so a recipient-conditioned rule does not alone force standing Knowledge. Possible cognitive giving is not present cognition.

The same source gives constructive sufficient routes: actual giving with priority gives actual Knowledge; everywhere cognitive giving gives worldwise Knowledge; necessary source existence with a complete intrinsic determinant yields standing cognition. The [formal model and positive implications](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/85e78a9e6d8994b215af19da69614dd2b84dc996b8a4c038b45d3b46f45a0ec2.lean) do not establish their metaphysical premises, infer consciousness from a table, or freeze every particular act. Its recorded formal evidence belongs to that packet, not a new replay by this refactor.

### Exact integer confidence budgets

The [arithmetic certificate contract](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/3c85cd1a8651edae64327ef4f0dd284d03055c82dd7d3cf36fcc66a49d9540d9.md) and [exact Python evaluator](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/reference/integer-v3/src/exact_budget.py) turn a **supplied controller-tail inequality** into integer/Fraction witnesses for a conservative bad-count threshold. Dyadic upper-log witnesses and upward rounding avoid floating-point logarithms and underflow. The [formal coefficient-envelope successor](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/4134b9222e5f5697ff4f9df05873ee0c985819fc10fbab843426f00422e98d31.lean) composes the original exponential coefficient and logarithmic progress rate with those witnesses.

The result remains `CONDITIONAL_PARAMETER_BOUND_ONLY`: the evaluator does not establish a winning controller, the transition law, a valid empirical separation margin or the model's applicability. An explicit work guard can refuse an expensive exact power; that is not a mathematical counterexample. The [candidate-three record correction](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/df3c0d9f67602b25449bde809132bf3d18e93cefdcded8336d9e0727e1edfee4.md) rejects numerically equal floats and Boolean/inexact field substitutions rather than accepting equality as canonical representation. This is one executable research work with formal/support components, not a new paper for each test or certificate.

### Truthful disclosure that selects a safe action

[Action-sufficient disclosure after exact diagnosis](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/9040abfeb65df6bf2cfc6ae6cf27384cfd2d21b8220e27f88400eedb1f26f222.md) asks what truthful integrity information lets a recipient act safely when correct diagnosis alone does not. In its static destructive-root model, the labelled report identifies `k` corrupt dissenters and leaves residual budget `b = f − k`. A guaranteed intact first choice exists **exactly when the additional message alphabet has at least `b + 1` values**. The positive construction names one intact root from a pre-agreed menu of that size; private or integrity-independent shared randomness cannot reduce the zero-error threshold. The zero-budget case needs no extra bit.

The source holds fixed root identity, target, governing rule, fault set, timing and action effects. It assumes truthful delivery and sufficient sender integrity knowledge, not merely an authentic signature or omniscience inferred from successful signalling. Erasing the dissenting identities while retaining their count can invalidate the smaller budget. The [formal component](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/ec03d96ddb213db0f65bf3c3780f611419a9433e4079a9d0f2553b79c9712fa3.lean) is discoverable without labelling the whole ordinary randomised argument kernel-verified. This is an action-selection contract, not a lower bound on scripture or proof that one ultimate source actually communicates.

## Necessity, objective correction and attribute ascent

These Sixth-final works connect actual warranted correction to original adequacy and positive source-attribute arguments. Their steps remain distinguishable: local objective correction, original production, a complete common source, existential necessity and authoritative disclosure are not interchangeable conclusions. Their retained R5 and source-appraisal records are dated arguments, not fresh decisions overruling later T20 research.

### Original actuality and existential necessity

[Read the argument](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/cfc0393b381fcd5fea90cce22c271420f5c6c9fbd74c30ec91b1c2563a876ee3.md) · [Strongest affirmative chain](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/cfc0393b381fcd5fea90cce22c271420f5c6c9fbd74c30ec91b1c2563a876ee3.md#7-the-strongest-positive-necessity-argument-with-its-burden-visible).

The argument begins with an actual received being or operation, not with a universal unexplained slogan about causation. It develops **faqr and ghinā** as recognition of received actuality and its provision, then asks what makes the original basis sufficient in itself. Mere absence of an observed supplier, essential unreceivedness wherever a thing exists, and unconditional existential sufficiency are different claims. A power cannot efficiently manufacture the whole bearer whose actuality exercising that power already presupposes; nevertheless, that observation is not by itself a refutation of every brute-contingent or noncausal account.

Its positive eight-step case connects real received actuality, productive completion, a particular original bearer, the insufficiency of conditional nature or self-production as a whole-existence explanation, and the exhaustiveness of intrinsic existence or further receipt. The additional explanatory and modal premises are defended explicitly, not obtained simply by renaming actual independence “necessity”. Receiving agents can have real active powers; the issue with a wholly received field is adequate production, not merely the number of members. The paper also distinguishes a necessarily existing whole from this very bearer necessarily existing. **Necessity of the source's existence does not make every particular act necessary.** This is a philosophical argument with visible premise burdens, not a new kernel-certified world-identification or a verdict about all later foundational work.

### Warranted correction and complete actual origination

[Main world-bridge argument](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/9509540e197dabd06c92e20870e65c4fc185d3a27b987531c1adce152dea8f08.md) · [Intrinsic exercises and productive support](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/e2ba2ea03f8b2a35d01b26fb6840febf4c8ea6f3bc6affa53f2068a03f53fa32.md) · [External termination and origin closure](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/c81850bf7c859141ef48fd31c428692c1d799dbc3a2395736eeddff445e69774.md) · [Original premise/rival map](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/aed490bf4860b4826da380c7f22dcef46972155d14fc2bd95b992492aa48261c.md).

The main paper requires a jointly adequate explanation of a true judgement, a subject's warrant and the actual process connecting that subject to its target. Merely placing those three facts together does not explain why the judgement is knowledge. Its affirmative source argument gives **target-sensitive cognitive and productive adequacy** real explanatory work, while separating the further claims of full scope, originalness, necessity and cognitive perfection. A world without the subject is not automatically a nearby false-belief case; a causally realised counterfeit and a local reliable power pose different questions. The source states the extra risk, cross-kind and field-completion premises needed by its stronger anti-sceptical route.

The two named constituents sharpen the unity argument. **Bearer support, externally received productive efficacy and ultimate provision are not one relation.** An intrinsic act depending on its own bearer need not be externally created. The positive activity-inclusive account asks whether one original source supplies the whole actual productive field, including any second putatively independent agent's exercised efficacy. The external-termination refinement makes this argument without assuming a separately existing “mode of reception”: actual contribution to an effect's origin already raises the completion question. Supplying all completed endpoints is weaker than supplying their entire actual origination, and the bridge must be warranted. These distinctions protect contingent intrinsic acts and genuine creaturely agency while making the complete-source argument more exact. The three texts are a connected work family, not three copies of a proof or an automatic conversion of engineering fault domains into independent Creators.

### Objective norm-fixing and warranted common repair

[Read the positive account](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/11627e65cd18f0f52f7f9f2e5d5f051076ea72f4d3560067e7dcc3d7332885d7.md) · [Constructive semantic repair](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/11627e65cd18f0f52f7f9f2e5d5f051076ea72f4d3560067e7dcc3d7332885d7.md#6-a-semantic-common-repair-criterion) · [Objections and affirmative replies](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/8e5446bdb8a28491f0fbc877102525a4eabde4a6a12e5c19403310c7575ba332.md) · [What would actually discriminate?](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/43839272a7bb1ba4a70a96d1e28acba5b1a3c73b9dd5409e5f42dc11c894e9f1.md).

The paper begins with a target, a specified respect and an obtaining relation. Once transcription of a source inscription is the task, whether it says 17 or 71 is not fixed by an editor's preference. It distinguishes **truth, constitutive correctness, instrumental success, epistemic warrant and binding obligation**; neither a reward nor a supplied rule settles all five. Fixing a task by convention does not make its target's properties conventional, while design, history, organisation and present use supply different further claims about proper function.

Its constructive common-repair result uses semantic retractions on a lawful state space: each is idempotent, its fixed points meet the relevant target, and the repairs commute. Their finite composition then satisfies the targets together while preserving that space. The text calls this elementary conditional mathematics rather than claiming novelty for the algebra; the hard application requires justified targets, a real admissible implementation and, under uncertainty, certificates valid for every retained case. Commutation is sufficient, not necessary. A separately stated fail-silent coverage argument uses at least one effective repair among redundant roots; it does not silently pretend an inert root satisfies the stronger semantic fixed-point condition.

The examples distinguish repairing a derived transcription from changing the preserved source, real unit conversion from flattering labels, and repairing an invalid method from simply reversing its accidentally correct answer. Its positive intellectual-source account concerns truth-linked understanding, genuine ends and proper function, not administrative status. The additional warrant for binding authority and perfect practical orientation is kept visible; it need not be settled before any local objective correction is possible.

### Original sourcehood, agency, Wisdom and disclosure

[Read the attribute argument](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/77a990a1968d9219806652ca7b9808d2680e96918629a50018ed04ef30094688.md) · [Positive agency account](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/77a990a1968d9219806652ca7b9808d2680e96918629a50018ed04ef30094688.md#42-the-positive-intentional-account) · [Dependency and discrimination map](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/0797149b6869d04aaeb335a37405d77a0eac19645f406fe53bf90fa396c84254.md).

Actual origination first warrants **Power over the effects actually produced**. The argument for unrestricted eligible power needs its own possibility and completeness premises. Likewise, co-located cognition and efficacy do not yet establish that apprehension of reasons guides action. The positive ascent appeals to original integrated, reason-responsive agency: the source of a correction made because its reason is understood must adequately account for that distinctive perfection, rather than replacing it with a success score. Standing capacity, determinate Will, good reasons and complete effective exercise remain distinct; a sufficient particular act entailing its effect does not entail that act in every possible circumstance.

Wisdom includes pursuing genuinely fitting ends, not simply knowing which ends are good or optimising a stipulated quantity. The source's primary-text discussion supplies an affirmative perfection and no-defect rationale, while separating objective value, practical responsiveness and their warranted application to the original source. Its disclosure criterion is also positive: known content, a recipient-directed intention and jointly exercisable appropriate expressive power can establish a capability to disclose. **Actual revelation additionally needs an actual address, authenticated source, intended content, truthfulness and recipient warrant.** This is not the claim that every natural sign is an intended message. The dependency map keeps those steps inspectable and treats its finite predicate controls as scope tests, not detectors of Wisdom, consciousness or revelation.

### Practical perfection and the malicious-source challenge

[Read the continuation](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/7bf4f9e1df2de0b49cbc677310d2a882dfdf698e2bd1c275f822dd8038ba67ff.md) · [Affirmative qiyās al-awlā](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/7bf4f9e1df2de0b49cbc677310d2a882dfdf698e2bd1c275f822dd8038ba67ff.md#3-the-strongest-positive-qiyās-al-awlā-argument).
