This continuation grants the challenging rival considerable strength—original actuality, Knowledge, Power and intentional activity—before asking whether intrinsically malicious ends are compatible with the source's perfection. Its positive argument first recognises practical excellence in appropriately equal circumstances, identifies the excellence apart from a creature's dependency or passion, then connects eligible excellence to original giving. Recognising the good of knowledge or a subject's genuine welfare is not replaced by defining “good” as whatever a proposed source chooses.

The paper distinguishes defeasible evaluative recognition, objective value and a metaphysical exclusion based on original priority and completeness. **Some real beneficence does not alone prove necessary, unrestricted impeccability**; the mixed-source case and extension to intrinsic practical completeness receive explicit additional treatment. Full knowledge does not mechanically cause good motivation, freedom from need is not freedom from every end, and privation does not make actual malicious agency disappear. False authoritative attestation supplies a particularly sharp role-relative defect, but truthfulness is not inferred from communication capacity alone. The argument therefore preserves affirmative grounds for Wisdom while stating the actual burdens of the strongest rival, rather than conferring a new scientific verdict by catalogue placement.

## Safe restoration and the exact cost of action disclosure

These works ask what a recipient needs to act correctly when some original roots can be corrupt. They distinguish knowing the right repair, possessing a safe way to perform it, receiving a useful message and controlling when an adversary learns the selected action. The same word “disclosure” does not make these operational games a proof of actual revelation or recipient warrant.

### Action-relevant integrity after exact diagnosis

[Read the complete argument](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/9f42b862f0c1fd75d0d8558fd9d4be8b70ed49d2b2284c64e3832b4dc42bce63.md) · [Destructive-root separation](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/9f42b862f0c1fd75d0d8558fd9d4be8b70ed49d2b2284c64e3832b4dc42bce63.md#3-destructive-root-architecture-exact-separation-theorem) · [Constructive fail-silent contrast](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/9f42b862f0c1fd75d0d8558fd9d4be8b70ed49d2b2284c64e3832b4dc42bce63.md#4-fail-silent-architecture-a-substantive-positive-contrast).

Exact identification of a defective rule need not identify a safe route for repairing it. The source constructs a whole-rule repair problem with `n = 2f + 1` authenticated roots and at most `f` static corrupt roots. A majority can disclose the correct repair while a truthful root still might be corrupt. With unanimous reports, the best compulsory first-attempt strategy has worst-case unsafe probability `f/n`; waiting forever instead fails restoration. After `k` dissenting reports, the corresponding risk is `(f-k)/(n-k)`. The latter formula uses the source's binary reporting, common-knowledge and static-root assumptions, not a universal confidence rule.

The positive result changes the available action semantics rather than pretending better diagnosis suffices. When justified containment restricts a corrupt attempt to doing nothing, all root actions are either the required repair or identity. Their commuting, idempotent, option-preserving structure lets any `f+1` **distinct actual roots** restore without identifying all corrupt roots or trusting a success announcement. A shared vulnerable enforcer can invalidate this premise; copied reports are not new roots. These results connect diagnosis, persistent whole-rule repair and the actual fault model. A finite simulated control is not evidence of an implemented physical restoration architecture.

### Positive-error disclosure and shared randomness

[Read the two contracts and proofs](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/052b34c2ae9594c118465c0b12b03aa1b93ccc71486183b7aad907820ca87b54.md) · [Complete shared-random frontier](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/052b34c2ae9594c118465c0b12b03aa1b93ccc71486183b7aad907820ca87b54.md#5-complete-frontier-with-shared-randomness) · [Recorded scoped ordinary review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/2345ce91be761877205ec312de101aeab84d44982b858554d975584f9ee7e4bc.md).

There are `u` possible action roots, `b` corrupt ones with `0 ≤ b < u`, and `M ≥ 1` complete messages. An informed cooperative sender knows the corrupt set; the receiver must select a root. A fixed codebook assigns a lottery to each message. With shared randomness, the sender also sees a randomly selected codebook before choosing the message, while the corrupt set was committed independently beforehand. These are different information resources, not alternative names for the same receiver coin.

The paper gives constructive fixed-codebook bounds and an exact shared-random value: `binom(b,M)/binom(u,M)` for `1 ≤ M ≤ b`, and zero for `M ≥ b+1`. A uniformly sampled menu of `M` distinct roots attains the shared bound: the sender uses an intact root whenever the menu contains one. The exceptional event is that the whole menu is corrupt. This is a worst-case static-corruption result, not a prior claim that roots fail independently. At `(u,b,M)=(8,3,2)`, shared randomness gives `3/28`, below the fixed-codebook optimum `1/4`. Earlier incompleteness of the general fixed case is historical: the complete later result below resolves that stated classification.

### An exact two-message frontier

[Read the theorem and its hypotheses](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/6adf2975e5eb92a7d20800ac69c5083ba15842e252f196c5194db14c721c6a01.md#theorem) · [Constructive interpretation](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/6adf2975e5eb92a7d20800ac69c5083ba15842e252f196c5194db14c721c6a01.md#constructive-interpretation).

For two fixed messages the best arbitrary probability rows achieve exactly the best two disjoint uniformly weighted blocks. The source proves the lower bound through ordinal quotas and **two** chain matroids, then transfers it to arbitrary real probabilities by summation by parts. It also supplies a constructive rational interpretation. Examples include values `1/3` at `(u,b)=(7,3)` and `3/8` at `(11,5)`. This is a genuine exact classification, not merely a probability-grid search. Extending the two-matroid proof to three would require an invalid inference; the separate three-message work supplies its own argument.

### Three messages and four corrupt roots

[Read the elementary odd-universe theorem](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/23d208a45f35240cae5c15f20ed33faab581b2b68ca211b4238cf8bd4c13df82.md#theorem) · [Read the complete b=4, M=3 extension](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/196cd14d8755ade8e9490fba7a77d2d942d6a0b2b646251f0ca5264b99709502.md#theorem) · [Recorded independent certificate/proof review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/78cec40e65446228c25605abb047efb148b61c9dd5800e74146e0215f870a58d.md).

The odd-universe result proves `1/s` for `u=2s+1`, `b=4`, `M=3`, with its separately treated small endpoint. The extension covers every `u ≥ 5`, giving `1/floor((u-1)/2)`. A singleton plus two nearly equal uniform blocks attains the value. Most regimes have ordinary proofs; the three even universes `u=8,10,12` use finite exact rational dual-certificate trees that cover the continuous probability domain. That distinction preserves the actual evidence instead of calling a finite numerical grid a universal proof.

The retained [author checker](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/support-source/859583035289eeed556695bd39c881c3fdcf9b382b843d15613fdf35f5aebfd4.py), [independent checker](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/support-source/cecb4253268826e60168d7515ebb1e14bb550da4e7a4967f8cf7dc6e294705cd.py) and [certificate 129dad49](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/support-source/129dad49ba10349e4574f861895d280c789fb6f97c42509adb126b5104a083c2.json), [certificate fc703bd3](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/support-source/fc703bd37d65086f1f070b958f4fc5048f5d0bef5686c0ccd55694a255f01cc0.json), [certificate efad8b9e](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/support-source/efad8b9e207d7e736b8284d5c4a9e516d012f5c2f3c1130d0e865dc002a5971b.json), [certificate d6c32408](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/support-source/d6c3240834d91e9c8c945ad8029286dc43dbae02ce005def613f1a7d7b7a8502.json) are supporting components, not extra papers. The recorded review distinguishes assertion-based author code, which must not be run under optimisation, from the explicit-check independent verifier. No certificate was freshly replayed by this curation. The smaller general-game cases are not silently advertised as satisfying the additional `u ≥ 2b+1` constraint of the inherited diagnosis application.

### The complete fixed-codebook classification

[Read the model and theorem](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/dcbaf45a6a43878061f33259605be18510a6ba09f816514c8a9a36112570ebde.md#model-and-theorem) · [Required range clarification](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/ca20fe4c55bf906d578c43f92bd44399cd0f59af5265db7276a2a4a0f353f3f8.md) · [Recorded broad review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/34eb5ea04eba65aa1049718b772010a5a3a951280d41b63e03131e3b1248f63a.md) · [Recorded arithmetic/semantics review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/5ef69e148d510f5ed907251c268b97793fe3abc8c0b17f2ee3e42ef85700042b.md).

For every `u > b ≥ 0` and `M ≥ 1`, the paper gives an exact fixed-codebook value and an attaining partition policy, including arbitrary overlapping real probability rows. In the range `1 ≤ M ≤ b+1`, put `N=b-M+1`; then

```text
V_fixed = max  floor((qN+M-1)/u) / q
          1 ≤ q ≤ u-M+1
```

For `M ≥ b+1` the value is zero, with extra unused messages allowed. These endpoint conventions also cover `b=0` and `M>u`; an empty maximum or a `0/0` binomial quotient is not evaluated. The optimal codebook can use disjoint uniform blocks even though the competing class permits arbitrary real rows. The value needs a linear number of rational candidates; recovering an optimal partition has a stated dynamic programme, not a claim of constant-cost real arithmetic or free delivery.

Its universal lower bound explicitly depends on the published **all-integer partition-Kneser hypergraph theorem** under the named hypotheses, not an unjustified prime-only variant or an extension of two-matroid intersection. Root copies in this proof are combinatorial devices, not additional independent integrity sources. The two retained scoped ordinary reviews accept this classification with the range clarification; the historical `pending` source path does not override them. Neither this account nor those source reviews constitute new external novelty certification, fresh formalisation, independent review of the present refactor, or a theorem about every possible communication resource.

### When the information and corruption arrive

[Read the four schedules](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/fcfb1786cde8e3c12524d3f1769c7016e91a77e028e588a655af2b7e474776fe.md#four-schedules-and-their-exact-values) · [Recorded timing-deletion review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/042b3587ffc374867bead681da780a5c683c80a44039f4d6373cb9c98839cf7a.md).

The order of information can change the optimum even with identical roots and message counts. The paper separately optimises four games: corruption before shared randomness; corruption after the codebook but before the message; corruption after the full message but before a private draw; and corruption after the realised root is exposed. At `(u,b,M)=(8,3,2)` the respective values are `3/28`, `1/4`, `3/8` and `1`. These are four resource-constrained optima, not four success rates for one unchanged protocol. In particular, transplanting the earlier random-menu protocol into an adversary that sees that menu may do worse than redesigning a fixed codebook.

The result keeps compulsory action, observability, independent unrevealed coins and the adversary's permitted timing explicit. Allowing abstention changes the problem; “minimax” is not permission to expose a person to harm. The model's informational limits are not assertions that an omniscient source lacks future knowledge. This mathematical timing note is distinct from the collection's administrative clock-rounding erratum.

## Criterion correction and charged live repair

These six related Seventh works make a concrete progression from correcting an interpreted rule to preserving its authorised execution through faults and version changes. Their particular models and later formal evidence are not interchangeable, and later successors receive separate entries.

### Installing a criterion is not editing the draft

[Original argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/4df16d4432931594e928292b9b1535f195588d8ac5bd6af4ab5cd7aa1b0cf7c2/CRITERION_INSTALLATION_ARGUMENT.md) · [Related transport argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/855b8f438e5d5f05b53e2d6ee151375a1601d3e568f53b31316b336f07f3def1/TRANSPORT_ARGUMENT.md) · [T07-CRITERION-V3 — proof and evidence scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t07-technical/README.md#t07-criterion-v3).

The installation work gives a **working rule-change transition**, not merely a suggestion to rename a checker. In a two-constructor language, `NORMALIZED_LF` wrongly accepts an extra trailing newline for an exact-byte recovery task, while `EXACT` accepts precisely the fixed source. The latter's adequacy holds for every candidate sequence, not just the tested examples. Installation updates only the interpreted rule, its version and its history. It intentionally leaves the defective draft untouched; that now-visible document defect needs a separately authorised repair.

The accompanying transport argument explains why a recipient can reuse an adequacy proof without thereby acquiring another recipient's live permission. Source version, draft revision, installed-rule version and authority epoch are distinct. A copied rule can remain adequate after a grant expires. The cover-and-preserve result characterises uniform evidence-class transport under its stated predicate class, not every ordinary fallible warrant. **This is positive retained-standard correction:** the recovery target and reasons used to diagnose the weak rule survive the repair. It is not an established instance of overturning the entire authority basis, nor proof that an arbitrary updater or real grant system implements the typed transition.

### Charged live interlocks and repair after quiescence

[Original argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c33e271b68f0ab941bab29cf8ca679ea53d1d26c108071e2d7a313ae222022ab/MODEL_AND_PROOFS.md) · [T07-DYNAMIC-V2 — proof and evidence scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t07-technical/README.md#t07-dynamic-v2).

The dynamic model combines a physical-veto premise, versioned commands, revocation memory, cancellation and an executable repair strategy. With `n` genuine roots and a lifetime union of at most `B` faulty roots, a `q`-root path and `r`-root revocation set satisfy the declared safety and availability contract exactly when

```text
q + r > n + B
q ≤ n − B,   r ≤ n − B.
```

The minimum is `n = 3B + 1`, with `q = r = 2B + 1`. This is **not a new universal distributed-systems threshold**: the source explicitly imports familiar quorum mathematics and contributes its exact place in the fully charged repair mechanism. Every actual landing must traverse the same complete-command live gates; clocks, control ports and active shared dependencies cannot be made trustworthy by naming them infrastructure.

After a stable authorised descriptor is delivered and bounded intact service is available, exhaustive safe attempts repair the whole target, including safely cancelling withheld trials. The cap starts at that stable-delivery boundary, not at an unobserved global change. Safety permits endless holds; progress and persistence additionally need their stated horizon and service premises. Actual external consent must satisfy the source's reservation/closure contract—certificate arithmetic cannot redefine an owner's immediate withdrawal.

### One unknown correlated-root class

[Original argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a59fc17c52c53d341d19fbae37622056b682df5b3b771ddbb8b7ef76bbd28662/GENERAL_UNKNOWN_CLASS_THEOREM.md) · [T07-UNKNOWN-ROOT-ABC — proof and evidence scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t07-technical/README.md#t07-unknown-root-abc).

This work replaces knowledge of the exact root map with a warranted **family of possible maps**. Exactly one unknown class of `c` labels shares a root; the other labels are singletons. For `B ≥ 1` faulty actual roots, set `k = B + c − 1`. The argument derives robust root-image intersection from the maps themselves: every compatible map gives more than `B` shared roots exactly when the label intersection contains at least `B + c` labels. It handles an alias class bridging disjoint label sets and does not export this equivalence to the excluded zero-fault corner.

For fixed, possibly nonuniform path and revocation families, robust safety plus availability is possible exactly when `m ≥ 3k + 1`. The resulting actual-root cost of the unresolved class is `2(c − 1)` beyond the known-map minimum. Cancellation and search use the same charged map/fault budget. The positive construction and lower bound concern that fixed observation-compatible interface; extra mapping probes, changing the family after discovery or root churn change the problem. The later A–C kernel development checks the combinatorial statements, not by itself the original D cancellation mechanism, E search result or real-world root attribution.

### Opaque-repair portfolios and their cost

[Original argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/4d5425b42dad562700a7572a84a077dbcf9330de54b10dd6c755df0d10d8fde4/COVERING_TRADEOFF.md) · [T07-COVERING-OPAQUE — proof and evidence scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t07-technical/README.md#t07-covering-opaque).

An allowed path avoids the bad set exactly when its complement contains that set. This makes the minimum fixed portfolio a **covering design**, and the source proves the correspondence rather than defining an optimum into existence. For the known-root interface the attempt optimum is `C(n, n − q, B)`; the unknown-class version uses its separately justified maximal bad-label parameter. A positive target-achievement lower bound assumes the target starts defective—an already correct target needs no repair attempt.

Under the complete opaque-failure observation contract, adaptively ordering trials or adding private randomness cannot improve the almost-sure worst-case cap for every fixed compatible world. The lower-bound world is chosen outside the private-coin quantifier. More independently justified roots can buy fewer attempts; copying an existing root cannot. The ordinary small-parameter frontier includes explicit witnesses and a separate incidence/repetition lower proof; a finite graph sublemma is not the whole argument's kernel certificate. The later covering kernel supplies quantified portfolio and search results at its named scope. Physical service, safe cleanup and wall-clock latency remain separate obligations.

### Finite histories without an assumed safety invariant

[Original argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/7ae9af2dec2088c39766758f5565f2c2bd6536fbb7a9bd80f140087d3fb17a62/STATEMENT_AND_SOURCE.md) · [T07-FINITE-HISTORY — proof and evidence scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t07-technical/README.md#t07-finite-history).

The finite-history extension proves that **every admitted landing in an arbitrary allowed finite trace** has the current complete command, not merely that a hand-selected demonstration ends safely. A reached-state induction derives authentic local descriptors, monotone revocations, recorded acknowledgements and durable command tombstones. It does not assume safe landings in the invariant, expose the fault set to the guard or let the guard consult an omniscient global epoch.

An intact overlap with an actual stored revocation certificate excludes stale execution; an intact cancellation acknowledger excludes every later copy of the same full command, nonce and path. Accepted repairs restore the whole table, and unchanged-epoch continuations preserve the goal. This is a positive safety and conditional persistence theorem, **not an eventual-service schedule**. Its original whole-table state space is not the later version/history-changing typed plant. The source/model correspondence and finite Python scenarios are not a general compiler correctness theorem.

### Compiled installation and repair with distinct grants

[Original argument](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/fca4691e58cc5f05745eb0dd275783a167c1f8a2c3ef1488ffa01ef49bc0f36f/ARGUMENT.md) · [T07-COMPILED-COMPOSITION — proof and evidence scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t07-technical/README.md#t07-compiled-composition).

The compiled reference composes the actual interpreted installer and document-repair transitions. It preserves source/custody fields and exact successor records while using a later, separately provided repair grant. The important repair to the earlier proof pattern is that these transitions are **not literally idempotent**: successful installation or repair increments a counter and history. Instead, the old expected version or revision prevents that fixed old action from landing again; fresh valid actions can still preserve the achieved goals while adding history.

For the specific four-root, one-fault, four-path interface, two sufficiently stable authorised batches implement installation and subsequent draft repair. The recorded eight-macro-attempt/twenty-four-logical-phase upper bound excludes grant issuance, dissemination and residual older work, and is not a physical timing promise. The source's native executable and finite assertions are useful implementation evidence; the named kernel components do not certify arbitrary complete runtime histories. The later history, clock and batch developments should be read as separate extensions, not silently credited to this earlier work.


## Independent evidence, faithful evaluation and finite controllers: the Ninth interfaces

These four work families develop different links in a trustworthy computation: an independently checked existence certificate, faithful modeled expression execution, a finite implementation of an accepted initial history, and an invertible change of complete command identity. A result at one interface does not silently fill a gap at the others.

### Finite common-policy evidence without rerunning synthesis

The finite typed-data calculus supplies an actual [checker syntax][t09-syntax], [soundness proof][t09-soundness] and [completeness construction][t09-complete]. For a valid fixed finite rational hidden-model input and query, **an accepted finite body exists exactly when the inherited deterministic common lawful almost-sure parity policy exists**. Any admitted measurable-seed winning policy also yields existence of accepted finite syntax. The new contribution is the independently checked evidence interface, not another claim to have discovered the inherited winning-region theorem. A winning query does not make every submitted body valid.

The checker validates positive dimensions and actual shape before enumeration, normalized rational rows, sparse menus with empty defaults, exact state/candidate keys, every submitted node and every positive receipt branch. Components require an attained even minimum and compare full numerical rows, not just positive supports. The path construction shortens repeated-state walks to at most `n−1` edges; equal endpoints admit an empty path. That bounds submitted evidence syntax, not stochastic hitting time.

[Checked composition][t09-composition] validates both original bodies before canonicalisation, retaining literal obligations rather than unioning components into an unproved witness. A new parent needs a separately checked absent support and all child obligations. Raw merge/sort/link can discard a malformed duplicate, so they are not untrusted-entry admission APIs. The [bound envelope][t09-binding] requires actual structured-input equality before a fresh check; an old acceptance bit, equal label or digest is not a substitute. Even a harmless input revision can invalidate the old envelope while a newly checked body remains valid.

The [ordinary verification-cost argument][t09-cost] is polynomial in **explicit materialised input, submitted AST and rational bit lengths**; certificates may themselves be exponential. It is neither a Lean machine-cost theorem nor a polynomial witness-size result. The [source-owned guide and evidence][t09-cert-guide] preserve formal components separately from that ordinary argument. A certificate does not extract a runnable controller or finite-state memory strategy. The separate 21-path shared-root runtime has no supplied encoding into these input tables. Model authority, a safe byte decoder, linked binary behaviour and physical safety remain additional application premises.

### Expression stacks, costs and the Python trust boundary

The [exact expression scope][t09-p1-scope] and [source/model map][t09-p1-map] connect a manually defined Lean stack machine to the pinned Python evaluator. The universal local result permits arbitrary pending work and pre-existing values: a successful expression leaves its value above the unchanged old value stack and does not execute the pending tail. It agrees with the expression semantics. Success costs one tick per leaf and two plus child costs per compound; a resource fault instead stops early with the same modeled error category and visible meter count. The [wording clarification][t09-p1-clarify] prevents treating available mathematical fuel as pops executed after failure.

Every popped item ticks before dispatch, including ready visits. Binary children execute left then right and reduction pops right then left. Variables bypass width checking; binary arithmetic precedes its result check. Power checks exponent plus one **before** shifting and has a distinct exponent-storage fault. Thus an expression can return a large register value under a small width budget, while a host allocation can fail before a modeled binary-result rejection. Adequate finite ideal bounds depend on the actual input and need not be cheap or physically safe to compute.

The admitted domain requires finite immutable correctly aritied tuples, exact builtin natural integers, ordinary dictionaries and valid natural budgets. Those are preconditions, not validations supplied by `Meter` or `eval_expr`; booleans, subclasses, malformed direct tuples and concurrent mutation are excluded. The [recorded theorem and finite evidence][t09-expression-guide] include independently written recursive-oracle and pending-stack controls, but AST/source identity and finite tests are not a verified CPython frontend. Parser, serializer, statements, loops, full runner and physical resource failures remain separate. The later tail-completion record preserves earlier failures and credits reused products only once.

### Finite reconstruction and an actual initial-state controller

The [finite primitive refinement][t09-refinement], [sampled-policy reconstruction][t09-history] and [uniform initial-state endpoint][t09-native] form one implementation lineage. Actual finite storage operations preserve shared-root mutation and distinct addressed-label receipts; a gate snapshot alone does not authenticate a policy. Callable reconstruction rejects missing or wrong-epoch samples, while proof-side finite representation recovers the **exact** accepted endpoint using full row-to-source equality. Successful reconstruction alone is guard-free: an illegal immediate completion can be reconstructed. Equal epochs or projected delivery events are weaker than authentic full policy inputs.

The positive operational result fixes one public ordered program **before** every matching hidden environment and permitted finite service decoration. In the specified empty epoch-zero ABC fixture, seven holds followed by 21 trials yield exactly 259 logical events, one installation and zero repairs, with the exact installed Plant. In the recorded worst-routing, withholding fixture, the first 20 trials (247 events) leave the Plant unchanged while preparing and cancelling commands; other admitted service decorations can install earlier. This is an actual accepted trace of emitted events, not inferred from synthetic success flags; missing/excess annotations and malformed reply-array lengths reject.

The [original result and execution records][t09-controller-guide] distinguish fresh kernel components from compiled finite assertions. This entry point explicitly rejects even a valid epoch-one repair. Public-program independence does not imply transcript opacity, and the ABC installation is not the earlier 3,013-byte repair example. Arbitrary reachable-idle entry, unknown function-state serialisation, physical storage/time, arbitrary asynchronous fairness and standalone linked-C deployment are outside the supplied endpoint.

### Bijective whole-history transport is not in-place permission

The [transport reading guide][t09-transport-read], [selected-order involution][t09-transport] and [ordered-trace equivalence][t09-trace] give a separate mathematical result. For each chosen support, swap the old order with one coherently supplied duplicate-free order. Other orders and all non-order command fields survive. This is injective even when distinct commands have the same selected-label support; sorting every path instead would collapse identities.

Transport every commitment, tombstone, receipt and corruption-payload key consistently. Then primitive operations commute with the transformation, and legal steps and complete finite traces hold **iff** they hold for the transported states and pointwise events. The same `badOpen` Boolean, typed effect and full non-key state fields are preserved. Reusing the changed command in an unchanged store can fail precisely because an old commitment or tombstone still has its old key.

The [scoped proof and review route][t09-transport-evidence] does not authorise physical in-place conversion, signature reminting or old-key reuse. Counterstates carry their explicit reachability conditions. Raw native-list equality, arbitrary interface invariance, public-program composition, an executable serializer and actual authority remain separate questions. The isomorphism explains what must change together; it does not grant permission to make those changes.

## Occurrence, completion and finite warrant: the Tenth works

These seven work/assessment entries route the twelve existing `T10-*` result scopes. They are not twelve new papers. The [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md) distinguish ordinary mathematics, component-level formal evidence, finite software checks and inherited source inspection.

### Open-context effects and the cost of reacquisition

**Open-context continuation and source-ordered reacquisition.** Read the [article](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/221a50045570edb56a62012eff862df258f9af0ae51d74d116ff224e5c5498e7/ARTICLE.md) and [exact effect algebra](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/b824c43b4f4fad672bb96e930241787b8d07d36a69d49f20ea9d85d2d51b43d2/EXACT_EFFECT_ALGEBRA.md). An open fragment can enter, suspend, restore and emit a source context. Its finite symbolic effect retains precisely the incoming origins needed by the selected output, without supplying their missing role values. Composition substitutes those origins and preserves the exact stack precondition. Distinct strings can retain one original origin; identical strings from different origins remain distinct.

After the boundary identities are warranted, the selected author-role question is a conjunction over the unresolved distinct origins. Under fixed positive request costs, adequate role-witness responses and the all-positive/single-flip locality contract, minimum worst-case deterministic exact decision cost equals minimum weighted cover of those origins. A minimum cover gives a completing policy. Genuine source-ordered **role-witness** windows remain interval-convex after copying and reordering the outputs, with the stated weighted dynamic programme. Arbitrary whole-card access is a different service and can realise general set cover.

The [oracle and force qualifications](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a282ebf891d26ca2e55fc93bdb73fa3159f60a6388545b930076c17fd73c7571/ORACLE_AND_FORCE_PRECISIONS.md) keep original-document role separate from present-carrier force. Merely reading a proposition, preserving a digest or having a source link is not adequate context. Extra informative metadata, a valid task-specific verdict or an available full-source read must be credited; they can defeat the narrow lower bound. This is ordinary source-attribution mathematics using established methods, not a new natural-language parser, authentication service, permission grant or superiority claim over an equally informed conventional implementation. See the [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md).

### A bounded handoff needs the right witness, not maximal retention

**Which source witnesses make a bounded handoff adequate?** The [bounded release paper](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a25c4464f85c4b9a8f920b2e208e35164cf821ef69f352d341b4c86046b434a4/BOUNDED_RELEASE_DECISION.md) derives a concrete preventive choice. The next task needs the three roles `a,b,c`; the surviving service offers only cards `ab` and `bc`, each costing one request. With a one-request limit, retaining `b` still leaves both edges unresolved and costs two reads in the worst case. Retaining either `a` or `c` permits completion with one. A warranted fixed-task verdict permits zero later reads. Copying `a` twice does not create an extra source obligation.

The compared one-witness retention policies are fixed before labels are known. A label-dependent selector can itself communicate information and therefore belongs to another observation contract. The [premise and alternatives ledger](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/68d885c2cec1f9551dc9899bac224c17ddb3ebd0f9e2858b7989fea19c12c9dc/PREMISE_AND_ALTERNATIVE_LEDGER.md) keeps the practical inference separate: organised legitimate reliance, knowledge of exposure, proportionate feasible care and absence of an overriding reason are independent premises. The conclusion permits any adequate arrangement; it is not a duty to retain one canonical artifact or support every future use forever. No real source withdrawal, physical experiment or universal sourcehood verdict is established. See the [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md).

### Finite source-context reference software

**Finite source-context continuation checker.** The [software guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/08d2b29597f4ee32240d7c54ed0fa70b1aa6d1e35d6d16253fd3fde078ddb04d/README.md), [context-effect implementation](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/0f65f172926e61ff7280ec2e13fd36b4ff287ea530ebbfa00c9dce831f3355fa/context_effects.py) and [cover/observation implementation](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/808a9297d27b903722b4fc04ad9a526d3cfc3e371b14e5047e1d33d1c6e3e79f/read_cover.py) provide runnable restricted models, not additional papers. The guide distinguishes symbolic context effects, the interval solver, exponential subset cover, and an independent finite decision-tree optimiser. Transcript locality and adequate role coverage are separate checks: a constant response can satisfy one while failing the other.

The supplied source-role fixture is an analytical input, not newly authenticated evidence. Reported unit, exhaustive, mutation and independent-model runs retain the [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md); reading them is not replay. The current public reproduction boundary excludes the historical wrapper requiring unpublished primary captures. The finite checks do not prove the unbounded argument, general parsing, actual authority or moral obligations. Normal-mode assertion-based controls must not silently be run under optimisation.

### Backward completion is not pointwise forward convergence

**Backward completion, forward convergence and dependent inverse limits.** Read the [ordinary mathematical correction](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/9ef042d82a12e7aadb8219e41741a21297172dcef72bea3102916eef84f960ad/INVERSE_LIMIT_AND_CONVERGENCE_CORRECTION.md) with its [uniform formal scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/9b3a728681a2a4f46db0bd1a8c9cf9de5f2c885ab4ab8b6eb6b68b4ffb4d5f62/CHECKED_SCOPE.md) and [dependent-space formal scope](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/10e2a4de40dd7a6a2c3fe1e7bc86683fc85f10d0ac236520ce37b7441fbedee0/CHECKED_SCOPE.md). Having predecessors at every finite depth, having one compatible infinite ancestry and having forward orbits approach a fixed point are different claims. The paper constructs complete bounded continuous countermodels to the unrestricted equivalences.

Its positive repair is precise: for a nonempty compact metric space and continuous self-map, singleton finite-depth survival, exact uniqueness of backward realisation and **uniform** forward attraction to a fixed point are equivalent. Compactness earns compatible predecessors rather than assuming them. Pointwise convergence is still insufficient, even for a compact homeomorphism. Strict contraction with bounded image gives another sufficient route; contraction on the whole real line alone does not force unique backward history.

The separate indexed formal component allows different nonempty compact Hausdorff coordinate spaces and continuous bonding maps. Survivors at a coordinate are exactly values of compatible full paths. In the metric case exact uniqueness is equivalent to shrinking coordinate ranges and forgetting remote boundaries on each **fixed finite horizon**. The horizon precedes the depth bound; no uniformity over every starting time or computable rate is inferred. The nonuniform proposal named only by historical hash is not presented as an available full paper.

The [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md) preserve the actual Lean components and the first failed adapter attempt. The ordinary compact-homeomorphism control, eventual-compactness/contraction extensions and source-domain interpretation are not promoted to formal results. Neither a mathematical state space nor an algebraic counterexample becomes a metaphysically possible cosmos merely by being represented.

### A computable completion and its exact query need different evidence

**From compact completion to finite recipient warrant.** The [finite-limit addendum](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/94a47f2ed5926fcef2e178544640eb7f713af3d33b49dd55d0a45445c8769320/MINIMAL_ADDENDUM.md) proves that a decreasing nonempty compact family whose survivors lie in an ambient open answer region eventually has a whole finite stage inside that region. Effective upper-compact/open representations give a terminating search on robust instances; ordinary compactness alone does not supply an algorithm.

For a valid total unary P02-L1 bit program, the bonding maps `(x+b_i)/2` give a unique computable completion with error `2^(-n)`. Finite dyadic interval endpoints provide sound and complete witnesses for each strict rational comparison. They do not provide equality witnesses. A bounded-step halting construction makes exact zero equivalent to nonhalting, ruling out a uniformly sound computably enumerable finite-certificate verifier complete for zero across that whole family. Individual global equality proofs remain possible.

For example, prefix `000` bounds the scalar in `[0,1/8]`, enough for the declared claim `<1/4`, not for `=0`. A different receiver rule cannot be silently substituted. The [eighteen mathematical controls](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/2f9f3b008b1afeac4ef9e3786835e811c95a9ad84d96c30c90dcda93f7b50b53/MATHEMATICAL_CONTROLS.md) are written expected-outcome arguments, not eighteen newly executed tests. The [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md) do not transfer nearby Lean verification to the P02 reduction or the threshold table.

### The bounded printed collation is a dated source assessment

**Bounded printed-source collation.** The [accepted short addendum](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/cb9aa16dc764ad0aa193ca464e2335a8474b782084a573ab1ede1420eda228ff/COLLATION_ADDENDUM.md) reports a specific pre-web discrepancy between the named English print/electronic reading and the Leonine main text, whose apparatus also records the negative variant. It leaves the translators’ Latin base and production responsibility unestablished. The inherited witness identities and acceptance are available through the [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md).

This is a source-assessment record supporting the occurrence case—not a new edition, a universal preservation claim, an independently repeated collation or an inference of culpability. Original captures remain outside this public body population. The source’s positive bounded finding is retained without claiming this refactor inspected those captures again.

### Bibliographic identity is not a verified final PDF

**Bibliographic identity and the proof-marked source witness.** The [dated verification note](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/cdff07e133a38347c1a0f5b31787af914c50f3023e6690a42a356ec97ab4e74e/VERIFICATION_NOTE.md) identifies its bibliographic record while distinguishing a publisher-hosted proof-marked witness, a different shorter attachment and an unavailable archive lead. Its bounded search found no correction but did not establish that none exists or that the inspected witness was final. No hash is attributed to unacquired PDF bytes.

Publication year, registration/deposit date and PDF revision date are different. This is a retained 3 October 2026 source check, not a fresh online verification. Its typography and final-domain qualification limitations remain alongside the ordinary mathematical correction. The [original Tenth result, review and replay scopes](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t10-technical/README.md) prevent turning a qualified witness-relative correction into rejection of every later version or the author’s complete philosophical position.

## Typed contexts, substitution and source representation: the Eleventh works

The four works in the [Eleventh source, result and suite guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t11-types/README.md) make distinct constructive advances. The research raw-index namespace called `P01DF`, the public P01DF owner, the finite nucleus `P01TC`, recursive-All `P01AC.Has`, and the later HasPlus/HasE variants are not one interchangeable calculus. Existing theorem qualification is retained without claiming adoption or new replay by this catalogue.

### Literal substitution in the original raw-index judgement

**Constructive substitution in the unchanged raw-index judgement.** The [complete proof/scope account](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/f47dc5f616e63c186630ed459221d2c80d10b67616f057add2a46c2ee10c2030/CHECKED_SCOPE.md) establishes both `Has p A → Has (psub σ p) (substIndex σ A)` and `Has p A → Has p (substType τ A)` for arbitrary total substitution maps. No new caller premise restricting images to closed, well-formed or finite-support types is introduced. The type substitution keeps the original polynomial literally, including opaque composite finite atoms. The [original source/kernel review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c0b598b80f5ccd3bbcc2a3c8c9c03c8c51e4e77c9ba92845eaed5982d0178bd8/REVIEW.md) explains why these are equalities of the existing syntax, not merely equal interpretations.

Index substitution reconstructs all fifteen existing Has rules. J’s motive uses a double lift, preserving both proof and endpoint coordinates. Arbitrary type substitution uses the existing unconditional All introduction/elimination rules and an internally proved finite support bound: temporary raising cancels at instantiation, so an inserted free `param 0` survives a later elimination. In the mixed square the index map must also act on the inserted type images. Leaving them unchanged is false and has a concrete syntax control.

This supplies reusable proof-level certificates within that exact raw-index judgement, not an executable serialised transformer or parser. It adds no atom-expansion rule, abstraction congruence, equality reflection, general typed context, normalisation or actual-source warrant. The source rule set—not the spelling of its namespace—determines where the result can be reused. The [Eleventh source, result and suite guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t11-types/README.md) retain the failed declaration-prefix adapter attempt and its qualified retry without duplicate scientific credit.

### Typed telescopes with derived heterogeneous soundness

**Finite typed-telescope nucleus and exact representation.** The [nucleus guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a8dac2778a7e2bcc4014868679c2c6bba79807fba6d5c4f302b1cae495772f26/CHECKED_SCOPE.md) constructs finite typed contexts, formation and syntax-generated typing for dependent Pi, represented Sigma, typed identity, Raw and arbitrary PER-valued parameters. Semantic coherence and the fundamental theorem are **derived conclusions**, never typing-rule admission premises. Raw predicates are defined before their laws, and generated mutual induction proves universal two-valuation soundness, endpoint transport and a strong diagonal law at related but possibly unequal valuations.

Literal typed substitution preserves source polynomial and target type. Each component is typed after earlier required components are instantiated; unused term images are atom zero, not an unrestricted identity tail. The representation invokes the existing contextual J itself and checks its two coordinates and exact source code. Concrete controls use unequal related I/SKK valuations and inhabited varying fibres, so the result is not supported only by empty domains.

The [original source/kernel review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/6e1832ad33853ba245c46ef65e7eb560bfb79f76ea5c566b5488a1b223a856c5/REVIEW.md) makes the legacy comparison precise: it consumes a retained **Type-valued** old-rule derivation tree, its support/translation certificate, and its displayed conversion endpoints. It does not recover erased construction history from a Prop-valued Has proof or hidden transitivity witnesses inside PolyConv. Both old All rules are excluded at every depth. `AllFinite` is an opaque finite impredicative leaf, not recursive dependent All. Identity is equality in the selected PER with the specified proof witnesses, not raw conversion reflection or numerical identity of people. The [Eleventh source, result and suite guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t11-types/README.md) preserve this positive bounded conjunction without claiming full U11, universes or canonical adoption.

### Recursive dependent All with two distinct binder sorts

**Recursive dependent All and mixed substitution.** The [complete proof guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/18db66b880752841d3103651ee62224312bd37c2b030a77b44011f56439b4140/PROOF_GUIDE.md) extends the nucleus with genuine recursive All over typed telescopes. All shifts type parameters in the surrounding context; Pi/Sigma shift term indices in the body **and in replacement types**. The two operations must follow their proved mixed equations. Formation and typing remain finite syntax, while raw unary/heterogeneous semantics are defined together before soundness.

Conditional semantic replacement requires the exact `ImageMatch` equations for both endpoints, the cross-relation and both diagonals. Those are metatheorem hypotheses, not a semantic oracle smuggled into typing. Smaller formation results derive them for admitted replacements. Literal mixed substitution separately preserves syntax and code, with atom-zero term padding and Bottom type padding. The represented mixed map allows different image PER environments at different term valuations; it does not substitute equality for the heterogeneous Link.

The [original source/kernel review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/963ff1bc8b09a31637ed9b616410807bd97d1565a6da6d5944573fd4e0bbba21/REVIEW.md) records complete bounded comparisons: every nucleus constructor translates, while a broader retained legacy-tree support judgement now includes both old All rules and genuinely dependent All. That does not alter the earlier nucleus restriction or expose hidden Prop proof internals. The concrete `Q = All α. Pi x:α. Sigma y:α. Id α x y` and its self-instance keep the exact bracket-abstracted pair polynomial. A malformed replacement counterexample shows why the diagonal hypotheses are necessary.

No universe decoder, Type:Type, raw eta, arbitrary abstraction congruence, global carrier repair or general frontend correctness follows. This is the exact recursive-All construction reported in the [Eleventh source, result and suite guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t11-types/README.md), not a general assertion that every typing representation is normalising or reifiable.

### An identity application without a normal unapplied source

**Polymorphic identity source-normalisation boundary.** The [boundary note](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/f9690e33ee57f8e665d47954e7dbe0cfedf7ddc6770557d3e2566ccd055dbc23/README.md) keeps one literal witness throughout: the exposed polynomial `app(app(atom S,atom K),app(atom K,atom Omega))`, evaluating to `t = S K (K Omega)`. Its retained old derivation and translated P01AC certificate have the displayed polymorphic identity target. That target is separately inhabited by the finite identity `I`.

For every raw argument `a`, `t a` contracts by S and then K to `a`. Nevertheless the unapplied `t` has **no normal reduct** in the specified full compatible source reduction. Inverting the reduction rules shows that every reduct retains the wrapper around an Omega reduct. Inherited confluence and finite normalisation then exclude every source-Conv-equivalent `FiniteDerives` representative, at any finite type. This is a source-preserving representation obstruction, not an empty-target argument.

The [original source/kernel review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/2c67e22d0b19126c63fc9f1e476dd38d06174bd9208d816680165a64b437ea23/REVIEW.md) distinguishes the exposed application polynomial from the opaque `atom(t)`, even though their evaluations agree. It also separates `(K I) Omega`, which has a normal reduct, from the present stronger witness. Returning an argument in two head contractions is not universal strategy termination or a proof that unapplied `t` is source-convertible to `I`. A PER-equivalent identity representative is not excluded. The later Thirteenth/Fourteenth work addresses further representation questions with its own rule sets; it does not turn this inherited bounded corollary into a new calculus. See the [Eleventh source, result and suite guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t11-types/README.md) for exact formal and review stages.

## Continuation repair geometry and information

These are the source-owned first-return package and Second-return mathematical notes, not new results created by this index. The [continuation result owner](../provenance/v5-research-continuations/RESULT_STATUS.json), [source map](../provenance/v5-research-continuations/SOURCE_MAP.json) and [isolated-suite guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/README.md) connect each existing result family to its exact sources, reviews and toolchain. Formal components, dated reviews and corrected interfaces retain separate scopes. The full collection remains broader than this first curation slice.

### The repaired P01–P03 research package

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/f3a3bb02cfa49054a46d4ff108ac0f8f603b78b10a4799952b6a336de2a16226.md). The first return supplies repaired finite-source/type-effect, dependent-index, measure and guarded-runtime components. Its status map distinguishes actual constructive interfaces: recursive PER semantics; finite raw-index dependent transport; exact finite-Mealy atomic mass; a computable numeric observer interface; and finite parser/runtime comparisons. `Option`/`PLift` is a real representation change, not evidence that every original signature survived unchanged.

The finite observer solver is a positive algorithmic result; it does not decide the infinite quantified property of every productive program. The structural primitive-recursion compiler is not a numeric codec theorem. The finite JSON agreement cases do not certify the whole parser, exporter, locking system or distributed revocation. This is one linked research-software package, not one new paper per compiled module or receipt. Its named `T1-*` result scopes remain individually available in the original result owner.

### A shared local repair direction

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/a9eb8521054eb3769ff424037f438ae38f0f0c113d73624efc9d05dea3b35483.md). For a finite truth hull, an interior point and a transverse perturbation, the note characterises common small coherent repairs under a family of positive-definite quadratic scores by agreement of their tangent normal-equation solution. The necessity does not simply assume convergent normalised repairs. The converse gives a common **strict** repair ray, not only an obstruction to repair.

On a categorical simplex, diagonal metrics give a proportional-weight consequence; in a general truth hull the geometry can leave more freedom. A norm read out from an accepted repair is a model parameter, not automatically a warranted norm. The supplied formal-scope record distinguishes the original positive-barycentric input from its later intrinsic-interior wrapper, and formal results from ordinary quantitative/symmetry consequences.

### Dependency blocks and quadratic norm freedom

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/55dc1c64cb207d0a4425ec989aab9c94a65490bda4191814fa4a055d5e148aab.md). The quadratic classification asks which changes of coordinate weights preserve the same corrective access on a finite truth hull. The diagonal ratio must preserve its tangent space, equivalently it is constant on each elementary affine-dependency component. Weight ratios inside a constrained block are fixed; relative scales between blocks can remain free.

These are affine dependencies of truth vectors, not assertions of statistical dependence between witnesses. The finite transverse tests establish a sufficient testing arrangement, not a proven minimal query budget. Its classical linear-algebra ancestry is acknowledged by the source. The result restricts compatible norm representations without selecting the true norm or all comparisons between coherent alternatives.

### Smooth categorical repair and its sharp exceptions

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/3d1c584a1b91f4d0190605cd73d1cba8ae3bf631e461865de301b4711928d9be.md). For two fixed coordinate-additive smooth proper Bregman generators on a genuine categorical simplex with at least three outcomes, universal local common repair is equivalent to one common positive scale and coordinatewise affine differences. The constructive Bregman minimiser provides the converse; neither its existence nor Hessian agreement is silently assumed.

Two outcomes are a real exception: the specified quadratic and logarithmic scores share arithmetic coherification without proportional generators. A three-coordinate counterexample has no common repair, and nonseparable scores need a different contract. The same standard identities must be retained across all coherent contexts. The formal-scope record identifies the exact theorem spine and the separate ordinary interpretations; it is not a historical-priority certificate or an executable optimiser for arbitrary smooth inputs.

### Smooth repair on general dependency blocks
