### Endpoint atoms can change the cost without changing the interface

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/263f4bcc9a50f73830f4d6826ccd70dca5527f09e1d1cf970cdee9a1c365f689.md). For the retained literal loss, an arbitrarily small atom at zero together with a divergent interior left inverse moment forces infinite expected total failure for every common measurable policy. A separate right-endpoint proof gives the corresponding right inverse-moment necessity. With both endpoint atoms positive, a common coherent measurable finite-budget policy exists **if and only if** the interior inverse-variance moment is finite, without a lower-growth hypothesis. An endpoint by itself is not fatal: pure zero, pure one and their endpoint-only mixture have the source's separate correct-report constructions after acquisition.

The negative statement concerns prior-averaged expectation, not necessarily infinitely many errors on almost every run. It does not say that every endpoint-contaminated prior is impossible. [The recorded endpoint review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/8dc7c18a19c65f1ce087c4e64e4537d6961284d363903451ecd2a40fcda26497.md) identifies the actual-process formal endpoints, including the all-zero-word overlap mechanism and the positive cases.

### The same report policy before and after contamination

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/2a585094e4cdb8c402854780aa9bb9fac3d8598349de448731acfb905c53150a.md). This bridge transports full observed words, their count functions and measurable seeded policies from the countable-tag construction to the real-parameter innovation experiment. It proves equality of actual expectations, not just agreement of parameter marginals. The concrete sparse prior then has a finite coherent witness, while every proper zero-atom contamination has infinite expected count for all policies on the **same** source space, loss and information interface.

The end-to-end terminal theorem has its own [same-interface transport review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/e40d89c1d65f9053bafc6aea41ba93af0b510586ba188e14ba5a8f6d9a50c71f.md). The mathematical witness need not be an extracted MAP program; complete replacement by the known endpoint is excluded from this proper-contamination claim. Transport of a law is not itself a new empirical experiment or a proof that a finite machine handles every input.

### An exact benchmark for passive two-coin mistakes

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/f53131ceb1c9e4a92237a9cdf6e0813f947e098a2135daefea81c9e205c8a36d.md). The retained review provides an ordinary proof audit of the symmetric two-coin minimax value. The coin parameters are `1/2−δ` and `1/2+δ`, with `0 < δ ≤ 1/2` in this review, so δ is half their separation. With passive iid receipts and a common causal policy, empirical majority with a first-observed-bit tie rule balances tagwise risks and simultaneously attains the post-acquisition optimum `1/(8δ²) − 1/2`. Charging a zero-receipt action and allowing independent randomisation changes the value to `1/(8δ²)`.

Its argument extends the candidate's positive-gap range through the separately treated perfect-separation endpoint. A fixed tie preference can be Bayes-optimal without being minimax; adding two maxima would also give an invalid lower-bound argument. The exact ordinary proof and finite control receipts remain review evidence for that frozen candidate. They are not a Lean theorem, a fresh replay, a finite-memory implementation, or a guarantee for controlled acquisition.

### Sparse learning can survive smooth local uncertainty

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/b3403229c91a718f3e8c61068649590ec090d208206d9922da2270f71e956658.md). Instead of one atom per centre, each component may be any probability law inside a sufficiently narrow relative interval. With the source's small-width promise, one coherent full-prefix classifier works for **all** those shapes precisely when the spacing series is finite. Uniform components therefore give an absolutely continuous finite-budget example with divergent inverse moments. Merely replacing atoms by densities does not destroy the positive result.

The explicit selector clips its thresholds and floors the empirical mean at `1/n`, so an all-zero prefix still yields a defined finite decision. A naive nearest-positive-support argmin need not exist. This classification is ordinary mathematics; its narrower formal process and policy kernels are not a formal proof of every continuous-measure lifting. The wider admissible-width result and critical case are separate successors below.

### The strict-width guarantee and its sharp quantifiers

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/767be5173842b1520af940db691120091d868677754e7b452a0c7467ece4665d.md). For `δ < ε` and the retained small-width bound, a shifted report works uniformly over every allowed component shape in sufficiently small tail clusters; a finite empirical head handles the others. The same spacing condition is necessary and sufficient for this distribution-free guarantee. The policy switches using the **observed selected** cluster, not the unknown true one. The upper bound deteriorates as the width margin shrinks.

At critical or larger width, the negative result is **existential over component laws**, not universal: balanced incompatible endpoint pairs, or narrow absolutely continuous edge strips, can force every policy's expected count to diverge. The [final ordinary extension](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/99847d69a9a9df978a7e596cc35b75b744e7661b72dd514d29bc3cf4d897cc6c.md) and [formal/ordinary scope review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/3cdf7cb6582b44a2198ce9527847416ee834aa7636999871081e1677600a9f60.md) distinguish genuine atomic actual-process formal endpoints from the ordinary positive continuous-shape theorem.

### At critical width the internal shape matters

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/e2afa027d652771010f909eb34642d5512c102a34c85a0ef90ff466b9fd7e0ae.md). Uniform mass across the whole critical interval still has finite expected count exactly when the spacing sum is finite. Two overlapping accepted-report intervals permit a local left/right rule. Only thin edge regions risk the wrong choice; integrating their small component mass keeps the extra cost finite. This is a constructive positive theorem, not an extrapolation of strict width at equality.

With the same centres, weights, tolerance and outer interval promise, concentrating mass into narrow edge strips instead gives the opposing all-policy lower bound. Both priors may be absolutely continuous and lack endpoint atoms. The shape promise is therefore material at the critical boundary; finite data cannot simply certify that a component is uniform. The positive theorem and its full continuous-mixture consequences retain their ordinary scope.

### Stable finite horizons can conceal infinite lifetime cost

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/c079f7733904976a602adae47284062ad23141dd9c79b04652e3561e74692a50.md). For zero-atom contamination, the source derives an exact finite-horizon optimal increment from the maximum all-zero-word likelihood score. One deterministic tie-resolved MAP policy attains every finite-horizon optimum under the stated countable support contract; this is an existence result, not an exact-real implementation.

For the exponential-square sparse support the long-horizon cost has a square-root-logarithmic leading law at each fixed proper contamination. Every finite-horizon optimum still approaches the original optimum as contamination vanishes, while every positive proper contamination has infinite lifetime cost. The two limits do not commute. The total cost need not increase monotonically to complete replacement: the known pure endpoint has zero positive-time cost. The exact clipping formula is special to endpoint contamination, not a general formula for interior contaminants.

### Harmful perturbations need not add an endpoint atom

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/b1a029f0cd442ff623d9783c15e09260cd6063bf976290010b3fba07ec0548bf.md). A finite-budget centre prior can be perturbed by any positive proportion of an incompatible critical-pair prior, within an arbitrarily small total-variation neighbourhood, yet every common policy then has infinite expected lifetime count. A separate ordinary construction compares uniform critical components with edge-strip components, retaining absolute continuity and the same outer supports and weights.

The all-policy quantifier permits redesigning the policy after the perturbed prior is specified; it is not merely failure of the old rule. At any fixed finite horizon, bounded counts still give a Lipschitz comparison. Atomic actual-process formal endpoints and ordinary continuous-prior consequences retain distinct evidence. This interior-contaminant construction includes complete replacement by its bad contaminant, unlike the pure-known-endpoint exception above.

### Two successful priors may have no successful proper mixture

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/09fce51476c5dcf19b21ae90da0d7aa8251741ae0d0ef7e695925aea07c96812.md). Two rescaled interior point priors each admit a finite-expected-count policy with the **same** tolerance and report interface. Every proper mixture instead gives infinite expectation for every common policy. Exact normaliser rescaling and within-pair incompatibility, rather than a false assumption that all support points are mutually incompatible, drive the theorem. The attached actual-process declarations and [original review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/23eedf82a166d1f6ab49d6fb91b918b40c25130864204e8f48877337cb3f9e8b.md) establish the scoped formal/nonformal division.

This does not contradict concavity of optimal Bayes cost, almost-sure eventual learning, or the finite-family controller theorem. Each of these two candidate priors contains infinitely many possible transition parameters. Revealing which side generated the run enables a finite-cost combined policy, but supplies a correlated observation—not an independent seed. The theorem blocks a generic black-box mixture guarantee under unchanged information, not every possible method of combination.

### Almost-sure eventual correctness without a finite mean

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/fe422970716f0b5c83033f0f790a4548d55d538b798e9a47f05095d81ea6f39c.md). One common empirical report has a finite conditional expected count at every fixed interior parameter. Joint measurability and Fubini then lift the **zero probability of infinite count** to every interior-supported prior, without requiring the numerical conditional bound to be integrable. For the proper mixtures just described, that same rule makes only finitely many errors almost surely while every policy has infinite prior-averaged expectation.

This is an independently derived ordinary addendum to the retained nonconvexity source, not another cohort or a restatement of the entire formal package. Its extra Lean probe checks the fixed-parameter bound; the arbitrary-prior almost-sure lifting remains ordinary. Parameter-dependent null sets are allowed. Neither a uniform deterministic deadline nor finite expected last-error time follows.

### An executable rational witness with an honest resource boundary

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/6b796642b23d6280cfec717f7a762a55fe76a2fbc67b16f6c6b0fc1008599476.md). The source replaces real-log boundaries by exact integer/rational comparisons on dyadic-square centres `2^(-(i+1)^2)`. For a nonempty truthful prefix it inspects at most `ceil(log₂ n)+1` candidate indices. It computes neither an infinite normaliser nor an exact-real Bayes argmax. The ordinary argument preserves the sparse criterion with a stated larger conservative constant.

The [runtime contract](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/236f78b5ecc313f47085a765f26ef2f0482fdaff154c2aebb7393a8c01c59236.md) and [entry-point guide](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/cab3e124b6ef2faad2c945c20d22d50e7fc2d1455367bcd4cb6dcf633908b3b9.md) distinguish an uncapped mathematical core from capped CLI/adapters. Candidate 3 also corrects a valid-input decimal-guard failure with chunked conversion, without changing a global interpreter guard. The [retained review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/251394f394fe75f9062aaa4dea33da2b3ffa3370572e14ba60e52ceb1c68a30b.md) credits those repairs while leaving physical memory, arrival-rate latency, prior authentication and interpreter/Lean extraction unproved. Critical-uniform mode cannot be substituted for strict-width arbitrary shapes. A supplied count summary represents receipts; it does not acquire or authenticate them.

### Why a latent failure criterion is not an observed loss table

[Read the source](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/fe1b8f9c639009f4232ef03f6f18d26927ad5a06249b02dde3d0defd1a1c30e9.md). The source's actual literal indicator is zero at one interior Bernoulli parameter and one at another for the same report. Any common nonnegative next-bit score with expectation zero at a full-support parameter must be zero at both possible bits; its expectation cannot then become one. The attached `literal_no_common_next_bit_score` declaration imports the retained indicator rather than a lookalike.

[The original comparison/review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/b6a6db5048293e4de7f286689ea635448ea61aec5f9a08acff0968892e10b5bb.md) relates this exact obstruction to ordinary prediction guarantees whose loss is indexed by observed outcomes. Countably many models are not the mismatch; the information/loss contract is. Fixed finite words do not remove full support. Signed, approximate or parameter-revealing scores, proper scoring rules and different feedback remain outside the obstruction. This is a source-attributed comparison, not a fresh audit or rejection of the external prediction literature.

## Remaining formal continuation developments and source ownership

### Concentration for the actual seeded raw tapes

[Read the retained statement](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/211febf4a1cf053563e4cefd1adc21a4778932bc6e39c9c414c09c9020e86853.md). For finite action and symbol alphabets, normalised nonnegative row laws and an independent probability seed, the proof starts with the actual product-tape law—not an assumed observation process. It bounds the event that some action/symbol empirical frequency at some deterministic prefix length `n ≥ N` deviates by at least `η`. For `0 < η ≤ 1` and `N ≥ 1`, its bound is

```text
|A| |Y| · 2 exp(−N η²/2)
────────────────────────
      1 − exp(−η²/2)
```

The [raw-prefix declaration](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/82b3e06719e80fe4e1f0a77fd72e157bfc30ef3b42992b3e6a5587b859404e3b.lean) passes through the exact finite-word pushforward before the countable cutoff union. The [unrestricted-threshold component](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/29942f882e2e223ddabd7bf19d1b4dbf5deb0ec3b7287c1868c315d505d46182.lean) uses `min(η,1)²/2` for arbitrary positive tolerance. This is a useful, explicitly non-sharp exponent; it is not advertised as sharp Hoeffding. Adaptive acquired-row counts do not become deterministic-prefix samples merely because their names resemble the raw indices. The source's [recorded concentration review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/1dd73154483e48f304d705067f869caad6d4cdca78ebb417a4261650f50cb6dd.md) remains separate from both the subsequent stopped-cost construction and a fresh replay.

### What an affine tail does and does not imply

[Read the retained statement](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/2d7ec3409db14cb075da9d60a8c0ebdcd564de32f3994a5e227fffacb31d4a42.md) and [formal tail-to-moment declarations](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/7f58b3c0270925b452c97ebeaaaa9383f7958f2c0c0b4fc6b578a7cde01ef4f7.lean). For a measurable extended-nonnegative cost `C`, a **supplied** tail `Pr(C > B + Vu) ≤ exp(−u)` for all `u ≥ 0`, with `V > 0`, yields finite expectation, almost-sure finiteness, positive exponential moments for `0 ≤ θ < 1/V`, and all natural-power moments. The actual coBüchi-count instantiation transports those conclusions to its named cost variable. It does not erase the tail hypothesis.

The proof first controls the extended integral; conversion to a real-valued cost occurs only after almost-sure finiteness has been established. Its displayed integer-envelope expectation estimate is deliberately conservative, not a claim of the sharp `B + V` bound. Boundary controls protect the open exponential range. The [existing review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/58500a16b7290bf5631b347fc94be113ebd7ec240e2169001761078f7392100c.md) and the later [actual-controller cost chain](#the-actual-generated-controller-has-finite-and-exponential-error-budgets) concern different steps: proving an actual tail is additional work, not a consequence of naming the abstract moment theorem.

### Recursive support and the same physical policy law

The [original recursive-equivalence declarations](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/fdd271bd99c4a7e1e79ddeba278e809ba9319936438fd4edb7c72e1bdecc426e.lean) connect three existence claims in one finite controlled-iid interface: an independently defined recursive support certificate, one lawful history policy with almost-sure eventual target goodness, and existence of one with finite expected total bad-action count. The common probability seed and policy are selected **before** quantifying the live hidden model. Fixed kernels may have zeros; the starting support must be nonempty. Public permission menus depend on current support, while target goodness is a different condition.

The [canonical sufficiency account](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/a90fd14b7987f39e21e16116ee532abc16b396f58f42b90523a6934646c91815.md) explains the positive implementation: finite action trees, first-exit truncation, residual-tree replay and a normalised potential pay the current physical action in the original canonical law. The [same-law necessity/equivalence review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/0bba51eceac85ed0c16234042f29f9f8167857f5db4f51009ba0a941b3e44748.md) explains common-seed selection, finite-query transfer, patching unused singular rows and the requirement to handle **all** feasible strict-support successors. A favourable single branch is not enough. Safety is asserted on feasible, action-compatible histories, not forged transcripts or impossible empty supports.

Finite expectation is an **existence equivalence**, not a property of every almost-sure winner. A different constructive policy can supply the finite budget. Good actions may continue gathering information forever. The theorem neither authenticates the public menu's normative authority nor implements every continuum scored-repair problem. Its exact formal sources and recorded review remain available without treating this reader curation as another kernel replay.

### Literal indexed observers and their scheduled runtime

[Read the literal-source invariant](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/98c1923c5c9ef77094ca28a63c32866fae56ae04b7a1597da59874935d9b1167.lean) and [scheduled-runtime equations](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/source-store/73b3cb0b813ff303a6735c3cf8ac3c8dbbc1ed5c5dd335d4e0c239b8ddb23cc3.lean). The observer state is exactly `(stage, acquired sentinel word)`, starting from `(0,1)`. A transition appends the current input bit and the output uses the preserved numeric-index evaluator at the correct stage. The schedule computes a sufficient finite stack-fuel budget for each code and finite horizon; at tick `k` it uses horizon `k+1`. Consequently the whole runtime output stream, and then its fair-input law, equal the literal indexed source semantics.

The [retained source/runtime review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/9e774f1b1e3366ca616619923df8a934b894bcb3886a69bab1cae1eca8298994.md) distinguishes malformed syntax or wrong arity, whose specified semantics defaults to zero, from an unfinished valid evaluation, which returns `none`. Default zero is not proof that the intended source ran. The schedule proves successful ticks before applying an optional-value projection; its fallback is not silently treated as a bit. A local simulation of the concrete splitter yields exact nonatomic defect `1/2`, rather than an estimate from finitely many outputs.

The [earlier observer-core review](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/8063ee424925df31d7bd8f6efb1f7d22c007fbb4e7723a66cd04902097b08550.md) keeps local synchronous and guarded simulation distinct from the later actual language implementation. No universal finite abstraction, single fuel bound for an infinite run, practical CPU bound, host/Python refinement or compiler for every P01 term follows. This is a source-owned research-software development with mathematical proof components, not a separate paper for each imported module.

### Sixth completion boundaries and subsequent research questions

The [Sixth handoff synthesis](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/dd653de8a16de0196c33109d11a2c447589d57f8a8cad4877c8383856c845a3a.md) preserves completed static disclosure and actual generated-controller endpoints, then asks four substantive questions: what warrants the existential step to a necessary particular source; what connects objective fittingness to essential truthful agency; how an integrity commitment can itself be warranted under a repair budget; and how a defective criterion can be corrected across recipients and versions. It supplies positive starting arguments, developed rivals, discriminating consequences and exact stopping points—not just a list of avenue names.

Its [fixed-baseline comparison](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/b2ae5609c728772b2d8a4fc7b7065d70ef604ada875c2b85eb66608a753511d9.md) requires that a claimed cross-avenue improvement actually consume its advertised source/norm premise. Removing an unused foundational premise must remove the claimed dependence, not the valid local result. This remains a **dated prospective handoff**, not proof that all four questions are still open after every later tranche, a new authority verdict or permission to start a research campaign. Later successor and T20 records retain their separately scoped advances.

### v5 continuation source, result and execution directory

The [collection guide](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/README.md) is the reading and execution reference for the First–Sixth returns and their final overlay. The [original registry](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-continuations/registry.json) owns the 68 scoped result identifiers, claims and limitations; [result status](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/RESULT_STATUS.json), [source aliases](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/SOURCE_MAP.json) and the [final overlay](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/SIXTH_FINAL_OVERLAY.json) retain status, original-member, version and selection authority. The reader crosswalk gives every declared result a work route without copying its verdict or minting another scientific ID.

This reference also gives an explicit **reported-result/availability route** for `T4-ENDPOINT`: the source owner credits recovered 59-byte incremental and 105-byte prefix programmes and their ordinary `(0,2)` cost, while the [recovery evidence account](../../docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/32266a84e5e96404970a7420dc690e71db78323281fd10805a899c6ca67b52e2.md) denies that source recovery restores vanished fourth-tranche Lean or audit evidence. Consult those retained source records for the exact supported bodies and procedures; this entry does not manufacture an executable from the brief registry claim.

All 1,200 collection paths now have explicit work/support/history dispositions in the [existing crosswalk](../project-closure/reader-refactor-issue-35/CROSSWALK.json). Shared Lean dependencies, tests, suite descriptors, receipts and repeated aliases route through this directory where no exclusive work attachment is warranted. They are not counted as additional papers, independent proofs or independent observations. [Reading and classification scope](../project-closure/reader-refactor-issue-35/COLLECTION-COVERAGE.md#complete-v5-continuation-disposition) states what was actually examined.

## Sixteenth renewal, certificates and supplied controllers

The retained companion has thirteen constituent papers and a final HasC theorem map. The entries below separate those papers from their programme report, empirical software and source-assurance reference. The existing [claim map][t16-claim-map] and [source index][t16-source-index] own their twenty-one result scopes and exact supporting objects; neither the shared volume nor each proof module gains separate paper credit.

### Finite certified renewal and effective selection of continuing lineages

[Read the constituent paper][t16-certified-renewal] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** The paper constructs a decidable rooted binary renewal tree with a uniformly computable certified plan for every requested finite horizon and a mathematical infinite lineage, but no single computable irrevocable infinite lineage. Its fixed authorised zero-input environment and delayed state-transfer semantics make this a renewal construction, not merely a list of unrelated finite objects. Permission revocation has its own orderly-stop behaviour.

The positive selection theorem is equally important: a computable path exists exactly when a rooted pruned computably enumerable subtree exists (equivalently, an appropriate decidable one). Pruning supplies an effectively discoverable successor at every stage. A width-two family has individually computable paths without a uniform selector; pointwise convergence of finite plans need not supply a computable stabilisation modulus or nested lineage. These distinctions do not cancel finite-horizon competence or assert that every practical renewal system fails. The family, dependency and coherent-selection hypotheses determine the contract.

### Randomised renewal in the explicit diagonal lineage

[Read the constituent paper][t16-private-random-renewal] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** In the particular explicit diagonal lineage, every computable irrevocable transducer using private fair coins has probability zero of generating an infinite admissible path. Partial-output tapes are allowed and count as failures. The argument exploits finite output cylinders rather than assuming a machine already supplies an infinite sequence on every tape.

This is a stronger property of the constructed tree, not a theorem that every computable tree without computable paths has measure-zero path set. Nor does it apply unchanged to noncomputable advice or externally informative observations. The later almost-sure paper in this volume gives the exact general probability-one equivalence under its own fixed-tree/private-randomness interface.

### A finite width population does not supply the missing renewal

[Read the constituent paper][t16-closed-generation-populations] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** A uniformly bounded-width population cannot complete every generation of the diagonal lineage when each generation is finite, closed once completed and parent-coherent. Private randomisation does not rescue that bounded closed-generation contract with positive probability in the explicit construction.

A positive contrast prevents overstatement: enumerating all nodes breadth first yields an unbounded-width service that completes every finite generation, although it selects no persistent infinite lineage. A bound on devices simultaneously active is not automatically a bound on a completed generation. Backtracking, reopened generations and checkpoint policies must therefore be analysed under their actual rules rather than silently imported into this theorem.

### What the continuation result says about preserving competence

[Read the constituent paper][t16-renewal-competence] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** This interpretative paper separates a certified finite plan, the availability of every further finite continuation, and one effectively maintained indefinite lineage. The finite successes are genuine. A finite three-generation promise does not need an infinite continuation theorem, while repeating finite evidence does not by itself produce a single coherent renewing policy.

The note does not refute a full invariant that already includes a warranted sustaining procedure and its continuing dependencies. Its application is a discipline for specifying what competence is preserved, over what horizon, with which active supplier and policy. It supplies an interpretation of the construction, not an additional theorem about all human cognition or personal identity.

### Every effective finite lookahead still leaves the selection boundary

[Read the constituent paper][t16-finite-lookahead] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** Any supplied total computable, finite-valued lookahead demand, even one growing with the entire history, can be installed as a decidable finite-admissibility condition without changing the diagonal tree’s infinite paths. The strengthened system retains arbitrarily long effective finite plans and still has no computable infinite selection.

The demand’s totality is an input premise, not a decision procedure for arbitrary supplied code. The lookahead witness concerns extensions in the original tree, not recursively certified viability in the strengthened tree. Thus arbitrarily large finite forecasts do not silently become an infinite supplier of coherent choices. The finite controller implementations retain their actual enumeration conventions rather than acquiring a different lexicographic algorithm from the prose.

### Deciding and certifying effective renewal policies

[Read the constituent paper][t16-policy-certification] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** In the specified syntax-total primitive-recursive tree representation, infinite-path existence is Π⁰₁-complete and computable-path existence is Σ⁰₃-complete, including the stated promise restriction. Validity of a supplied ordinary computable path or enumerated pruned witness has a different quantifier/totality burden. Consequently a sound effective finite certificate discipline cannot be complete for the unrestricted computable-policy existence question.

This does not erase the constructive restricted services: a supplied pruned witness supports extraction, and a bounded finite-graph problem can have a complete terminating solver. The classification depends on what the input language guarantees. Replacing its syntax-total presentation with arbitrary possibly non-total tree code changes the infinite-path index question. The source distinguishes the kernel-checked constructive reduction from the ordinary hierarchy arguments; a classification proof is not a practical runtime or an executable general selector.

### Unary equality conditionals and exact finite semantic identity

[Read the constituent paper][t16-unary-equality] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** For one natural-number input, natural constants, addition, multiplication and arbitrarily nested equality conditionals whose children stay in that grammar, the exact function class is an eventual natural-coefficient polynomial with finitely many exceptional values. A canonical polynomial/exception representation yields all-input equality checking and a concrete counterexample when equality fails.

The written sparse comparison and the checked dense root bound are distinct procedures; a sparse comparison bound assumes the exceptional maps have already been produced. The function “return 7 at input 2, otherwise return the input” is a genuine extension beyond the older restricted unary languages, without expressing unrestricted parity or truncated predecessor. The result is not multivariate semantic identity, recognition of arbitrary raw programs, or a formal parser theorem. The literal source compiler and the separate Python/JSON reference preserve their different representation contracts.

### Semantic completeness and current identity in the exact compiler image

[Read the constituent paper][t16-compiled-identity-obstruction] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** The exact source compiler maps the variable and its addition-by-zero variant to semantically equal, already typed endpoints, but the original all-rule `Has` and `HasPlus` calculi still lack any proof of the resulting internal identity. This is not just failure of the proposed proof `I`: conversion reflection and two distinct normal source terms exclude every candidate proof under the stated old rules.

Normal-form difference is relevant only with that conversion argument; arbitrary syntactic inequality is insufficient. The semantic equality checker and the relevant interpreted `F`/`G` claims remain positive. The paper distinguishes this exact compiler-image obstruction from a broader uninhabited-type claim and from separately introduced `HasE` or `HasC` judgements. It does not retroactively modify the old calculus to make the desired proof available.

### A sound full rule certificate extension for unary semantic identity

[Read the constituent paper][t16-certificate-extension] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** The separately named `HasC` extension retains the original two context, seven formula and eighteen typing constructors and adds a finite unary-identity certificate rule. The full mutual soundness result ranges over the represented formed contexts under the original `H`/`E` law hypotheses; existing derivations embed without turning this into a conservativity claim.

The exact completeness equivalence is **closed-context**: compiled unary equality, a successful checker, interpreted equality and existence of a `HasC` proof coincide there, with an explicit proof supplied in the positive direction. Under an assumed false identity, a nonempty context can contain its variable proof while lacking a satisfying `H` valuation, so the checker converse cannot be extended to all contexts. The separate final HasC theorem map documents this same paper, not a fourteenth article. `HasC`, `HasE` and other source calculi retain their individual rules and identities.

[Exact theorem map][t16-hasc-map] · [full formal scope][t16-core-scope].

### The time available to witness absence of a bearer

[Read the constituent paper][t16-temporal-witness] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** The paper studies one and the same bearer across its actual history. If possible absence must have an actual-time witness and the relevant actual presence must be preserved, the absence witness has to precede every actual presence. A bearer actually present at an attained first instant then satisfies the specified necessity criterion. A beginningless history can instead use the appropriate same-bearer coinitial presence premise.

The premise burden matters: strict-past preservation alone leaves an equality case; a finite age bound need not provide an attained first instant; some bearer at each time need not be the same bearer. The source’s rationale invokes anchored possibility and settled actuality, but its world application still requires those claims to be warranted. A necessary actual participant is not, solely thereby, the unique complete unreceived supplier of everything. This positive temporal route keeps its scope and is not a later blanket verdict on sourcehood.

### Almost sure private randomisation and effective committed continuation

[Read the constituent paper][t16-almost-sure-renewal] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** For a fixed rooted prefix-closed tree and effective irrevocable output with private fair coins, an almost-sure successful policy exists exactly when a computable path exists, equivalently when a suitable c.e. pruned subtree exists. The positive-probability finite-output prefixes can be enumerated and are viable and pruned under the probability-one hypothesis, yielding effective deterministic continuation.

A separate nested-cylinder construction also finds a computable successful tape for the original machine; it is not the claim that any fixed seed works. The sharp probability-one premise cannot be replaced by merely high success probability: counterexamples below one retain the no-computable-path obstruction. Randomness interacting with informative external observations, resource bounds or another game interface is not removed by this theorem.

### Finite signed certification and program producing synthesis

[Read the constituent paper][t16-signed-synthesis] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** On the stated finite hidden-static-model input with exact rational transitions and a fully observed state, a finite signed certification service enumerates the complete certificate-body domain and returns either an accepted body or a checkable structural refutation of every body. Source fields, labels, orders and unused nodes remain bound to the exact input; the reflected formula is not obtained by treating the final checker result as an unexplained atom.

For valid input with nonempty live support, the negative result excludes a common lawful almost-sure parity policy, using the inherited necessity theorem for the broader policy class. Invalid input and empty support are separate cases, not impossibility certificates. Finite termination is a mathematical guarantee, not a tractability claim; the recorded native enumeration covers the stated small domain. This work is the signed decision/synthesis interface. The following paper separately proves that its submitted positive body produces the promised actual policy.

[Separate signed/controller proof ownership][t16-controller-scope].

### Direct extraction from submitted finite parity certificates

[Read the constituent paper][t16-submitted-controller] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** The compiler consumes the literal accepted finite certificate body and produces a common history-dependent controller whose induced original history law is lawful and almost surely satisfies the parity objective for each live actual model. The common policy is fixed before that actual-model quantifier; no true-model oracle is supplied to it.

The proof concerns the controller actually returned by the submitted implementation, not merely existence of another successful controller. Its code is finite and each step terminates, while histories and counters can grow without a fixed bound. It is therefore not automatically a finite-state machine, a uniform deadline guarantee or transport of every older cost bound. The signed service and direct-controller source closure is separately qualified, with inherited modules shared rather than counted twice.

[Separate signed/controller proof ownership][t16-controller-scope].

### Orthemology Sixteenth Research Report

[Read the source][t16-report] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** The dated report relates the thirteen mathematical papers to the wider sourcehood, recipient-warrant, norm-fixing and competence questions. Its foundational assessment preserves affirmative reasoning about original productive adequacy and intrinsic acts while separating actual non-reception, essential independence, intrinsic necessity and identification of a common original source. A model that denies a granted complete supplier is not automatically a counterexample under that grant; application still owes warrant for the grant.

The occurrence section keeps complete source-relative readings separate from partial records and from textual-person variants; it does not license Cartesian recombination or claim a new critical edition. The empirical section distinguishes scoring outcomes, active-answer agreement and size-standardised comparisons, rather than inferring a causal mechanism from one aggregate score. Original bibliographic/source assessments and later scientific developments keep their own dates and scopes. The report’s three final document reviews are described in the source but their absent bodies are not treated as newly inspected evidence here.

### Sixteenth social-learning game reanalysis

[Read the source][t16-reanalysis] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Contribution.** This source-only package implements the Sixteenth game-log analyses, separate from the earlier Fifteenth reanalysis and from the research report describing its results. It provides a pinned environment, exact input-manifest checks, a replay entrypoint, analysis/contrast/sensitivity code and synthetic test fixtures. The two required external input files are identified rather than silently downloaded or republished; the output directory must meet the package’s fresh-output rules.

Its recorded results distinguish a game’s negative score from correctness among the active answerers and keep missing/tied roster information visible. The stated size-standardised contrasts and sensitivity ranges are descriptive estimands, not identified causal effects. The retained full-replay and independent-replay records refer to those historical executions. This curation did not rerun the analysis, reacquire participant inputs or upgrade synthetic smoke tests into empirical replication.

### Sixteenth proof recovery and signed-controller source assurance

[Read the source][t16-core] · [Original collection and evidence scope][t16-guide].

**Purpose.** This is the source-assurance and replay reference for the companion, not another mathematical paper. The preserved core contains 111 own-module sources, including inherited material; the signed/direct-controller closure is a separate 116-module closure with overlapping inherited dependencies. Those counts are not additive independent formal results.

The recovery guide distinguishes historical exact identities, recovered full fixture payloads without historical digest matches, a regenerated-and-reviewed fixture, and the newly derived audit controls. The formal scope retains ordinary written results alongside checked declarations and distinguishes them from the separate Python/JSON representation. Recorded compiler/library trust boundaries and source-build observations remain explicit. Inherited reviews are evidence with their own objects and dates, not fresh independent review of this refactor. Use the existing source index and claim map to recover the full component population rather than duplicating a scientific manifest in this catalogue.

[Core scope][t16-core-scope] · [fixture recovery][t16-recovery] · [Python/JSON boundary][t16-python-reference] · [signed/controller closure][t16-controller-scope].

## Seventeenth: foundational appraisal and finite assignment inference

These four works have different jobs: a dated philosophical appraisal, its source guide, a mathematical companion and a closed inquiry’s supporting software. The eleven original result scopes are not eleven independently authored papers. The governing [Seventeenth claim map](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t17-successor/CLAIM_MAP.json) retains their statements, assumptions, evidence and limitations.

### Seventeenth foundational research: original being, perfection and warranted uptake

**Contribution.** The 5 October 2026 report gives affirmative, defeasible rational appraisals of whole-existence original completion, an actual same-bearer Necessary Being, essential independence under its additional constitutive judgement, eligible standing perfections and source-owned veracity. These are not merely a conditional model with no defended world application. The report argues that an account wholly received throughout lacks original productive actuality, connects actual qualified resources to their bearer, and uses contingency-as-need to reach that witness’s necessary existence. It credits Sixth with the stronger rationale rather than inventing a new missing premise.

The distinctions remain substantive. Necessary existence or actual nonreceipt alone does not supply essential independence. The pure-perfection route extends beyond qualities already donated to creatures, but bare necessity does not yield unrestricted power. The report’s veracity conclusion is exceptionless for genuinely source-owned contextual assertions **if its defended same-bearer premises hold**; defeasibility concerns our warrant, not an exception silently inserted into the conclusion. Actual utterance, attribution, content, recipient warrant and rightful practical force still require their own support. The source preserves continuing contingency-to-need and veracity dissent while recording that the original-completion critic accepted the corrected argument. Those are not interchangeable review outcomes.

The report separately credits the cumulative common-Creator case but withholds its stronger exclusive-actual-coverage conclusion at this dated stage. That neither retracts its positive Necessary Being result nor establishes the possibility of plural ultimates. It distinguishes intrinsically possible objects within standing power from willing consistent with essential wisdom, and completed voluntary determination from uniqueness of the whole history. Later foundational works retain their own appraisals; this report is not their current master verdict.

Its positive epistemology matters directly: competent uptake of a warrantedly understood, undefeated ordinary report can warrant belief without prior independent speaker certification or a uniquely settled philosophy of testimony. Hidden defects can defeat knowledge without automatically cancelling the listener’s entitlement. Shared-source transmission can preserve warrant without creating extra independent roots. Objective creaturely fittingness and command-dependent reasons likewise support a qualified local direction without identifying every benefit with duty or force with unlimited authority.

### Guide to the six foundational sources and their interpretative relationships

**Contribution.** This source guide distinguishes six supplied versions, their source voices, positive metaphysical/epistemic arguments, page locators and concrete translation or editorial problems. Its role is interpretative navigation and source fidelity, not six newly authored Orthemology papers or a fresh claim by this refactor to have read all underlying books. The Asfahani translation, Oneness excerpt, Suleiman study, El-Tobgui dissertation, modern anthology and al-Ujayri defence are not interchangeable independent witnesses.

The guide preserves actual Creator-recognition through intact fiṭrah as a historical claim, distinct from Turner’s explicitly conditional modern proper-function construction. Basic cognition need not await a second testimonial licence: qualitative tawātur and functionally equivalent routes can corroborate or restore undermined cognition. Developing or repairing recognition is not the same as manufacturing truth from popularity, nor does lack of a uniquely agreed epistemological explanation automatically defeat sound cognition. The guide records whose primary and secondary voices support each interpretation.

Original giving and the broader pure-perfection argument are credited as distinct routes; standing attributes and their particular exercises, a required productive source and all-field common Creatorhood, and logical possibility and comparative evidential plausibility remain distinct. Its local translation and numerical corrections do not become a global verdict rejecting unrelated recognitional or necessity arguments. Source copies, anthology adaptations and a scholar’s repeated treatment of one primary tradition do not multiply independent corroboration.

The guide’s own 1,393-page reading/190-page visual snapshot remains inherited metadata. This curation reads the guide, not those six PDFs anew; the separately bounded Arabic checks are not a complete 938-page scan review or manuscript collation. External links retain their original access and redistribution boundaries.

### Finite inference under assignment uncertainty

**Contribution.** This single thirteen-section companion supplies ordinary finite proofs for a weighted assignment statistic when the joint assignment mechanism is incompletely specified. The sharp recorded-trajectory null—including availability and every ingredient frozen by the statistic—and a valid conditional assignment-law contract are **separate premises**. Marginally fair assignments can still be associated with potential responses; even a true no-effect null does not repair that omitted conditioning relation.

For a warranted family containing the actual conditional law, inclusive robust tail suprema give finite-sample-valid tests. On complement-closed support, fair marginals alone make the usual complement-invariant absolute statistic’s robust tail one. This is not universal futility of all inference under all models. The positive result is an exact Bellman/linear-programme recursion under rectangular full-prefix interval bounds, attaining its value through appropriate endpoint choices. Additional cross-history restrictions can destroy sharpness, while leaving a conservative outer bound. A partial sum may replace full history only when it retains the assignment state required by the contract.

The paper’s two-sign example distinguishes dependent and product optimisation: with each conditional plus probability in [1/4, 3/4], the matching-sign event has sharp sequential probability 3/4, versus at most 5/8 among independent coordinate-specific product laws. Directed tails under deterministic-coordinate bounds do have exact endpoint-product optimisers; adding the separately maximised opposite tails is generally only an upper bound. The paper supplies outward numerical enclosures, safe weight coarsening, bounded unfinished-subtree calculations and precise Monte Carlo conditions rather than treating an approximate best policy as a certified robust upper bound.

A second positive construction uses full joint-law invariance under every prespecified block flip. Conditional on an assignment orbit, a uniform inclusive rank test is valid without knowing the orbit probabilities. Symmetric block marginals or one common fair orientation are insufficient; finer blocks require stronger joint invariance. This family and finite full-prefix-bound families are not generally nested. The source credits classical finite-group testing, dynamic programming and concentration rather than claiming their historical invention.

The final reader is a derived projection of the final DOCX. The earlier `Mathematical_Companion_Content_v2.md` is retained as a reading/source form of the same mathematics: the source relationship record says the final DOCX added only authorship/date paragraphs, not a new result. Neither Markdown claims matching pagination, and neither a valid finite model nor its synthetic examples recovers an experiment’s actual assignment process.

Each group has equal opportunity weight; missing responses contribute no recorded directional evidence and are not imputed neutral beliefs. The initial conservative upper bounds equal one on the frozen sensitivity grid. The lower certificate depends on Tyurin’s non-identically distributed Berry–Esseen theorem with constant weakened to one, not on this package reproducing its proof. It does not lower-bound every individual law in the family, identify an exact robust tail, or establish invariance, equivalence, power or cognitive mechanism.

## Eighteenth: hidden change, actual controllers and source-specific foundations

### One hidden change: finite certificates and actual parity controllers

**Contribution.** The mathematical companion characterises actual almost-sure parity control when a finite stochastic system can undergo one irreversible hidden mode change. The controller sees physical states and its own actions, not the mode or departure-pair priority; the adversary can change mode after seeing the current action but cannot see a private seed or future randomness. Common nonempty lawful menus, the exact observation law and the minimum priority recurring infinitely often belong to the problem, not to a later convenience assumption.

Its positive theorem equates a lawful measurable private-seed winner, a finite accepted positive certificate, the compiled deterministic effective history controller, and membership in the greatest uncertain winning region. The known-mode and uncertain regions carry all-positive-successor safety as well as component/path witnesses; a path witnesses positive reachability, not control over the next random receipt. Negative certificates must exhibit the complete descending greatest-fixed-point traces, not simply an arbitrary smaller fixed point. Reduction from adaptive changes to fixed change indices uses trajectory coupling and a countable union of null events, not conditioning on a random change and assuming independence.

The literal controller keeps global departure counts, alternates candidate modes and advances a phase once on a rejection or component exit. The strict test gate `N_e > r` eventually ignores finitely sampled stale rows while allowing old observations in recurrent rows to be diluted. A stable wrong mode can still yield winning parity on its row-agreed recurrent component: success is not a guarantee that the hidden mode is eventually announced correctly. A finite program can require unbounded counters. Fixed-mode success alone is insufficient: in the two-state alternating/staying example, a single late change traps the process at an odd-priority state.

For explicit rational inputs, thresholding priorities before maximal-end-component decomposition gives a polynomial method; the source proves P-completeness at that encoded-input scope. Equal-row components with different priorities can need two different minimum witnesses. The efficient and exhaustive solvers must preserve the winning-state union, not identical component lists. This is an ordinary complexity result, not a Lean complexity theorem, a tight runtime formula or a deployment guarantee.

The representation results have different quantifiers. Every positive arbitrary-real instance has a nonuniform effective winner using finite exact masks and rational calibration advice; that advice cannot uniformly be recovered from arbitrary Cauchy names, even for the source’s rational-valued halting encoding. With **common priorities**, one deterministic support-only policy works for every numerical realisation of the trusted supports, without an equality mask or supplied lower probability bound. It uses one-sided rejection and a support-inclusion argument. With different hidden priorities, the swapped-Bernoulli example has the same supports and equality mask but no such common policy. Exact supports are inputs; finite observations do not certify a probability zero.

These results concern probability-one infinite parity against each legal adversary. Lawfulness and certificate-region closure are proved; the parity objective does not require avoiding odd priorities at every finite prefix. The results do not promise finite memory, a deadline, finite expected learning cost, arbitrary numbers of mode changes or a physically completed infinite execution. They are constructive mathematics with specified contracts, not a theorem identifying human cognition with this model.

### Eighteenth rational certificate-to-controller semantic endpoint

**Contribution.** `HiddenChangeSemanticEndpoint.lean` connects an accepted, fully bound positive body to the literal Lean controller’s measurability, common-menu lawfulness on all histories and winning on the actual one-change law. Its necessity theorem covers arbitrary lawful measurable private-seed policies on a probability space, not merely the compiler’s own policy class. The signed alternatives give mutually exclusive and exhaustive positive/negative outcomes at the stated finite-rational input and initial state.

The module’s `DeterministicWinner` is existential. The source does not turn that definition into an executable arbitrary-input selector for a positive body. Nor does the theorem authenticate an external parser, its bytes or a deployed binary. Lean permits some bounded-walk/repeated-trace bodies that the separate Python API canonicalises more strictly; no accepted-language correspondence is inferred. The ordinary arbitrary-real and P-completeness arguments are outside this module’s kernel claim.

### Eighteenth HasE transport and inhabited-endpoint countercontrols

**Contribution.** The retained HasE controls test proposed proof methods without changing Has, HasPlus, HasC or HasE. The U-domain control uses a hypothetical inhabitant of a polymorphic type inside the actual typing context; it does not produce a closed inhabitant of that type or a new raw-endpoint coercion. The inhabited-endpoint module then applies the retained dependent J rule to exact closed functions at `N → N`, where the final type is itself inhabited. It establishes the literal H body, endpoint/proof telescope coordinates, formation premises and transported identity.

The separate de Bruijn reduction diagnostic supports an ordinary beta–eta distinction for these exported endpoints; that claim is not itself a Lean theorem. Together these controls defeat a proposed global erased-equality invariant and a final-type-only repair of it. Failure of those methods is not an all-rule noninhabitation proof. The endpoints are not shown to be in the literal unary compiler’s image.

At this Eighteenth stage, the frozen compiler `x/x+0` HasE identity remains open, while the older Has/HasPlus obstruction and separately named HasC result retain their scopes. The current result of a later investigation must be read from its later source, not overwritten by this dated open label. The retained review also explains why changing a negative-control target to a reflexive one cannot justify a broad uninhabitation claim: that reflexive target has an explicit positive witness.

### Exact hidden-change implementations and recovered-composite qualification

**Contribution.** The package supplies an exact rational reference solver, an efficient threshold/MEC solver, canonical positive/negative body checkers and a history-controller interface. A resource-cap or model-validation error is not a negative scientific answer. Positive paths and complete negative traces have explicit canonical forms. Off-policy histories, unreachable memory and a reached controller state are different interfaces; the runner’s fallback is not evidence that an arbitrary supplied history actually occurred.

The efficient implementation is checked against the reference at declared finite populations, including forged-negative-body controls. The one-hidden-change and HasE Lean closures remain separately scoped, with exact sources and trust boundaries linked by the package. Its historical prepared-environment limits coexist with the outer package’s separately qualified source-build/cache acquisition recipes. The HasE source-build supplement labels its derived copy instead of rewriting the old metadata. Official external caches are not fresh external source builds. Repeated copied files, test-event counts and reused dependency modules are not independent discoveries or experimental populations.

The code-evidence summary retains actual earlier packaging failures and excludes elaboration-stuck or diagnostic-mismatch attempts from credited semantic controls. ### Eighteenth foundational appraisal and the closed noncausal extension

**Contribution.** The dated report retains affirmative arguments concerning Necessary Being, essential independence, actual sourcehood, eligible standing perfection and warranted uptake. Its conditions remain substantive: standing power is not automatically exclusive actual origination; assertion, actual revelation, recipient warrant and directive authority are not identical. The Eighteenth control problem makes continuation and implementation contracts sharper without supplying additional metaphysical premises by itself.

The original unity discussion already addresses consented assistance, agreement and foreknowledge. Its surviving dispute concerns whether the relevant cooperation would externally complete a supposed original source’s efficacy, not whether a reply was absent. The report retains positive unity support while recording that dissent. An unresolved objection is not a construction of several ultimate sources or proof of equal credibility, and a dated restriction does not erase later affirmative research.

The report closes an attempted **unrestricted noncausal extension** as unearned. A true representation does not itself supply the represented actuality; the proposed exhaustive step assigning every objective fact to representation or a suitably qualified power-bearing source is not established there. This is a specific extension boundary, not a retraction of all preceding productive-source or cognitive-adequacy arguments. Its twelve-avenue discussion links those foundations to the new controller and representation results with their actual hypotheses.

### Eighteenth source and validation guide

**Role.** This guide connects the report’s six foundational sources, the carried Seventeenth appraisal, the bounded Eighteenth rereading, the one-change mathematical companion and the component-specific software/formal evidence. It separates an original author’s position, an editor or translator’s contribution, and the research programme’s reconstruction. Its historical page and visual-check counts are descriptions of prior work, not new source authentication by this refactor.

The guide identifies what the current arguments rely on and what they do not establish. Its bibliographic screens do not turn every reference into a fully read proof or recursively expand this repository’s corpus. The recorded access failure for one external item is retained; the self-contained complexity argument is not silently replaced by that unavailable source. The guide has a useful reference disposition without duplicate theorem or paper credit.

## Nineteenth common-source appraisal and source retrieval

### Common original provision and warranted attestation

Read the [complete Nineteenth research report](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/ca551178b117da992aa2de5c46bba14499d394125ba550626703b30a753caf3f/Orthemology_Nineteenth_Research_Final_v1_20261006.md), its [present judgement](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/ca551178b117da992aa2de5c46bba14499d394125ba550626703b30a753caf3f/Orthemology_Nineteenth_Research_Final_v1_20261006.md#the-present-judgement) and the [original eight-result owner](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t19-successor/README.md). This is the dated **affirmative sufficient-defeasible-warrant appraisal** of one complete original source of the warranted actual productively connected field. It is not merely a compatibility example, and not an unrestricted uniqueness theorem extracted from the phrase “necessary being”.

The [positive causal rationale](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/ca551178b117da992aa2de5c46bba14499d394125ba550626703b30a753caf3f/Orthemology_Nineteenth_Research_Final_v1_20261006.md#the-positive-causal-rationale) distinguishes differentiated real contributions from alleged concurrent duplication of the same entire original production in the same respect. Causal nonduplication is defended through productive individuation, not inferred just from a contradiction that already assumes production by one agent alone. A separate source-mode premise evaluates adequate unborrowed provision as the fitting mode of a qualified perfect original's actual agency. The report owns this evaluative extension; standing ability alone neither proves deficient exercise nor vindicates every chosen role. The [preserved causal grounds and dissent](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/2f60ec3cdb8a99651d9343d8543d06c9248eef3bf175f97b97dc5be2d7889aaa/causal_nonduplication.json) and [source-mode/field record](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/92f457f800082152fa932642ed72035c3033ee729d15a1994d3edcd4477b9b90/source_mode_and_field.json) make both premise burdens inspectable.

[Local coverage propagates through genuine productive connections](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/ca551178b117da992aa2de5c46bba14499d394125ba550626703b30a753caf3f/Orthemology_Nineteenth_Research_Final_v1_20261006.md#from-local-coverage-to-the-connected-field), retaining original contribution through real subordinate agency. Connectedness does not supply the local completeness norm; agreement, covariance or common knowledge does not itself constitute a productive crossing. An infinite history is not excluded merely for having no first temporal event. Common original provision does not erase a creature's proximal act or make every creature's assertion the source's own speech. The report retains the distinction between inseparable standing perfections, an original mode, an activity kind and a timely token act.

The [positive intentional-attestation route](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/ca551178b117da992aa2de5c46bba14499d394125ba550626703b30a753caf3f/Orthemology_Nineteenth_Research_Final_v1_20261006.md#a-positive-route-with-independent-evidential-content) concerns discriminating message-bearing organisation, control, correction of misattribution and differently vulnerable evidence in its actual context. These are not universal checkboxes or a demand to exclude every imaginable alternative. Universal creation, a benefit after a request and a challenge without opposition do not automatically identify source adoption. The al-Walīd analysis retains its positive reception-and-hostile-strategy evidence without inventing a recorded failed composing trial or making that bounded case a general verdict on inimitability.

[Avenue 3's obligation distinction](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/ca551178b117da992aa2de5c46bba14499d394125ba550626703b30a753caf3f/Orthemology_Nineteenth_Research_Final_v1_20261006.md#avenue-3-and-rightful-guidance) is consequential: an independently authenticated, determinate assertion that a recipient has an obligation can warrant that normative proposition under the same source's relevant knowledge and owned veracity. It need not itself create the duty or await a second independent proof of that very duty. A bare imperative, however, is not thereby shown obligatory, and the token cannot bootstrap its own source attribution. Recipient, time, content, jurisdiction where constitutive force is claimed, and operational permissions remain distinct.

The report preserves reasoned dissent on its causal and evaluative premises without converting dissent into an established plural-original countermodel. Its affirmative conclusion is field-scoped; actual revelation authentication, all necessary realities beyond the field, every earlier modal bridge and operational deployment are not settled by it. Its exact 6 October repository and reading claims are historical. Later T20 appraisals must be read at their own sources rather than silently replacing either judgement.

## Twentieth exact identities, modal scope and grounded support

These are distinct source-owned developments. Their original theorem, review and interpretation scopes remain attached; neither a shared tranche nor a successful integration gives them one blanket scientific status.

### Generic Church probe and an inhabited extensional endpoint

**Contribution.** In the unchanged current `Has` rules, a generic Church-carrier input `n` has the exact Raw identity `n I I = I`. The work then types the literal functions `F = λn. n I I` and `G = λn. I` at `N → Raw`, and derives their closed inhabited `HasE` identity using the inherited dependent J rule and the separate typed extensionality rule. This is a concrete positive Church-input result, not an empty-domain equality.

The source records scoped checking of all 46 theorem declarations and a separate trust-zero review. That formal evidence remains affirmative even though this refactor performs no new compilation. The underlying proof-coordinate mechanism is inherited. Ordinary erased beta–eta distinction is not promoted to a kernel theorem.

The endpoint is `N → Raw`, not the literal compiler’s `N → N` question. Carrier promotion and current-Has promotion require missing premises; deliberate statement mismatches do not prove noninhabitation. Even independent numeral counts can satisfy the unit probe, so it supplies neither full generic uniformity nor the missing projection-fusion law. Read the scalar-fusion successor for the exact remaining compiler obligation.

**Contribution.** Keep the inherited compiled terms `p` and `q`, with `p = I` as an equality of syntax and `q` the literal compiled `x+0`. ### After fixed witnesses: three tested negative routes

**Contribution.** This written follow-up tests what the fixed-witness reduction really permits. First, a constant normal witness does not justify introduction-only inversion: the actual J derivation can erase the evidence subterm while retaining the evidence-dependent target type. Restoring that type after inversion is the unsolved step, not a syntactic formality.

Two concrete semantic candidates use one shared application operation and pass the generic unit probe. They would separate the compiler endpoints, but fail the extensional arrow relation at a carrier formed from Raw and its identity type. A definedness-tag repair would invalidate the actual extensionality interface; collapsing the separating Raw values removes the displayed separation instead.

These are discriminating failures of proposed methods. They neither prove all possible models impossible nor supply a target inhabitant, a whole-rule separator or a decision procedure. The original result owner registers this written-method outcome as `T20-HASE-FURTHER-FAILED-ROUTES`; that registration does not turn it into a proof of noninhabitation or a Lean execution. ### Causal nonduplication and retained productive originality

**Contribution.** The productive route has a valid conditional form. Suppose the same particular `g` retains an entire original productive contribution in every relevant alternative. If whole productive reception of `g` would supply a distinct source of that **same contribution in the same respect**, causal nonduplication excludes such reception. The effect and respect may vary between alternatives; no one transworld act token or efficient self-production is required.

The scope inquiry locates the substantive modal work in productive-role retention and complete matching attribution. Necessary existence, standing power and even actual exercised derived efficacy do not by themselves preserve original provenance. A participant in a productive account cannot silently become the complete matching producer required by the proof.

The source expressly preserves T17’s positive warrant for the Necessary Being and essential independence. A proposed extra argument’s failure to improve that warrant is not its retraction. The finite role-switch assignment omits T17’s constitutive premise; it is not a countermodel to the full accepted premise set or evidence of a metaphysically possible rival. Pure production and a wider ontological reading of reception also require an explicit typing bridge.

### Grounded derivations, retraction and late source-root identification

**Contribution.** A conclusion remains finitely derivable after removing available roots exactly when at least one inclusion-minimal original support survives. Alternative derivations are retained as alternatives rather than flattened into one necessary conjunction. An unanchored endorsement cycle supplies no finite derivation, while an anchored cyclic rule graph can be useful. Truth follows conditionally from sound primitives and truth-preserving rules; losing the represented derivations does not itself make a historical truth false.

A further exact theorem addresses a source map learned later: archive **all inclusion-minimal** label cuts before the map is known, then map and minimise to recover every minimal root cut. Keeping only cheapest label cuts can lose the cheapest root cut. For supports `{a₁}`, `{a₂,b}`, `{a₃,b}`, the cheapest label cut `{a₁,b}` maps to two roots, whereas the larger minimal cut `{a₁,a₂,a₃}` maps to the single root `a`.

The occurrence controls distinguish evidential invalidation, prospective grant revocation and present applicability. A historic obligation can remain derivable after a live issuing grant is removed. The recorded formal review and bounded independent integer controls support their stated results, not actual authority or source authentication. General Python-to-Lean refinement and arbitrary nonmonotone or infinite continuation remain outside the theorem. The initial design’s future tense is historical; the later theorem map and review provide the completed formal scope.

## Twentieth same-bearer composition, observation and source-transfer audit

### From an original resource to the same necessary bearer

**Why read it:** [the typed bridge](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/930d562958832a8cdc4001145092565d31d7efa2ba67e20e39b83ebac0f6e138/README.md#result) makes the resource-to-bearer step explicit rather than hiding an original bearer inside the definition of an original resource. An actual received target has an original resource through positive completion; realisation supplies its intrinsic bearer; same-respect support transport makes receipt of that bearer incompatible with originality of that very resource. The subsequent necessity and essential-nonreceipt steps retain the **same** bearer and reuse the existing SourceIdentity theorems.

The [seven-premise contract](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/05a520f8160b28af5c8d90f3045424288c601e585344a09f71f7dc305dffd6be/DESIGN_AND_PLAN.md#exact-semantic-contract) separates target occurrence, completion, realisation, support transport, binary receipt representation, contingency-as-need and constitutive reception. A needs no modal assumption or binary representation. Nonreceipt wherever the bearer exists is narrower than necessary existence and does not itself need necessity; the inherited binary UniformRoot conclusion still needs its adapter. Infinite acyclic deletion controls distinguish local supply from whole original completion without relying on finite cycles.

The [retained independent component review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/6fc6b49f9999f78392548b0095bb865c06da4ea49d9fe00257c636e967dd53cc/REVIEW.md) and selected proof/readback files record scoped verification. Positive philosophical appraisal remains at its original source owner, not replaced by the logical controls.

### A fixed source across a connected productive field

**Why read it:** the [anchored incidence construction](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/0ff5b6519190744335ab79a4d5007f863bf49b54ecb35f671801871d7af8fbad/README.md#main-inference) carries one fixed source through a field instead of proving unrelated existential witnesses at each occurrence. Independent qualification, defect classification and actual conformance supply complete provision at its relevant roles. Sound actual use and role applicability propagate that coverage along genuine shared-contribution paths from an actual anchor.

### Copied deletion traces and what fresh observations add

**Why read it:** the [complete two-word calculation](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/ab2729c1927d164129161f785e1eaa3f4b71c6e32b2eb94f93d17c4cc3f06598/MODEL_DERIVATION_AND_PROPOSAL.md#3-exact-two-word-experiment) makes repeated access and new information quantitatively different. At retention probability 1/4, traces of 00 and 01 have total-variation distance 1/4. Any positive number of copies of **one realised trace** preserves that distance, so optimal equal-prior discrimination remains 5/8. Zero observations give 1/2, not the positive-copy formula.

For fresh independent masks of the same fixed word, the written calculation gives total variation `1-(1-p)^M` and optimal equal-prior success `1-(1-p)^M/2`. Sharing the original word is intended conditional sampling, not itself evidence of dependence. Copies can improve availability and fault tolerance without adding observational content. Equal encoded content with different source labels still has identical content-channel laws; even known 00 leaves two equally weighted position masks when the trace is a single zero.

The [implementation and later review boundary](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a0f78f8aa5b928561fd533e9084a0d3e18e9c7ce3775504e5ebdfd06ffeea03a/README.md#verified-scope) and [independent application review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/73d79019e20da7fcf602d64f01e664a8c927d9dc664ff2fb7894c0a27b94e723/REVIEW.md) preserve the finite Lean proof/attainment credit while leaving the all-M fresh-product contrast as written mathematics. This is an application of inherited H-ICS/T9 information principles, not a new general decoder or upstream replay. Faithful encoding, channel law, sampling resources, attribution, truth and authority still need their own evidence; content recovery is not authentication.
