### Cooperation, complete efficacy and the later common-source reassessment

The [source reconstruction](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/3ce900f8e6720713d4d4767df95e86b0ca31e30e9054547604c0f762dced5b05/SOURCE_ARGUMENT.md#4-strongest-source-faithful-reconstruction) is an affirmative argument about complete original efficacy, not merely a prediction that two agents would disagree. It first excludes reciprocal provision of the powers needed to provide anything. It then examines equal whole producers, necessary agreement, differentiated assistance and coalescing effects; the actual connected field carries the resulting local exclusion across related contributions. The strength of “complete” power and actual determination matters: retaining an unused ability while changing the undertaking does not preserve the source's fixed comparison.

Its two explicit further bridges distinguish **B6**, outcome attainment from exclusive original production of the same numerical effect, and **B5**, possible concentration of two power profiles from either already identified bearer's eligibility for that concentration and consequent dependence. The source argues for those transitions through productive perfection. The [model and inference audit](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/aad1327aa577fa6edb2b90477481cd2849b6ffe1701c27a3ddfdec69d15b8e67/MODEL_AUDIT.md#5-premise-preservation-matrix) explains why the existing finite controls do not preserve the entire intended upstream package. That is not a proof that every bridge is true. When P2b or another bridge is the conclusion being tested, assuming it among its own independent antecedents would make the test circular.

The [dated later reassessment](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/3f049a92d1da97dcbd2b4e8126d1c566543138ad274cb9334d5e9ea759b3033c/ROOT_REASSESSMENT.md#present-judgement) retains the author's provisional acceptance of at least one actual Necessary Being and one complete original source of the relevant actual connected created order, while withholding universal SourceMode. Its positive reasons concern complete original provision and integrated agency; failure to produce a countermodel is not their substitute. The [retained bounded warrant review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/f75458925a91eba498182217fe4b045349d23591c98433210783e54b136d99fe/REVIEW.md#7-what-follows-for-one-actual-common-original-source) separates conditional deduction, unrestricted metaphysical warrant and defeasible actual-field assent. This is inherited research review, **not independent C05 review of this refactor**, and does not certify the entire divine-action source campaign.

Read this beside the [earlier synthesis](#the-dated-foundational-audit-and-its-conditional-achievements), not as permission to rewrite it. The [current result owner](../provenance/v5-successors/RESULT_STATUS.json) and [complete prior appraisal history](../provenance/v5-successors/T20_CONTEXT_REAPPRAISAL_HISTORY.json) retain the scientific change boundary. ### Typed emergence: fresh acts, created effects and a stipulated activity gap

The [ontology/type audit](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/e9e55a70a8a84323d76514f095227067a7b962f6c718daa6e75ce2b4dbf83e05/AUDIT.md#checked-formal-control-and-limits) and [original formal construction](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/e3e5480db5b874448b7f4448ce6bce66e426995afbcb45e31ce61f755e03fdfe/OriginationTypeControl.lean) provide a positive, explicitly chosen interpretation. Indexed worlds distinguish one ground, act-tokens, effects and another emergent kind. Existence is a world-relative predicate; a kind or capacity is not an extra individual. Fresh-act witnesses include actual existence at the later index and exclusion of that same token at all earlier indices. The construction proves renewable fresh-act possibility, distinguishes one absent kind from total absence of emergent activity, and represents an act producing a created effect without itself satisfying the model's Created or ExternalReceipt predicate.

The modal accessibility relation and actual trajectory are separate inputs. The chosen actual path has a total gap followed by a new act, while each occurring emergent token permanently ceases. Exactly one stipulated object is necessary across these model worlds. None of this proves metaphysical possibility, real-world uniqueness, a source-author's endorsement of total inactivity, or a universal theory of dependence. Applying a whole-bearer contingency/receipt premise to every temporally new intrinsic act needs an additional typed application argument; no earlier conditional theorem is invalidated by this control.

The [original author execution receipt](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/4690dbd95f752e80c1eed8cff5788b73088893493cc802acc18ffe32921dba1c/EXECUTION_RECEIPT.json) and [retained model review](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/3afc19f45c2b17f175acb36672352c64ef8623b4179af095c4fb3aadf9033a86/REVIEW.md) preserve the recorded Lean 4.19.0 checking and rejected overclaims at their actual scopes. This navigation work performs no fresh kernel replay. The [late result map](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/LATE_RESULT_MAP.json) records **T20-EMERGENCE-TYPE-CONTROL** once; its later [historical application qualification](#divine-action-source-audits-and-the-stronger-activity-premise) must not be erased by a model-level PASS. Both layers remain reachable through the [finite context/source lookup](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/CONTEXT_SOURCE_INDEX.json).

#### Late evidence handoff for the typed emergence control

The [7 October, 16:31 successor note](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a1c8ae6e7ae4cc8a8e4b1ebe166b2a273a5bd8842c3f56c904b863a346e5ffcd/RESULT_EVIDENCE_SUCCESSOR_NOTE_v2.md#what-changed) is an additive documentary continuation of the [original fifteen-result handoff](#the-original-fifteen-result-evidence-and-method-handoff). Its [v2 ledger](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/6b1e3b7a3ce32842eda6deada3c5bad3ef740215dba77b2f7750d0de51ba333b/RESULT_EVIDENCE_LEDGER_v2.json) preserves those fifteen result rows unchanged and adds the already-owned [typed emergence control](#typed-emergence-fresh-acts-created-effects-and-a-stipulated-activity-gap). These four successor documents support this existing research note; they are neither another mathematical work nor replacement primary reading forms. Their distinctive contribution is `OTC-X01`: a documented owner correction, source return, typed interpretation and review chain, together with an explicit guard on applying older conditional results.

**What the application guard establishes.** The note connects the model's exact distinctions to a concrete inference that needs another premise. An intrinsic act can be temporally new, inhere in its bearer and produce a created effect without itself satisfying the chosen `Created` or `ExternalReceipt` predicates. Applying whole-bearer `ContingencyNeed` to that act therefore requires B-admission and whole-existence applicability; temporal novelty alone does not supply them. The interpretation is indexed by natural-number worlds and object identities, not a finite model merely because its implementation is short. Ground existence, power, accessibility and the actual trajectory remain stipulated inputs. Modal fresh-act availability and an actual activity gap answer different questions; G-empty is weaker than all-emergent-empty, and the auxiliary kind's actual cessation case is vacuous because that kind never occurs on the chosen trajectory.

The ledger's application overlay names five existing conditional owners. It reports no defect in their inspected historical proofs, no old-row mutation and no replay of their science. Follow those owners for their positive results and original premises:

**Scientific evidence and receiving state.** The original record credits a 152-line Lean 4.19.0 construction at trust zero, nine named author theorems, seven selected author axiom readbacks, and two substantive false-overclaim rejections. The independent reviewer rebuilt the same source, reproduced the seven original readbacks and checked seven additional probes. No upstream mathematical theorem transfer or new reading of the six Drive objects is asserted. The successor assembly and this reader curation run none of those scientific checks or primary readings again.

At the [captured receiving boundary](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a1c8ae6e7ae4cc8a8e4b1ebe166b2a273a5bd8842c3f56c904b863a346e5ffcd/RESULT_EVIDENCE_SUCCESSOR_NOTE_v2.md#evidence-and-receiving-boundary), there are **sixteen ledger rows: fifteen registered T20 owners and one proposed owner**, with admission pending. The present receiving row is `CANDIDATE`, with `fresh_evidence: NOT_RUN` and count `0`; that does not erase the original checked work. The historical 261-result registry hash in the companion is not the current registry's byte identity, and the original inventory counts are not current catalogue totals.

**Identity and limits.** [SOURCE_BINDINGS_v2.json](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/30019626dd1d708c0ce1e77d27311e640354e7fb983badd78d52f7cb1de614e6/SOURCE_BINDINGS_v2.json) separates original inputs from included public projections. Its 23 late input bindings comprise 21 included-projection records resolving to nineteen distinct paths, because two pairs of identical logs share payloads, plus two omitted original review inputs. The separately hashed reviewer definition and parent-binding records have no included bodies; the related public review is not their byte-equivalent substitute. Only two author metadata line-span endpoints were corrected; initial metadata, scientific source and execution identities remain preserved. [VALIDATION_v2.json](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/1ffc2e72f4e4ec8b8c1b7cef9e095d11320012c69ec32bf07487511d09e3c11c/VALIDATION_v2.json) records documentary checks, including fifteen-row preservation, eighteen author-manifest members, 22 definition/source identities and eight final-review bound files. Its PASS supplies no new reading, replay or native admission. The note is an exact public body and the JSON companions are derived projections, selected by the [original context index](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/CONTEXT_SOURCE_INDEX.json); original author hashes must not be used as public-body hashes.

This successor leaves the dated 14:34 appraisal unchanged and does not certify actual metaphysical uniqueness, cosmology or total-cessation doctrine. The [later common-source reassessment](#cooperation-complete-efficacy-and-the-later-common-source-reassessment) and [subsequent divine-action application qualification](#divine-action-source-audits-and-the-stronger-activity-premise) remain separate routes; a model-level PASS cannot erase the stronger historical activity premise. The source also leaves report and source-guide successors independently coordinated. Use the [verification route](VERIFICATION-GUIDE.md#checking-the-late-result-evidence-handoff) to keep document lineage, original scientific credit, captured admission and later application limits distinct.

### Divine-action source audits and the stronger activity premise

The [causal-action contextual audit](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/3f82732faadc37cca6de77a1c1b65983c11568dddbd1990a84e7d182571a7e3b/LOCUS_AUDIT.md#audit-b-divine-action-and-emergence) and [Ibn ʿUthaymīn locus audit](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/3deb84870b2d2de2da472946031d2886e06d03efbc76e9e2b6c6ff968e738b50/LOCUS_AUDIT.md#result-and-evidential-boundary) explain why essential power, activity generally, a particular kind, an act-token and its created effect cannot be exchanged in an application. They report positive source arguments against reducing every renewed intrinsic act to a separated creature or an externally acquired perfection. They also identify stronger premises than enduring capacity alone: the later commentary's perfection-of-activity argument and the earlier causal argument against total stripping of successive voluntary activity followed by an unexplained restart.

Consequently, the typed control's globally inactive interpretation remains a valid reduced-assumption separation, but it does **not** preserve that fuller always-active doctrine. General activity does not imply that every kind or token is perpetually actual. One token ending is not global inactivity, and modal availability alone does not establish an actual continuous trajectory. This is an application and historical-scope distinction, not a retraction of the checked formal construction.

The [edition/page correspondence](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/054c0d46bfa050d5146664154b73dc0777afae16af6d8d7b4d3325505c6fb101/PAGE_CORRESPONDENCE.json) and [dated transcription receipts](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/763a7bbaf28a7bc2f06af67738aa6c9f3abeeaa68ba65c36db1181a4f9acc2c7/READING_RECEIPTS.json) retain witness-level limits: electronically bound edition pages differ from scan observations; the commentary's web section times are not independently listened-to quotation timestamps. These public analyses are not the withheld witness bodies. No fresh authentication, audio inspection, new theorem or independent refactor review is claimed. The [finite context/source lookup](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/CONTEXT_SOURCE_INDEX.json) links the exact selected records and keeps their role distinct from the model's original proof evidence.

## Later Twentieth reading methodology

### Arabic reading methodology and source-formal feedback

The [Arabic reading method](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/1aedc48f3e7507cb0f14921fcd0905c1f7a89137ed8cabb5450a316903635219/ARABIC_READING_METHOD.md#twelve-premise-checks) and [eleven feedback cases](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/182829543432a0f771f552f74cb3bc2896e5b7e536c0e93bac231b0a3935aef8/CROSS_STREAM_CASES.md) form one retrospective methodological reference. They explain how existing Arabic study informed twelve premise topics, how formal models exposed ambiguities, and what later returns could change. The topics run from Necessary Being and original sourcehood through agency, wisdom, veracity and attestation to testimony, copying and continuation. The four JSON companions support this account; they are not additional papers or theorem owners.

The [verification guide](VERIFICATION-GUIDE.md#checking-what-a-source-reading-receipt-establishes) explains how first reading, local returns and retrospective synthesis carry different evidence. This methodological supplement adds no new primary-reading or proof-replay credit.

The cases also make substantive differences visible. [AR-X07](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/182829543432a0f771f552f74cb3bc2896e5b7e536c0e93bac231b0a3935aef8/CROSS_STREAM_CASES.md#ar-x07-locate-the-veracity-objection-in-the-fittingness-premise) locates the rescue or overriding-good objection at the premise excluding a fitting exercise with a declarative shortcoming. It distinguishes that normative premise from recognising a shortcoming and from actual conformance; its Arabic alignment with the existing [veracity decomposition](#owned-token-truth-and-the-separate-veracity-premises) is explicitly retrospective. [AR-X05](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/182829543432a0f771f552f74cb3bc2896e5b7e536c0e93bac231b0a3935aef8/CROSS_STREAM_CASES.md#ar-x05-use-signs-and-delegated-acts-at-their-actual-scope) distinguishes an effect's origin, intentional sign use, certified mission and the content of later speech. It records earlier primary checks while keeping the specialised token transfer unimplemented. Neither case earns a new theorem through a useful comparison.

For a mathematical example, [AR-X06](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/182829543432a0f771f552f74cb3bc2896e5b7e536c0e93bac231b0a3935aef8/CROSS_STREAM_CASES.md#ar-x06-repeated-traces-do-not-identify-their-source-or-occurrence) connects the speech/carrier distinction to the interpretation of [copied observations](#copied-deletion-traces-and-what-fresh-observations-add). Its Arabic link is interpretative, not a premise or historical derivation of the finite statistical theorem. The [handoff rule](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/182829543432a0f771f552f74cb3bc2896e5b7e536c0e93bac231b0a3935aef8/CROSS_STREAM_CASES.md#handoff-rule) preserves original proof ownership and permits a pure mathematical result to have no Arabic dependency. Source-guided design, subsequent contextual checks and retrospective alignment must retain their separately documented chronologies.

**Inspect the support:** [Method/phase and topic records](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/f7171879244b4660b547bcb75bb3b1c5007c570cc4403050636452744535ff04/ARABIC_READING_METHOD.json) · [Case chronology and original result endpoints](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/a9bbeb51d33e7e60c82a8228582502e6e40aabc559f95b77fdac3e9826d98597/CROSS_STREAM_CASES.json) · [Typed source bindings](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c42bc9ed720bc810df050b38592d980a265e312098ba0356c77a8bea51daf062/SOURCE_BINDINGS.json) · [Original author validation summary](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/3a6e8f9587b050f8093dc48d15cc468b41b3c5a9afbfe5e167ad22707926f274/VALIDATION.json) · [Existing reading-index projection](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/95f8c77ed7ebd217a5108669ad911ee00a5e92d17c7aab641c8f1f10a7ddf998/READING_INDEX.json) · [Finite context/source lookup](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/CONTEXT_SOURCE_INDEX.json). The bindings identify original records and separately selected projections; they do not include every bound original body. The appraisal called current inside this supplement is the retained **7 October 2026, 14:34 UTC** assessment, unchanged by its methodological audit. The [later cooperation reassessment](#cooperation-complete-efficacy-and-the-later-common-source-reassessment) has its own source and history. ### Six supplied Drive inputs, inherited reading and T20 use

The [Drive-corpus methodology account](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/866e0d85f3524013ce7e5e6df1907fe184139259028d6e9b4ded91379893f0fa/DRIVE_CORPUS.md#what-was-actually-read-and-revisited) reconciles six exact supplied English PDF objects with their inherited reading records and later research uses. Its five public components form one supporting reference: the narrative, a structured corpus ledger, inherited coverage, source bindings and an original verification receipt. The [existing Seventeenth source guide](#guide-to-the-six-foundational-sources-and-their-interpretative-relationships) retains the original reading and interpretative account. Neither this methodological audit nor this reader synopsis acquires six new book readings or another set of mathematical results.

The distinction between an intellectual work and its exact supplied object matters to the argument:

| Supplied input | Object and attribution boundary retained by the audit |
|---|---|
| *Reason, Revelation and the Reconstitution of Rationality* | El-Tobgui's 353-page 2013 thesis, not the later book; Wisnovsky and Hallaq are not coauthors. |
| *A Commentary on the Creed of Asfahani v2.3* | The 248-page Wordsmiths translation selectively harmonises Arabic editions; the base creed, commentary and editorial apparatus have separate voices. |
| *A Collection on the Epistemology of Ibn Taymiyyah* | A 117-page modern anthology with repagination and abridgement; complete supplied-object coverage does not restore omitted material. |
| *Ibn Taymiyyah on the Oneness of Allah* | A 55-page English excerpt whose translator, publisher, date and exact Arabic exemplar remain unidentified; its extended unity span is absent from the v2.3 translation. |
| *Ibn Taymiyya and the Attributes of God* | Suleiman's 403-page study in El-Tobgui's English translation; reconstruction, primary quotation and reported opposing positions remain distinct. |
| *The Incoherence of Atheism* | Al-ʿUjayri's 217-page supplied text includes al-Sakran's closing appendix; cited scientific claims retain their mediated evidential status. |

The [coverage receipt](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/4833ff7cf28db11f9f39972feaf46961fb67d8f7b6e43561f24e68288f932e7b/T17_SIX_SOURCE_COVERAGE.json) and [verification guide](VERIFICATION-GUIDE.md#checking-the-six-input-drive-corpus-audit) distinguish inherited reading, later returns and selected visual checks. Their overlapping counts supply no additional independent witness or new primary-reading credit.

The [nine source-use areas](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/866e0d85f3524013ce7e5e6df1907fe184139259028d6e9b4ded91379893f0fa/DRIVE_CORPUS.md#per-result-source-links-and-use-limits) show how those inputs bear on later questions while preserving each inference's additional premises. The distinction between received existence, concrete sufficiency and necessity informs the [original-resource to same-bearer bridge](#from-an-original-resource-to-the-same-necessary-bearer); constitutive transport and representation assumptions still need their own account. Similarly, the [causal nonduplication audit](#causal-nonduplication-and-retained-productive-originality) requires original-role retention and the receipt-to-same-contribution overlap to reach its essentiality conclusion. The presence of relevant source language does not establish every modern formal premise.

For [connected creation](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/866e0d85f3524013ce7e5e6df1907fe184139259028d6e9b4ded91379893f0fa/DRIVE_CORPUS.md#connected-creation-and-the-actual-common-source-appraisal), *Oneness* PDF 37–40 already discusses connected creation and original field provision; fuller cooperation replies also appear in separately attributed notes at PDF 33 and 47. T20's reread therefore helps recover the source's positive common-source argument and its treatment of compatible cooperation. It does not discover that argument anew or reduce it to possible disagreement. The [anchored-field theorem](#a-fixed-source-across-a-connected-productive-field) retains its conditional proof and application premises, and the [later cooperation reassessment](#cooperation-complete-efficacy-and-the-later-common-source-reassessment) retains its own dated analysis. The diagram defect in Figure 3a was identified in T17 and confirmed in T20; this does not authenticate the translation's unknown ancestry.

The [veracity use](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/866e0d85f3524013ce7e5e6df1907fe184139259028d6e9b4ded91379893f0fa/DRIVE_CORPUS.md#source-owned-veracity-and-the-ownership-boundary) preserves the [separate premises for knowledge, control, fittingness and actual conformance](#owned-token-truth-and-the-separate-veracity-premises). A productive source is not automatically the speaker of a creature's assertion, and veracity alone does not authenticate a revelation. In the anthology, Turner's proper-function construction and its conditional Islamic extension likewise remain distinct from historical claims of actual Creator-recognition. Repeated MF 6:73 references and adapted articles do not become independent witnesses. The [testimony/copying connection](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/866e0d85f3524013ce7e5e6df1907fe184139259028d6e9b4ded91379893f0fa/DRIVE_CORPUS.md#cumulative-testimony-and-shared-source-copying) motivates dependence-sensitive assessment; the [copied-observation theorem](#copied-deletion-traces-and-what-fresh-observations-add) keeps its own mathematical source and proof. These books do not own HasE, scalar-fusion, normalisation, controller-transfer or modal-crossing theorems through conceptual relevance.

The map includes questions, inherited corrections and appraisal labels as well as registered results. In particular, `T20-GLOBAL-PLENITUDE-APPRAISAL` and `CURRENT-1434-RECOGNITION` do not introduce native result identifiers: the [existing foundational synthesis](#the-dated-foundational-audit-and-its-conditional-achievements), **T20-FOUNDATIONAL-PREMISE-SYNTHESIS**, remains the named synthesis owner. The audit preserves the **7 October 2026, 14:34 UTC** appraisal—provisional Necessary Being and actual field-bounded common-source acceptance, with universal SourceMode withheld. Its later audit timestamp does not create another philosophical verdict or supersede the separately routed cooperation reassessment. Numerical corrections and translation problems retain their original discovery dates and limited implications.

**Inspect the audit support:** [Six-object histories, overlaps and result/question map](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/6d020b39ac31311ec388678c79dd342ea6b5ee6144e1565e37b16a8d66d11761/DRIVE_CORPUS.json) · [Original evidence and object bindings](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/4e41aaebbcd30e448cad2b3ac006511c219386e3a0b93d92c416f2ffef04c382/SOURCE_BINDINGS.json) · [Original verification receipt](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/3cfc7b197a1c39b4f2c0b67ac3922e8875027b930a59c443fac22a9987e6a1b4/VERIFICATION.json) · [Finite context/source lookup](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/CONTEXT_SOURCE_INDEX.json). ### The original fifteen-result evidence and method handoff

The [original method handoff](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/d2bf1a9cbfb1e78b79e4ef630fb9c44ba6fb36067557658a6a432e63245fb7c2/METHOD_INTEGRATION_HANDOFF.md#how-to-use-the-evidence), prepared **7 October 2026, 16:07 UTC**, reconciles fifteen existing T20 result owners. Its narrative and three JSON companions form one supporting reference. For each result, the account connects an exact scoped achievement to inherited mechanisms, original checks and reviews, source-reading relationships, rejected transfers and obligations for a stronger claim. It creates no result owner, fresh proof, experiment or primary reading.

The central distinction is between **original research credit and receiving execution**. The original component proofs, finite controls, written analyses and separate AI reviews remain affirmative evidence at their recorded scopes. The [receiving boundary](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/d2bf1a9cbfb1e78b79e4ef630fb9c44ba6fb36067557658a6a432e63245fb7c2/METHOD_INTEGRATION_HANDOFF.md#receiving-and-finalisation-boundary) records CANDIDATE, fresh_evidence NOT_RUN and zero receiving independent-evidence count. Those fields describe the integration stage; they neither erase the original research nor become a fresh replay through this handoff. The cited independent reviews are AI-conducted, with their own source receipts, rather than external human-specialist certification.

All fifteen IDs come from the [frozen claim map](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/CLAIM_MAP.json). Their existing work destinations are retained below; the two external-transfer assessments share one work. The [structured result ledger](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/87d09884accfd207240e10d520289cc99947c6a59d1b8ce3adb3b1a8fa880671/RESULT_EVIDENCE_LEDGER.json) supplies the complete signatures, assumptions, original receipt aliases and residual obligations.

For a worked use of the handoff, consider [grounded support and later root cuts](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/d2bf1a9cbfb1e78b79e4ef630fb9c44ba6fb36067557658a6a432e63245fb7c2/METHOD_INTEGRATION_HANDOFF.md#6-grounded-support-exact-retraction-and-later-source-root-cut-transport). Keeping every inclusion-minimal label cut allows later identification of a fixed origin map, followed by mapping and minimisation, to recover the minimal root cuts. Keeping only the cheapest earlier label cuts can discard a later optimum. The handoff records positive original theorem/review and finite-control evidence for that statement, while leaving general Python-to-Lean refinement, complexity, actual source identity and operative duty/jurisdiction outside its established scope. A reader can therefore distinguish a useful proved archive rule from the further evidence needed to use it in a real inquiry.

The [cross-stream fields](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/87d09884accfd207240e10d520289cc99947c6a59d1b8ce3adb3b1a8fa880671/RESULT_EVIDENCE_LEDGER.json) preserve the same discipline. The [Arabic methodology](#arabic-reading-methodology-and-source-formal-feedback) keeps actual first-pass/return receipts and retrospective alignments separate; later Safadiyya reading is not made the cause of the earlier ABC bridge. NOT_APPLICABLE records that no relevant proof-source relation is asserted. The [Drive account](#six-supplied-drive-inputs-inherited-reading-and-t20-use) supplies inherited source-use and appraisal context without a sixteenth owner. The math joins distinguish later applicability assessments from earlier result development and an independently developed local application from upstream theorem transfer. These joins do not close the separate math-methodology family or create new upstream reading/replay credit.

The handoff preserves a deliberate historical difference: fourteen canonical rows agreed with their frozen rows at preparation, while the foundational synthesis kept the earlier **08:40** withholding and the separately dated **14:34** provisional Necessary Being/common-source appraisal distinct. The latter withholds universal SourceMode and accepts basic Creator-recognition as epistemologically admissible. Its “current” label belongs to that dated record. The [present result owner](../provenance/v5-successors/RESULT_STATUS.json) and [later cooperation reassessment](#cooperation-complete-efficacy-and-the-later-common-source-reassessment) retain their own authority and chronology. This original handoff does not substitute for the later result-ledger-v2 family.

### Mathematical reading, local applications and transfer limits

**Question.** How can an external theorem become useful to an Orthemology problem? The [7 October mathematical utilisation account](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/fd16c7ddcbb724e2170b5682414f476cf6e71e7ffb82c49d2ddbcb52d4068cfd/MATH_UTILISATION.md#result-and-scope) follows each exact manuscript through its research question, recorded inspection, proposed service, semantic mapping and remaining obligations. Representation, available information, observation law, objective, quantifiers and resource accounting must agree before a source result becomes a target service. The account identifies one independently developed finite application, explains two bounded transfer rejections and preserves other conditional research leads. It is a retrospective methodology reference; the existing mathematical works retain their results.

The [component ledger](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/d0e73b80d86fd9367c434e7711cb1bb40d5e0f61821db03392b5cd2590b61ff5/MATH_UTILISATION.json) records 52 exact manuscripts across 18 priority families plus the separate local application. Its [inspection account](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/fd16c7ddcbb724e2170b5682414f476cf6e71e7ffb82c49d2ddbcb52d4068cfd/MATH_UTILISATION.md#coverage-and-inspection-levels) distinguishes seven complete supplied proof-body reads, two targeted body reads and 43 abstract-only priority manuscripts. The complete reads are the four 104 papers, the 122 decoder, 245 PTS and 373 Monge; the targeted reads are the 122 lower bounds and 235 certificate-search lemma. Full reading is recorded reading of supplied proof text, not independent certification. The eight reading/formal/transfer statuses are separate axes attached to exact components: neither a family's formal link nor another manuscript's reading status propagates across the family. The 90-family/190-abstract focus overlaps the recorded 372-family/722-abstract screening; it is not an additional population. Acquired PDFs in 140, 243 and 365 retain their recorded body-unread status.

**Worked application: copying an observation.** The [family 122 account](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/fd16c7ddcbb724e2170b5682414f476cf6e71e7ffb82c49d2ddbcb52d4068cfd/MATH_UTILISATION.md#family-122-separates-three-manuscripts-from-one-local-application) separates three upstream manuscripts from `MATH-122-APPLICATION`. The upstream question supplied a research lead; [H-ICS information/composition](#information-and-composition) and the Ninth's information limits already owned the general information principle. A separate length-two deletion model compares `00` and `01` at retention `1/4`. Their complete observation laws have total variation `1/4`; any positive number of copies of one realised trace preserves that distance and sharp optimal equal-prior bounded randomised success `5/8`. Zero data gives distance `0` and success `1/2`. Equal encoded words with different source labels have equal laws in the stipulated word-only channel. Even for known `00`, observing `0` leaves the two singleton-retention masks equally likely. This makes precise why repeated access can improve durability without supplying fresh information or identifying the historical source or occurrence. The [existing copied/fresh-observation work](#copied-deletion-traces-and-what-fresh-observations-add), `T20-TRACE-OBSERVATION-COPYING`, owns the result and its evidence.

### The Twentieth mathematical companion and its original result owners

**Question.** What do the Twentieth mathematical results establish, and how do their separate arguments fit together? Dot's [7 October mathematical companion](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/40e374a62246636ffdbd424f517094dbaa7a7d00cc177675bed19377598e44fd/MATHEMATICAL_COMPANION.md) provides a three-part explanation: [what the computer checked](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/40e374a62246636ffdbd424f517094dbaa7a7d00cc177675bed19377598e44fd/MATHEMATICAL_COMPANION.md#part-1-what-the-computer-checked), the [ten-question foundational synthesis and A-H architecture](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/40e374a62246636ffdbd424f517094dbaa7a7d00cc177675bed19377598e44fd/MATHEMATICAL_COMPANION.md#part-2-foundational-audit-synthesis), and [further mathematical results](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/40e374a62246636ffdbd424f517094dbaa7a7d00cc177675bed19377598e44fd/MATHEMATICAL_COMPANION.md#part-3-further-mathematical-results). It is a substantial supporting reference with its own explanatory authorship. The arguments, declarations, countermodels and scientific credit remain with the originating works below. Preparing the companion added no scientific replay; its separately dispatched AI reviews are not external human peer review.

**The foundational dependency chain.** The [typed original-resource bridge](#from-an-original-resource-to-the-same-necessary-bearer) preserves the target, resource and bearer while deriving actual originality, same-bearer necessity and essential nonreceipt under distinct premises. Its B/C steps reuse the earlier SourceIdentity theorems; the [prior formal ownership map](#prior-formal-ownership-before-the-new-bridges) also retains Sixth's all-ancestor theorem. The [anchored field bridge](#a-fixed-source-across-a-connected-productive-field) uses qualification, source-mode classification and actual conformance, faithful participation and a genuine connected anchor to obtain coverage. **CND supplies the additional uniqueness step; it is not needed for coverage.** Visibility extends represented-rival exclusion to actual contributors, while positive inventory prevents vacuous Complete-labelled uniqueness. The [original dependency map](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/2f112bb4d0af7919ff5acd9593247d1ee83a702779f4c0d609a4333eabc12d0e/SOURCE_AND_PREMISE_MAP.md#separated-mathematical-dependencies) and [formal core](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/2a3c7de1e7a9028345bae3d059d7cc6398f4067326e10db915d8d89b55fcb3d1/AnchoredSourceBridge.lean) expose which assumptions each conclusion uses. Admissibility and inventory certificates are supplied premises, not facts discovered by propagation.

The [veracity work](#owned-token-truth-and-the-separate-veracity-premises) distinguishes knowing counterfeit as a shortcoming from the additional premise excluding it from fitting exercise. Its local truth theorem needs exact owned-content awareness and deliberate presentation; universal veracity needs coverage of the occasion domain. Creating another speaker's token does not make that token the source's own assertion. The [literal-same-bearer joint model](#joining-the-three-interfaces-on-literally-the-same-bearer) gives simultaneous inhabitation of the selected interfaces, with an outside original still present. It supplies a finite compatibility result, not a general derivation of each later interface's premises from the earlier one. The [dated foundational synthesis](#the-dated-foundational-audit-and-its-conditional-achievements) explains these achievements and the open general refinement from incidence coverage to independently interpreted Sixth ancestry.

A failed attempt without that premise does not prove general noninhabitation. For a concrete explanation check, use the support family `{a1}`, `{a2,b}`, `{a3,b}` in the companion: identifying all three `ai` with one root makes the larger inclusion-minimal label cut yield the smaller root cut. The original [written reconstruction theorem](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/b7dd5174207871abbff81e4c499bf74eabc7305402c7d88971e8b12bf96b4037/EXACT_PROBLEM_AND_WRITTEN_PROOF.md#theorem-7-exact-reuse-after-the-actual-origin-map-is-learned) supplies the argument.

Those records remain distinguishable from the [later source-informed cooperation assessment](#cooperation-complete-efficacy-and-the-later-common-source-reassessment); neither mathematical consistency nor document QA decides their philosophical warrant. The report attributes the revised weighting to reasons about original provision, genuine one-bearer agency and explanatory economy; assessor agreement is not evidence for its conclusion. Comprehensive agency could conceal unresolved coordination or impose a greater explanatory cost. A sufficiently grounded conceptual objection could defeat the inference without a completed rival cosmology. This appraisal neither authenticates revelation nor decides all unrelated necessary reality, and it does not blanket-retract or freshly adjudicate every earlier attribute claim, including Seventeenth's essential-T0 assessment.

**Source reading supplies arguments and constraints.** The [Arabic study account](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c8611b26ae09858c157059c1ed83690a586c98da5ac5a6aae8412ede077661cd/REPORT.md#arabic-source-study-and-rereading), read with the [methodological source/formal map](#arabic-reading-methodology-and-source-formal-feedback), attributes completed selected received bodies to the original studies. It does not grant this refactor new whole-volume reading, scan collation, historical authentication or apparatus coverage. Nubuwwat's token example needs warranted custody and a certification purpose: theft, unauthorised seal use or a different purpose can defeat the inference, and certification that a messenger was sent does not certify everything the messenger says. Safadiyya distinguishes a necessary existent from the actual Maker, and producing a quality in another from possessing it intrinsically. Its stronger exclusion intent is preserved while its causal-role and unity bridges remain open to examination. A formal dependency order is not a universal psychological acquisition sequence.

The [twelve-avenue account](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c8611b26ae09858c157059c1ed83690a586c98da5ac5a6aae8412ede077661cd/REPORT.md#position-across-the-twelve-research-avenues) connects these fronts to objective norms, restoration, provenance, collective diagnosis, identity, semantic fidelity, type theory, measure/computability, occurrence-sensitive language and empirical qualification. **The model synopsis has a bounded mathematical achievement.** The original emergence work reports nine named author theorems, seven selected author axiom readbacks, two rejected overclaims and seven additional review probes. These counts describe different objects. The model uses indexed worlds and fresh act identities, with ground, power marker, modal relation, trajectory and ExternalReceipt extension supplied as inputs. An empty selected act-kind extension with possible later activity differs from the stronger all-emergent-empty example; a counterexample separates them. Capacity and genus are predicates, not additional necessary individuals. The model does not establish complete voluntary-agency semantics, actual-world uniqueness or metaphysical possibility. The [later divine-action source audits](#divine-action-source-audits-and-the-stronger-activity-premise) retain the further historical application question. In particular, successive individuals replacing one another does not establish simultaneous cessation of every emergent kind.

The late ontology activity has its own chronology: Safadiyya and Darʾ volume 8 returns, ten Asbahani images revisited and a bounded first reading from previously acquired Darʾ volume 10. Those acts are not backdated into the older reading index, the methodological audit's zero-credit ledger or the unchanged 14:34 appraisal. The report's later result-evidence-ledger reference likewise distinguishes the original **fifteen-owner retrospective baseline** from a separately proposed emergence-control owner at capture. Sixteen rows were not proof of sixteen already admitted native owners. The existing emergence work and its [late evidence handoff](#late-evidence-handoff-for-the-typed-emergence-control) retain original credit and later admission/currentness routes.

**Reading counts describe specific activities.** Eight completed study groups count study groups, not every individually read body or volume. The supplied-object record's 1,393 pages and 190 visual checks, Eighteenth's 188 distinct reread pages, and Twentieth's Oneness text return with thirteen selected images overlap; they are not additive independent witnesses. Alternate translations or editions do not multiply witness independence, and complete extracted-text reading is not complete visual decipherment of scans and apparatus. Mathematical screening covers 722 abstracts; 52 exact manuscripts were selected in eighteen priority families, with seven full proof-body readings and two targeted readings. The 713 uncredited manuscript bodies belong to the overall screened population, not to the 52 selected bodies, and are deliberately unperformed scope. Formal inspection, execution and a faithful target transfer remain separate. PTS normalization does not transfer across HasE's proof-changing conversion and erasure; an older lower-bound formalisation does not prove a newer decoder; Ninth and Sixteenth retain their original common-policy/controller credit.

### The Twentieth source guide and its expanded reading edition

The [original GUIDE.md](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/7b728e2675cfb232ef160c730babee3f13c844a9b3e09ceabb471d579c975bd4/GUIDE.md) and [expanded GUIDE_v2.md](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c966158d0c2f2e2dc8007c1de9abbce0587a04cfd918fe1c9284508dbeeff6ee/GUIDE_v2.md) are two reading editions of **one historical supporting reference**. The guide explains what the original studies read, which arguments and textual controls matter, where to return, and which inferential steps remain disputed. It gives those studies communicable structure without acquiring their primary-reading or theorem credit. The [original context index](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/groups/t20-successor/CONTEXT_SOURCE_INDEX.json) fixes each public source identity; the [reading index](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/95f8c77ed7ebd217a5108669ad911ee00a5e92d17c7aab641c8f1f10a7ddf998/READING_INDEX.json) supplies the historical witness/receipt and coordinate framework.

Direct comparison finds a replacement of original line 9 and a 16-line insertion at v2 lines 14–29. **The other 63,254 original bytes are identical**, including every work-specific account and the complete 7 October 2026, 14:34 UTC appraisal. The revised paragraph distinguishes continuous first reading, local returns while a wider traversal continues, reflection after the relevant selected body is understood, and later question-triggered returns. Retrospective synthesis adds no new primary reading; rereading one witness does not create an independent witness. No complete second traversal is claimed for the new ordered studies. The original guide remains the primary; the revision is not a second scientific owner.

**Eight completed study groups have eight specified boundaries.** The paired Darʾ and Minhaj selections each form one group. Completion refers to received selections and coordinate systems, rather than every historical composition, volume, scan or edition. The guide preserves wider historical, ethical and devotional arguments even when their immediate formal use is unclear.

| Study group | Reading achievement recorded by the guide | Qualification affecting its use |
|---|---|---|
| Darʾ 8–9 | Whole selected electronic bodies: volume 8 has 1,873 paragraphs; volume 9 has 1,873 distinct credited newline positions. | These are different units. Truncated attempts were repaired; full printed-apparatus reading and manuscript collation are not claimed. |
| Minhaj 2–3 | 437 and 424 stable local lines through 54 untruncated first-pass windows. | Website sections are not print volumes. Later analytical returns overlap the first coverage. |
| Received original Sawaʿiq | 4,413 electronic lines through 88 windows, explicitly distinguished from al-Mawsili's abridgement. | The received historical composition is incomplete. The 1442 and 1441 editions and auxiliary restorations remain distinct witnesses and evidence. |
| Shifa | Whole received labels 2–307 through 76 untruncated windows. | Matching print edition remains unverified; labels are electronic coordinates and two original display identifiers remain null. |
| Jamiʿ, sixth collection | Eighteen received treatises and contents traversed through electronic reading and scan continuation. | Accounting for 444 scan positions is not 444 authorial pages or visual inspections: 248 distinct positions were viewed. Manuscript plates are absent. |
| Miftah | Main received body 3–1603, 1,601 positions, plus 103 actual preliminary positions. | Counts include supplied apparatus. Only 53 of 1,998 PDF positions were visually inspected; the historical projected treatment of will remains incomplete. |
| Nubuwwat | 436 records through 51 complete windows. | Bounded print controls do not complete the 975-position edited body and apparatus; the alternative acquired PDF remains outside the selected reading scope. |
| Safadiyya | 1,947 received electronic records through 85 complete windows. | 150 received-body scan positions and 39 modern-text positions have separate scopes. Seven correction-catalogue positions are within the 39; the 94 entries do not constitute an exhaustive critical edition. |

**The positive arguments and their open bridges remain visible.** In [Shifa's cooperation passage](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c966158d0c2f2e2dc8007c1de9abbce0587a04cfd918fe1c9284508dbeeff6ee/GUIDE_v2.md#h02-ability-to-act-alone-and-actual-whole-production), agents can each have the capacity to carry alone while actually sharing the load. The rejected description is two actual independent whole productions of the same effect. Jamiʿ likewise grants standing solo ability and then argues that another's contribution inhibits complete sole exercise. The remaining question concerns the further ranking of every limited original role as defective; merely naming the capacity/action distinction does not answer the source's actual-efficacy argument.

The guide also recovers affirmative giver/perfection, wise-timing and genuine secondary-causation arguments. Completing a product does not automatically complete its maker; invoking universal original provision in a reply does not independently prove that premise. Necessary existence does not alone identify the Maker or supply the entire qualified living, knowing and willing profile. In [Nubuwwat's envoy example](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c966158d0c2f2e2dc8007c1de9abbce0587a04cfd918fe1c9284508dbeeff6ee/GUIDE_v2.md#nb03-private-tokens-mission-scope-and-strong-alternatives), token origin, deliberate sending, mission scope and the truth of later reported content are separate. Theft, unauthorised seal use and a different commission are considered alternatives with possible evidential force. Generic production or answered distress does not itself establish normative certification.

These readings supply grounds and constraints for the existing [cooperation/common-source assessment](#cooperation-complete-efficacy-and-the-later-common-source-reassessment), while the [final research report](#the-twentieth-final-report-and-its-dated-research-assessment) supplies the programme synthesis. The guide preserves the dated provisional acceptance of Necessary Being and, at lower confidence, one complete original source of the actual connected order. Universal SourceMode remains withheld; principled intrinsic harmony remains a serious objection. Earlier withholding appraisals are not rewritten as unanimous precursors. None of these assessments authenticates a particular revelation or a universally reliable empirical faculty.

**Textual controls can change an inference.** Sawaʿiq printed 420's `بلا تجدد سبب` differs in causal polarity from electronic `بأن تجدد سبب`. Its local contrast restored at 1037–1038 and substantial setup restored at 1111–1113 use an auxiliary abridgement in different ways; the entire former passage is not reconstructed. Such restoration is not independently surviving original wording or extra electronic first-pass credit. The [prepared specialist questions](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/5b6e38a335ae31e2fe3082a36313acec9f62a9ebf78c695b61b20cb291f547fa/SPECIALIST_REVIEW_QUESTIONS.md) connect these witness issues to product-to-maker dependence, strict supremacy, necessary agreement, nonduplication, fitting veracity and intentional certification. They are an agenda for inquiry, not evidence that external specialist review occurred.

The revision's methodological additions route to the already owned [Arabic reading/source-formal account](#arabic-reading-methodology-and-source-formal-feedback), [six-input Drive audit](#six-supplied-drive-inputs-inherited-reading-and-t20-use), [mathematical utilisation account](#mathematical-reading-local-applications-and-transfer-limits) and [original fifteen-result handoff](#the-original-fifteen-result-evidence-and-method-handoff). Drive coverage of 1,393 pages/190 visual checks, 188 distinct Eighteenth reread pages, and the Twentieth Oneness text/thirteen-image return overlaps. Five original PDF hashes remain inherited custody identities; only retained Oneness was freshly rehashed among the six. The Arabic Asbahani scan is a distinct edited object. Mathematical screening of 722 abstracts, 52 priority manuscripts in eighteen families, seven full proof-body reads and two targeted reads does not imply body reading of the remaining 713 screened items, upstream replay or a completed theorem transfer.

The [typed-emergence work](#typed-emergence-fresh-acts-created-effects-and-a-stipulated-activity-gap) and its [late evidence handoff](#late-evidence-handoff-for-the-typed-emergence-control) own the later formal/control contribution. Safadiyya/Darʾ8 returns, ten Asbahani image rereads and a bounded first reading of previously acquired Darʾ10 section 017 are a separately dated delta. Earlier acquisition of section 017 established a boundary, not that passage's reading credit. This delta is outside the sealed eight-study index and zero-credit retrospective audit; the [later divine-action source audits](#divine-action-source-audits-and-the-stronger-activity-premise) preserve the further application question. An empty selected act kind does not establish simultaneous absence of all emergents, metaphysical possibility or a historical total-cessation doctrine.

The [joint source-readback narrative](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/7b49b13c05f553ae1cd48ff630146a1d19281dd9099a7701547894b48a74020a/REPORT_GUIDE_V2_READBACK.md) and [derived receipt](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/f08aceaf869f487af3c8859cafc66c86b3653bf48454ca1fc0eda7a62e374350/REPORT_GUIDE_V2_READBACK.json) are the same shared support used by the report. They accept bounded source changes for document authoring and expressly disclaim reviewer proof replay. The [guide v2 QA](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/c86ead4315d1855a717552c0e65aae6c0ffcdccd5852c96cc6416eee2e384be1/GUIDE_v2_QA_MANIFEST.json) reports 255 authored records, eleven literal relative-reference occurrences and twenty author-inspected pages; its `scientific_replay` and `source_edited` flags are false. The [current-document map](../../experiments/orthemology-v5-successors/source-store/49e7980551767643cf3edf6748b3e9466bb5383465673a40967a60189973f6c6/CURRENT_DOCUMENTS_v2.json) selects report v2, guide v2 and the unchanged mathematical companion v2 for three document roles, retaining report/guide v1 as historical artifacts. These role selections do not establish scientific adoption. Word outputs are owner-only, and the public selection omits page images and build/finalisation bodies; this curation performs no rendering or additional visual inspection.
